#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Base16Palette {
    #[serde(default = "default_base00", alias = "base00")]
    pub base00: String,

pub fn hex_to_color(hex: &str) -> Color {
    let clean = hex.trim().trim_start_matches('#');
    if clean.len() == 6 {
        let r = u8::from_str_radix(&clean[0..2], 16).unwrap_or(0);
        let g = u8::from_str_radix(&clean[2..4], 16).unwrap_or(0);
        let b = u8::from_str_radix(&clean[4..6], 16).unwrap_or(0);
        Color::from_rgb8(r, g, b)
    } else if clean.len() == 8 {
        let r = u8::from_str_radix(&clean[0..2], 16).unwrap_or(0);
        let g = u8::from_str_radix(&clean[2..4], 16).unwrap_or(0);
        let b = u8::from_str_radix(&clean[4..6], 16).unwrap_or(0);
        let a = u8::from_str_radix(&clean[6..8], 16).unwrap_or(255);
        Color::from_rgba8(r, g, b, (a as f32) / 255.0)
    } else {
        Color::WHITE
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ShellTheme {
    pub palette: Base16Palette,
    pub background: Color,
    pub surface: Color,
    pub surface_alt: Color,
    pub border: Color,
    pub muted: Color,
    pub text: Color,
    pub text_strong: Color,
    pub danger: Color,
    pub warning: Color,
    pub caution: Color,
    pub success: Color,
    pub focus: Color,
    pub font_family: String,
    pub font_size_normal: u16,
    pub font_size_overlay: u16,
    pub border_width: f32,
    pub control_border_width: f32,
    pub slant_width: f32,
    pub gpu0_temp_warn: u32,
    pub gpu0_temp_danger: u32,
    pub gpu0_vram_warn: u32,
    pub gpu0_vram_danger: u32,
    pub overlay_cut: f32,
    pub overlay_angle: f32,
}

impl ShellTheme {
    pub fn from_settings(settings: &ShellSettings) -> Self {
        let palette = &settings.palette;
        let background = hex_to_color(&palette.base00);
        let surface = hex_to_color(&palette.base01);
        let surface_alt = hex_to_color(&palette.base02);
        let border = hex_to_color(&palette.base03);
        let muted = hex_to_color(&palette.base04);
        let text = hex_to_color(&palette.base05);
        let text_strong = hex_to_color(&palette.base06);
        let danger = hex_to_color(&palette.base08);
        let warning = hex_to_color(&palette.base09);
        let caution = hex_to_color(&palette.base0a);
        let success = hex_to_color(&palette.base0c);
        let focus = hex_to_color(&palette.base0d);

        Self {
            palette: settings.palette.clone(),
            background,
            surface,
            surface_alt,
            border,
            muted,
            text,
            text_strong,
            danger,
            warning,
            caution,
            success,
            focus,
            font_family: "Fira Sans Condensed".to_string(),
            font_size_normal: settings.global_font_size,
            font_size_overlay: settings.overlay_font_size,
            border_width: settings.global_border_width,
            control_border_width: settings.control_border_width,
            slant_width: settings.slant_width,
            gpu0_temp_warn: settings.gpu0.temp_warn,
            gpu0_temp_danger: settings.gpu0.temp_danger,
            gpu0_vram_warn: settings.gpu0.vram_warn,
            gpu0_vram_danger: settings.gpu0.vram_danger,
            overlay_cut: settings.overlay_cut as f32,
            overlay_angle: settings.overlay_angle as f32,
        }
    }
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::fs;
use std::path::PathBuf;

use super::theme::Base16Palette;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, Default)]
pub struct ContentRule {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub pattern: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub readout: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub sound_path: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct FriendProfile {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub name: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub summary: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_profile_color")]
    pub color: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub sound: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub sound_path: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub tts: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub default_readout: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub content_rules: Vec<ContentRule>,
}

fn default_profile_color() -> String { "#f7f700".to_string() }

pub fn find_friend_profiles_path() -> PathBuf {
    let home = dirs::home_dir().unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/tmp"));
    let candidates = [
        PathBuf::from("resources/friend_profiles.json"),
        home.join("projects/Moon-Beauty-Shell/resources/friend_profiles.json"),
        dirs::config_dir().unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/tmp")).join("moon-beauty-shell/resources/friend_profiles.json"),
    ];

    for path in &candidates {
        if path.exists() {
            return path.clone();
        }
    }
    PathBuf::from("resources/friend_profiles.json")
}

pub fn load_friend_profiles() -> Vec<FriendProfile> {
    let path = find_friend_profiles_path();
    if path.exists() {
        if let Ok(content) = fs::read_to_string(&path) {
            if let Ok(list) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<FriendProfile>>(&content) {
                return list;
            }
        }
    }
    Vec::new()
}

pub fn save_friend_profiles(profiles: &[FriendProfile]) -> Result<(), std::io::Error> {
    let path = find_friend_profiles_path();
    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
        fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
    }
    let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(profiles)?;
    fs::write(path, json)?;
    Ok(())
}

impl Default for ShellSettings {
    fn default() -> Self {
        let mut visible = HashMap::new();
        for key in &[
            "calendar", "music", "alarm", "weather", "unified", "notify", "clock",
            "audio", "mic", "net", "cpu", "gpu", "ram", "tray", "battery", "custom",
        ] {
            visible.insert(key.to_string(), true);
        }

        Self {
            bar_height: default_bar_height(),
            global_font_size: default_font_size(),
            overlay_font_size: default_overlay_font_size(),
            tooltip_font_size: default_tooltip_font_size(),
            slant_width: default_slant_width(),
            slant_mode: default_slant_mode(),
            bar_shape: default_bar_shape(),
            module_shape: "slant".into(),
            global_border_width: default_global_border_width(),
            control_border_width: default_control_border_width(),
            global_padding: default_global_padding(),
            capsule_spacing: default_capsule_spacing(),
            hardware_poll_interval_ms: default_poll_interval(),
            notifications_enabled: true,
            notif_hold_duration_sec: default_notif_hold(),
            notif_volume: default_notif_volume(),
            notif_corner: default_notif_corner(),
            notif_shape: default_notif_shape(),
            notif_screen_name: default_notif_screen(),
            notif_baseline_y: default_notif_baseline(),
            notif_margin_x: default_notif_margin_x(),
            notif_stack_overlap: default_notif_overlap(),
            notif_card_width: default_notif_card_width(),
            notif_card_height: default_notif_card_height(),
            notif_slide_in_ms: default_slide_in(),
            notif_drop_pause_ms: default_drop_pause(),
            notif_drop_distance_px: default_drop_distance(),
            notif_summary_size: default_summary_size(),
            notif_body_size: default_body_size(),
            notif_excluded: default_excluded(),
            friend_profiles: load_friend_profiles(),
            bar_left: default_bar_left(),
            bar_center: default_bar_center(),
            bar_right: default_bar_right(),
            visible_capsules: visible,
            capsule_slants: HashMap::new(),
            capsule_shapes: HashMap::new(),
            capsule_bar_widths: HashMap::new(),
            standalone_windows: HashMap::new(),
            gpu0: GpuThresholds::default(),
            gpu1: GpuThresholds {
                temp_warn: 65,
                temp_danger: 75,
                vram_warn: 1,
                vram_danger: 0,
            },
            palette: Base16Palette::default(),
            tts_command: None,
            overlay_cut: default_overlay_cut(),
            overlay_angle: default_overlay_angle(),
        }
    }
}

impl ShellSettings {
    pub fn config_path() -> PathBuf {
        let config_dir = dirs::config_dir().unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/tmp"));
        config_dir.join("moon-beauty-shell").join("settings.json")
    }

    pub fn get_window_size(&self, id: &str, def_w: u32, def_h: u32) -> (f32, f32) {
        if let Some(win) = self.standalone_windows.get(id) {
            (win.width as f32, win.height as f32)
        } else {
            (def_w as f32, def_h as f32)
        }
    }

    pub fn load() -> Self {
        let path = Self::config_path();
        if path.exists() {
            if let Ok(content) = fs::read_to_string(&path) {
                if let Ok(mut settings) = serde_json::from_str::<ShellSettings>(&content) {
                    let disk_profiles = load_friend_profiles();
                    if !disk_profiles.is_empty() {
                        settings.friend_profiles = disk_profiles;
                    }
                    return settings;
                }
            }
        }
        let mut def = Self::default();
        def.friend_profiles = load_friend_profiles();
        def
    }

    pub fn save(&self) -> Result<(), std::io::Error> {
        let path = Self::config_path();
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
            fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
        }
        let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(self)?;
        let tmp_path = path.with_extension("tmp");
        fs::write(&tmp_path, &json)?;
        fs::rename(tmp_path, &path)?;

        let _ = save_friend_profiles(&self.friend_profiles);
        Ok(())
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/style/shape_box.rs ===
use iced::mouse::Cursor;
use iced::widget::canvas::{self, Canvas, Frame, Geometry, Path, Stroke};
use iced::{Color, Element, Length, Point, Rectangle, Size, Theme};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub enum ShapeType {
    Rounded(u16),
    Flat,
    Slant { left: SlantSide, right: SlantSide },
    Hexagon { cut: u16 },
    AngledHexagon { cut: f32, angle_deg: f32 },
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum SlantSide {
    Left,
    Right,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ShapeBox {
    pub shape: ShapeType,
    pub background: Color,
    pub border_color: Color,
    pub border_width: f32,
    pub slant_width: f32,
    pub width: Length,
    pub height: Length,
}

impl Default for ShapeBox {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            shape: ShapeType::Slant {
                left: SlantSide::Left,
                right: SlantSide::Right,
            },
            background: Color::from_rgb8(15, 15, 15),
            border_color: Color::from_rgb8(0, 51, 153),
            border_width: 2.0,
            slant_width: 12.0,
            width: Length::Fill,
            height: Length::Fill,
        }
    }
}

impl ShapeBox {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub fn shape(mut self, shape: ShapeType) -> Self {
        self.shape = shape;
        self
    }

    pub fn background(mut self, color: Color) -> Self {
        self.background = color;
        self
    }

    pub fn border(mut self, color: Color, width: f32) -> Self {
        self.border_color = color;
        self.border_width = width;
        self
    }

    pub fn slant_width(mut self, width: f32) -> Self {
        self.slant_width = width;
        self
    }

    pub fn size(mut self, width: Length, height: Length) -> Self {
        self.width = width;
        self.height = height;
        self
    }

    pub fn view<Message: 'static>(self) -> Element<'static, Message> {
        let w = self.width;
        let h = self.height;
        Canvas::new(self)
            .width(w)
            .height(h)
            .into()
    }
}

impl<Message> canvas::Program<Message> for ShapeBox {
    type State = ();

    fn draw(
        &self,
        _state: &Self::State,
        renderer: &iced::Renderer,
        _theme: &Theme,
        bounds: Rectangle,
        _cursor: Cursor,
    ) -> Vec<Geometry> {
        let mut frame = Frame::new(renderer, bounds.size());
        let w = bounds.width;
        let h = bounds.height;
        let half = self.border_width / 2.0;

        if w <= half * 2.0 || h <= half * 2.0 {
            return vec![frame.into_geometry()];
        }

        let path = match self.shape {
            ShapeType::Flat => Path::rectangle(
                Point::new(half, half),
                Size::new(w - self.border_width, h - self.border_width),
            ),
            ShapeType::Rounded(r) => {
                let radius = (r as f32).min(w / 2.0).min(h / 2.0);
                Path::rounded_rectangle(
                    Point::new(half, half),
                    Size::new(w - self.border_width, h - self.border_width),
                    radius.into(),
                )
            }
            ShapeType::Slant { left, right } => {
                let sw = self.slant_width.min(w / 2.0);
                let x1 = if left == SlantSide::Right { sw + half } else { half };
                let x2 = if left == SlantSide::Left { sw + half } else { half };
                let x3 = if right == SlantSide::Left { w - sw - half } else { w - half };
                let x4 = if right == SlantSide::Right { w - sw - half } else { w - half };

                Path::new(|builder| {
                    builder.move_to(Point::new(x1, half));
                    builder.line_to(Point::new(x3, half));
                    builder.line_to(Point::new(x4, h - half));
                    builder.line_to(Point::new(x2, h - half));
                    builder.close();
                })
            }
            ShapeType::Hexagon { cut } => {
                let c = (cut as f32).min(w * 0.35).min(h * 0.45);
                Path::new(|builder| {
                    builder.move_to(Point::new(c + half, half));
                    builder.line_to(Point::new(w - c - half, half));
                    builder.line_to(Point::new(w - half, c + half));
                    builder.line_to(Point::new(w - half, h - c - half));
                    builder.line_to(Point::new(w - c - half, h - half));
                    builder.line_to(Point::new(c + half, h - half));
                    builder.line_to(Point::new(half, h - c - half));
                    builder.line_to(Point::new(half, c + half));
                    builder.close();
                })
            }
            ShapeType::AngledHexagon { cut, angle_deg } => {
                let rad = angle_deg.to_radians();
                let sqrt2 = std::f32::consts::SQRT_2;
                let dx = (cut * rad.cos() * sqrt2).min(w * 0.45).max(0.0);
                let dy = (cut * rad.sin() * sqrt2).min(h * 0.45).max(0.0);

                Path::new(|builder| {
                    builder.move_to(Point::new(dx + half, half));
                    builder.line_to(Point::new(w - dx - half, half));
                    builder.line_to(Point::new(w - half, dy + half));
                    builder.line_to(Point::new(w - half, h - dy - half));
                    builder.line_to(Point::new(w - dx - half, h - half));
                    builder.line_to(Point::new(dx + half, h - half));
                    builder.line_to(Point::new(half, h - dy - half));
                    builder.line_to(Point::new(half, dy + half));
                    builder.close();
                })
            }
        };

        if self.background.a > 0.0 {
            frame.fill(&path, self.background);
        }

        if self.border_width > 0.0 && self.border_color.a > 0.0 {
            frame.stroke(
                &path,
                Stroke::default()
                    .with_color(self.border_color)
                    .with_width(self.border_width),
            );
        }

        vec![frame.into_geometry()]
    }
}
\n

pub mod shape_box;

pub use shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType, SlantSide};

/// Shell font family. Matches `fontFamily` in the QML build (Shapes.qml / Theme.qml).
pub const SHELL_FAMILY: &str = "Fira Sans Condensed";

/// Build a `Font` using the shell family at the given weight.
pub fn shell_font(weight: iced::font::Weight) -> iced::Font {
    iced::Font {
        family: iced::font::Family::Name(SHELL_FAMILY),
        weight,
        ..Default::default()
    }
}

pub trait TextExt {
    fn bold(self) -> Self;
    fn shell(self) -> Self;
}

impl<'a, Theme, Renderer> TextExt for iced::widget::Text<'a, Theme, Renderer>
where
    Theme: iced::widget::text::Catalog + 'a,
    Renderer: iced::advanced::text::Renderer,
    Renderer::Font: From<iced::Font>,
{
    fn bold(self) -> Self {
        self.font(shell_font(iced::font::Weight::Bold))
    }
    fn shell(self) -> Self {
        self.font(shell_font(iced::font::Weight::Normal))
    }
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::path::PathBuf;
use tokio::io::{AsyncReadExt, AsyncWriteExt};
use tokio::net::{UnixListener, UnixStream};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum OverlayTarget {
    Launcher,
    Calc,
    Clipboard,
    Dictionary,
    Unicode,
    Notes,
    Pass,
    Power,
    Todo,
    Settings,
    Web,
    Email,
    Reminders,
    Rng,
    Amogus,
    NotificationsHistory,
    Lockscreen,
    Annotator,
}

impl OverlayTarget {
    pub fn as_arg(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Launcher => "launcher",
            Self::Calc => "calc",
            Self::Clipboard => "clipboard",
            Self::Dictionary => "dict",
            Self::Unicode => "unicode",
            Self::Notes => "notes",
            Self::Pass => "pass",
            Self::Power => "power",
            Self::Todo => "todo",
            Self::Settings => "settings",
            Self::Web => "web",
            Self::Email => "email",
            Self::Reminders => "reminders",
            Self::Rng => "rng",
            Self::Amogus => "amogus",
            Self::NotificationsHistory => "history",
            Self::Lockscreen => "lockscreen",
            Self::Annotator => "annotator",
        }
    }

    pub fn from_str(s: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        match s.to_lowercase().trim() {
            "launcher" | "app" | "run" => Some(Self::Launcher),
            "calc" | "calculator" => Some(Self::Calc),
            "clipboard" | "clip" => Some(Self::Clipboard),
            "dictionary" | "dict" | "def" => Some(Self::Dictionary),
            "unicode" => Some(Self::Unicode),
            "notes" | "note" => Some(Self::Notes),
            "pass" | "password" => Some(Self::Pass),
            "power" | "pwr" => Some(Self::Power),
            "todo" | "td" => Some(Self::Todo),
            "settings" | "cfg" => Some(Self::Settings),
            "web" | "search" => Some(Self::Web),
            "email" | "mail" => Some(Self::Email),
            "reminders" | "remind" => Some(Self::Reminders),
            "rng" | "dice" | "roll" => Some(Self::Rng),
            "amogus" | "amongus" | "sus" => Some(Self::Amogus),
            "history" | "notif_history" => Some(Self::NotificationsHistory),
            "lockscreen" | "lock" => Some(Self::Lockscreen),
            "annotator" | "annotate" => Some(Self::Annotator),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum IpcCommand {
    Toggle(OverlayTarget),
    Open(OverlayTarget, Option<String>),
    Close(OverlayTarget),
    CloseAll,
    DismissLatestNotif,
    JumpToLatestNotif,
    ToggleHistory,
    ReloadSettings,
    TestNotification,
    IncomingNotif {
        app_name: String,
        summary: String,
        body: String,
        is_urgent: bool,
    },
    Ping,
}

pub fn get_socket_path() -> PathBuf {
    let runtime_dir = std::env::var("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR")
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| format!("/tmp/user-{}", std::process::id()));
    PathBuf::from(runtime_dir).join("moon-beauty-shell.sock")
}

pub async fn send_client_command(cmd: IpcCommand) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>> {
    let path = get_socket_path();
    let mut stream = UnixStream::connect(path).await?;
    let encoded = serde_json::to_vec(&cmd)?;
    stream.write_all(&encoded).await?;
    stream.shutdown().await?;

    let mut response = String::new();
    stream.read_to_string(&mut response).await?;
    Ok(response)
}

pub struct IpcServer {
    listener: UnixListener,
}

impl IpcServer {
    pub fn bind() -> Result<Self, std::io::Error> {
        let path = get_socket_path();
        if path.exists() {
            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        }
        let listener = UnixListener::bind(path)?;
        Ok(Self { listener })
    }

    pub async fn accept(&self) -> Result<(UnixStream, IpcCommand), Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>> {
        let (mut stream, _) = self.listener.accept().await?;
        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
        stream.read_to_end(&mut buffer).await?;
        let command: IpcCommand = serde_json::from_slice(&buffer)?;
        Ok((stream, command))
    }
}


#[derive(Debug, Clone, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]
pub enum AnnotatorCommand {
    Show {
        path: String,
        watermark_cli: Option<String>,
    },
    Close,
}

pub fn get_annotator_socket_path() -> std::path::PathBuf {
    let runtime_dir = std::env::var("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR")
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| format!("/tmp/user-{}", std::process::id()));
    std::path::PathBuf::from(runtime_dir).join("mbs-annotator-ctl.sock")
}

pub fn send_annotator_show(
    path: String,
    watermark_cli: Option<String>,
) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>> {
    use std::io::Write;
    use std::os::unix::net::UnixStream;
    let sock_path = get_annotator_socket_path();
    let mut stream = UnixStream::connect(sock_path)?;
    let cmd = AnnotatorCommand::Show { path, watermark_cli };
    let json = serde_json::to_vec(&cmd)?;
    stream.write_all(&json)?;
    stream.shutdown(std::net::Shutdown::Write)?;
    Ok(())
}

=== FILE: ./src/backend/system.rs ===
use std::fs;
use std::path::Path;
use tokio::process::Command;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct CpuMetrics {
    pub usage_pct: u8,
    pub temp_c: u32,
    pub top_procs: Vec<ProcessItem>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct GpuMetrics {
    pub name: String,
    pub usage_pct: u8,
    pub temp_c: u32,
    pub power_w: u32,
    pub vram_free_gib: u32,
    pub top_procs: Vec<ProcessItem>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct RamMetrics {
    pub total_gib: f32,
    pub available_gib: f32,
    pub effective_avail_gib: f32,
    pub zram_ratio: f32,
    pub zram_saved_gib: f32,
    pub top_procs: Vec<ProcessItem>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct NetMetrics {
    pub iface: String,
    pub hw_type: String,
    pub ip: String,
    pub down_str: String,
    pub up_str: String,
    pub ping_str: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct BatteryMetrics {
    pub has_battery: bool,
    pub percent: u8,
    pub status: String,
    pub power_w: f32,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ProcessItem {
    pub pid: u32,
    pub name: String,
    pub metric: String,
    pub is_highlight: bool,
}

pub struct SystemCollector {
    prev_rx_bytes: u64,
    prev_tx_bytes: u64,
    prev_iface: String,
    prev_net_time: std::time::Instant,
}

impl Default for SystemCollector {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl SystemCollector {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            prev_rx_bytes: 0,
            prev_tx_bytes: 0,
            prev_iface: String::new(),
            prev_net_time: std::time::Instant::now(),
        }
    }

    pub async fn collect_cpu() -> CpuMetrics {
        let mut temp_c = 0;
        // Try /sys/class/thermal first (may be empty on some setups).
        for i in 0..10 {
            let path = format!("/sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone{}/temp", i);
            if let Ok(raw) = fs::read_to_string(&path) {
                if let Ok(v) = raw.trim().parse::<u32>() {
                    temp_c = if v > 1000 { v / 1000 } else { v };
                    if temp_c > 0 { break; }
                }
            }
        }
        // Fallback: /sys/class/hwmon k10temp (AMD), coretemp (Intel),
        // zenpower (AMD Ryzen OC). These systems have no thermal_zone files.
        if temp_c == 0 {
            if let Ok(entries) = fs::read_dir("/sys/class/hwmon") {
                for entry in entries.flatten() {
                    let dir = entry.path();
                    let name = fs::read_to_string(dir.join("name"))
                        .map(|s| s.trim().to_string()).unwrap_or_default();
                    if name == "k10temp" || name == "coretemp" || name == "zenpower" {
                        if let Ok(raw) = fs::read_to_string(dir.join("temp1_input")) {
                            if let Ok(v) = raw.trim().parse::<u32>() {
                                temp_c = v / 1000;
                                if temp_c > 0 { break; }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        let read_stat = || {
            let stat = fs::read_to_string("/proc/stat").unwrap_or_default();
            let line = stat.lines().next().unwrap_or("");
            let parts: Vec<u64> = line.split_whitespace().skip(1).filter_map(|x| x.parse().ok()).collect();
            if parts.len() >= 4 {
                let idle = parts[3] + parts[4]; // idle + iowait
                let total: u64 = parts.iter().sum();
                (idle, total)
            } else { (0, 0) }
        };
        let (i1, t1) = read_stat();
        tokio::time::sleep(tokio::time::Duration::from_millis(100)).await;
        let (i2, t2) = read_stat();
        let usage_pct = if t2 > t1 {
            let dt = t2 - t1;
            let di = i2 - i1;
            (100.0 * (dt.saturating_sub(di)) as f64 / dt as f64).clamp(0.0, 100.0) as u8
        } else {
            0
        };

        let top_procs = Self::get_top_cpu_processes().await;

        CpuMetrics {
            usage_pct,
            temp_c,
            top_procs,
        }
    }

    pub async fn collect_gpu(card_id: &str, is_nvidia: bool, nv_index: u32) -> GpuMetrics {
        if is_nvidia {
            if let Ok(output) = Command::new("nvidia-smi")
                .args([
                    &format!("--id={}", nv_index),
                    "--query-gpu=utilization.gpu,temperature.gpu,power.draw,memory.free",
                    "--format=csv,noheader,nounits",
                ])
                .output()
                .await
            {
                let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
                let parts: Vec<&str> = text.split(',').map(|s| s.trim()).collect();
                if parts.len() >= 4 {
                    let u = parts[0].parse().unwrap_or(0);
                    let t = parts[1].parse().unwrap_or(0);
                    let p = parts[2].parse::<f32>().unwrap_or(0.0) as u32;
                    let v = parts[3].parse::<u32>().unwrap_or(0) / 1024;
                    return GpuMetrics {
                        name: format!("NVIDIA GPU {}", nv_index),
                        usage_pct: u,
                        temp_c: t,
                        power_w: p,
                        vram_free_gib: v,
                        top_procs: Vec::new(),
                    };
                }
            }
        }

        let base = format!("/sys/class/drm/{}/device", card_id);
        let usage_pct = fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/gpu_busy_percent", base))
            .ok()
            .and_then(|s| s.trim().parse::<u8>().ok())
            .unwrap_or(0);

        // Enumerate all hwmon entries under the card's device. The old code
        // hardcoded `hwmon0`, which on amdgpu/AMD setups does not exist;
        // amdgpu typically registers as hwmon2 or hwmon3.
        let mut temp_c: u32 = 0;
        let mut power_w: u32 = 0;
        if let Ok(entries) = fs::read_dir(format!("{}/hwmon", base)) {
            for entry in entries.flatten() {
                let hw = entry.path();
                if temp_c == 0 {
                    if let Ok(raw) = fs::read_to_string(hw.join("temp1_input")) {
                        if let Ok(v) = raw.trim().parse::<u32>() {
                            temp_c = v / 1000;
                        }
                    }
                }
                if power_w == 0 {
                    for fname in ["power1_average", "power1_input"] {
                        if let Ok(raw) = fs::read_to_string(hw.join(fname)) {
                            if let Ok(v) = raw.trim().parse::<u32>() {
                                power_w = v / 1_000_000;
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                if temp_c > 0 && power_w > 0 { break; }
            }
        }

        let vram_free_gib = (|| {
            let total = fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/mem_info_vram_total", base))
                .ok()?
                .trim()
                .parse::<u64>()
                .ok()?;
            let used = fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/mem_info_vram_used", base))
                .ok()?
                .trim()
                .parse::<u64>()
                .ok()?;
            Some(((total.saturating_sub(used)) / (1024 * 1024 * 1024)) as u32)
        })()
        .unwrap_or(0);

        GpuMetrics {
            name: card_id.to_uppercase(),
            usage_pct,
            temp_c,
            power_w,
            vram_free_gib,
            top_procs: Vec::new(),
        }
    }

    pub async fn collect_ram() -> RamMetrics {
        let meminfo = fs::read_to_string("/proc/meminfo").unwrap_or_default();
        let mut total_kb = 0.0;
        let mut avail_kb = 0.0;

        for line in meminfo.lines() {
            if line.starts_with("MemTotal:") {
                total_kb = line.split_whitespace().nth(1).and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
            } else if line.starts_with("MemAvailable:") {
                avail_kb = line.split_whitespace().nth(1).and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
            }
        }

        let total_gib = total_kb / (1024.0 * 1024.0);
        let avail_gib = avail_kb / (1024.0 * 1024.0);

        let zram_orig = fs::read_to_string("/sys/block/zram0/orig_data_size")
            .ok()
            .and_then(|s| s.trim().parse::<f32>().ok())
            .unwrap_or(0.0);
        let zram_comp = fs::read_to_string("/sys/block/zram0/compr_data_size")
            .ok()
            .and_then(|s| s.trim().parse::<f32>().ok())
            .unwrap_or(0.0);

        let mut zram_ratio = 1.0;
        let mut zram_saved_gib = 0.0;
        if zram_comp > 0.0 && zram_orig > 0.0 {
            zram_ratio = zram_orig / zram_comp;
            zram_saved_gib = (zram_orig - zram_comp) / (1024.0 * 1024.0 * 1024.0);
        }

        let top_procs = Self::get_top_ram_processes().await;

        RamMetrics {
            total_gib,
            available_gib: avail_gib,
            effective_avail_gib: avail_gib + zram_saved_gib,
            zram_ratio,
            zram_saved_gib,
            top_procs,
        }
    }

    pub fn collect_battery() -> BatteryMetrics {
        let ps = Path::new("/sys/class/power_supply");
        if !ps.exists() {
            return BatteryMetrics::default();
        }

        let mut bat_dir = None;
        if let Ok(entries) = fs::read_dir(ps) {
            for entry in entries.flatten() {
                let name = entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().to_string();
                if name.starts_with("BAT") {
                    bat_dir = Some(entry.path());
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        let Some(b) = bat_dir else {
            return BatteryMetrics {
                has_battery: false,
                percent: 100,
                status: "Mains".into(),
                power_w: 0.0,
            };
        };

        let cap = fs::read_to_string(b.join("capacity"))
            .ok()
            .and_then(|s| s.trim().parse::<u8>().ok())
            .unwrap_or(0);

        let status = fs::read_to_string(b.join("status"))
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| "Unknown".into())
            .trim()
            .to_string();

        let power_now = fs::read_to_string(b.join("power_now"))
            .ok()
            .and_then(|s| s.trim().parse::<f32>().ok())
            .map(|p| p / 1_000_000.0)
            .unwrap_or(0.0);

        BatteryMetrics {
            has_battery: true,
            percent: cap,
            status,
            power_w: power_now,
        }
    }

    pub fn update_net(&mut self) -> NetMetrics {
        // Preferred interface: the one carrying the default route.
        let primary: Option<String> = (|| {
            let route = fs::read_to_string("/proc/net/route").ok()?;
            for line in route.lines().skip(1) {
                let p: Vec<&str> = line.split_whitespace().collect();
                if p.len() >= 2 && p[1] == "00000000" {
                    return Some(p[0].to_string());
                }
            }
            None
        })();

        let dev = fs::read_to_string("/proc/net/dev").unwrap_or_default();
        let mut chosen_iface = String::from("none");
        let mut chosen_rx: u64 = 0;
        let mut chosen_tx: u64 = 0;
        let mut fallback_rx: u64 = 0;
        let mut fallback_tx: u64 = 0;
        let mut fallback_iface = String::new();

        for line in dev.lines() {
            let parts: Vec<&str> = line.split_whitespace().collect();
            if parts.len() < 10 { continue; }
            let iface = parts[0].trim_end_matches(':');
            if iface == "lo" { continue; }
            let rx = parts[1].parse::<u64>().unwrap_or(0);
            let tx = parts[9].parse::<u64>().unwrap_or(0);

            if let Some(ref want) = primary {
                if iface == want {
                    chosen_iface = iface.to_string();
                    chosen_rx = rx;
                    chosen_tx = tx;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if rx > fallback_rx {
                fallback_rx = rx;
                fallback_tx = tx;
                fallback_iface = iface.to_string();
            }
        }
        if chosen_iface == "none" && !fallback_iface.is_empty() {
            chosen_iface = fallback_iface;
            chosen_rx = fallback_rx;
            chosen_tx = fallback_tx;
        }

        // If we're looking at a different interface than last time, seed the
        // deltas from this sample so the rate isn't computed against a
        // mismatched counter (which showed a huge spike on iface changes).
        if self.prev_iface != chosen_iface {
            self.prev_iface = chosen_iface.clone();
            self.prev_rx_bytes = chosen_rx;
            self.prev_tx_bytes = chosen_tx;
            self.prev_net_time = std::time::Instant::now();
            return NetMetrics {
                iface: chosen_iface,
                hw_type: "".into(),
                ip: "".into(),
                down_str: "0.0Kb".into(),
                up_str: "0.0Kb".into(),
                ping_str: "--".into(),
            };
        }

        let now = std::time::Instant::now();
        let dt = now.duration_since(self.prev_net_time).as_secs_f32().max(0.1);
        let rx_rate = (chosen_rx.saturating_sub(self.prev_rx_bytes) as f32 / dt) * 8.0;
        let tx_rate = (chosen_tx.saturating_sub(self.prev_tx_bytes) as f32 / dt) * 8.0;

        self.prev_rx_bytes = chosen_rx;
        self.prev_tx_bytes = chosen_tx;
        self.prev_net_time = now;

        let hw_type = if chosen_iface.starts_with("wl") {
            "WiFi"
        } else if chosen_iface.starts_with("ww") {
            "Cellular"
        } else if chosen_iface.starts_with("wg") {
            "WireGuard"
        } else {
            "Ethernet"
        };

        NetMetrics {
            iface: chosen_iface,
            hw_type: hw_type.to_string(),
            ip: "".into(),
            down_str: Self::format_bits(rx_rate),
            up_str: Self::format_bits(tx_rate),
            ping_str: "--".into(),
        }
    }

    fn format_bits(bps: f32) -> String {
        if bps >= 1_000_000_000.0 {
            format!("{:.1}Gb", bps / 1_000_000_000.0)
        } else if bps >= 1_000_000.0 {
            format!("{:.1}Mb", bps / 1_000_000.0)
        } else {
            format!("{:.1}Kb", (bps / 1_000.0).max(0.0))
        }
    }

    async fn get_top_cpu_processes() -> Vec<ProcessItem> {
        let Ok(out) = Command::new("ps")
            .args(["-eo", "pid,pcpu,comm", "--sort=-pcpu", "--no-headers"])
            .output()
            .await
        else {
            return Vec::new();
        };

        String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout)
            .lines()
            .take(8)
            .filter_map(|l| {
                let parts: Vec<&str> = l.split_whitespace().collect();
                if parts.len() >= 3 {
                    let pid = parts[0].parse().ok()?;
                    let cpu = parts[1];
                    let name = parts[2..].join(" ");
                    Some(ProcessItem {
                        pid,
                        name,
                        metric: format!("{}%", cpu),
                        is_highlight: false,
                    })
                } else {
                    None
                }
            })
            .collect()
    }

    async fn get_top_ram_processes() -> Vec<ProcessItem> {
        let Ok(out) = Command::new("ps")
            .args(["-eo", "pid,rss,comm", "--sort=-rss", "--no-headers"])
            .output()
            .await
        else {
            return Vec::new();
        };

        String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout)
            .lines()
            .take(8)
            .filter_map(|l| {
                let parts: Vec<&str> = l.split_whitespace().collect();
                if parts.len() >= 3 {
                    let pid = parts[0].parse().ok()?;
                    let rss_kb: u64 = parts[1].parse().ok()?;
                    let name = parts[2..].join(" ");
                    Some(ProcessItem {
                        pid,
                        name,
                        metric: format!("{} MiB", rss_kb / 1024),
                        is_highlight: false,
                    })
                } else {
                    None
                }
            })
            .collect()
    }

    pub async fn kill_process(pid: u32) -> Result<(), std::io::Error> {
        if pid <= 1 {
            return Err(std::io::Error::new(
                std::io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                "Refusing to terminate PID <= 1",
            ));
        }
        Command::new("kill").args(["-9", &pid.to_string()]).status().await?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

use tokio::process::Command;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct AudioStatus {
    pub volume_pct: u8,
    pub is_muted: bool,
}

pub struct AudioBackend;

impl AudioBackend {
    pub async fn get_sink() -> AudioStatus {
        if let Ok(out) = Command::new("wpctl")
            .args(["get-volume", "@DEFAULT_AUDIO_SINK@"])
            .output()
            .await
        {
            let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
            return Self::parse_wpctl(&text);
        }

        if let Ok(out) = Command::new("pactl")
            .args(["get-sink-volume", "@DEFAULT_SINK@"])
            .output()
            .await
        {
            let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
            let mute_out = Command::new("pactl")
                .args(["get-sink-mute", "@DEFAULT_SINK@"])
                .output()
                .await
                .map(|o| String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).contains("yes"))
                .unwrap_or(false);

            if let Some(pos) = text.find('%') {
                if let Some(start) = text[..pos].rfind(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_digit()) {
                    let vol = text[start + 1..pos].parse().unwrap_or(0);
                    return AudioStatus {
                        volume_pct: vol,
                        is_muted: mute_out,
                    };
                }
            }
        }

        AudioStatus::default()
    }

    pub async fn get_source() -> AudioStatus {
        if let Ok(out) = Command::new("wpctl")
            .args(["get-volume", "@DEFAULT_AUDIO_SOURCE@"])
            .output()
            .await
        {
            let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
            return Self::parse_wpctl(&text);
        }
        AudioStatus::default()
    }

    pub async fn adjust_sink_volume(step_pct: i8) {
        let sign = if step_pct >= 0 { "+" } else { "-" };
        let frac = format!("{:.2}{}", (step_pct.abs() as f32) / 100.0, sign);
        let _ = Command::new("wpctl")
            .args(["set-volume", "@DEFAULT_AUDIO_SINK@", &frac])
            .status()
            .await;
    }

    pub async fn toggle_sink_mute() {
        let _ = Command::new("wpctl")
            .args(["set-mute", "@DEFAULT_AUDIO_SINK@", "toggle"])
            .status()
            .await;
    }

    pub async fn toggle_source_mute() {
        let _ = Command::new("wpctl")
            .args(["set-mute", "@DEFAULT_AUDIO_SOURCE@", "toggle"])
            .status()
            .await;
    }

    pub async fn cycle_sinks() {
        if let Ok(out) = Command::new("wpctl").arg("status").output().await {
            let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
            let mut sinks = Vec::new();
            let mut in_sinks = false;
            let mut current_id = None;

            for line in text.lines() {
                if line.contains("Sinks:") {
                    in_sinks = true;
                } else if line.contains("Sources:") || line.contains("Filters:") {
                    in_sinks = false;
                } else if in_sinks {
                    if let Some(num_str) = line.split('.').next() {
                        let id_str = num_str.trim().trim_start_matches('*').trim();
                        if let Ok(id) = id_str.parse::<u32>() {
                            sinks.push(id);
                            if line.contains('*') {
                                current_id = Some(id);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            if sinks.len() > 1 {
                let next_id = if let Some(cur) = current_id {
                    let idx = sinks.iter().position(|&x| x == cur).unwrap_or(0);
                    sinks[(idx + 1) % sinks.len()]
                } else {
                    sinks[0]
                };

                let _ = Command::new("wpctl")
                    .args(["set-default", &next_id.to_string()])
                    .status()
                    .await;
            }
        }
    }

    fn parse_wpctl(text: &str) -> AudioStatus {
        let is_muted = text.contains("[MUTED]");
        let mut volume_pct = 0;
        if let Some(vol_str) = text.strip_prefix("Volume:") {
            let num_part = vol_str.split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or("0.0");
            if let Ok(f) = num_part.parse::<f32>() {
                volume_pct = (f * 100.0).round().clamp(0.0, 150.0) as u8;
            }
        }
        AudioStatus {
            volume_pct,
            is_muted,
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/backend/mpris.rs ===
use std::path::PathBuf;
use tokio::process::Command;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct MprisTrack {
    pub title: String,
    pub artist: String,
    pub album: String,
    pub is_playing: bool,
    pub position_sec: f64,
    pub length_sec: f64,
    pub volume_pct: u8,
    pub url: Option<String>,
}

pub struct MprisBackend;

impl MprisBackend {
    pub async fn poll() -> Option<MprisTrack> {
        let output = Command::new("playerctl")
            .args([
                "metadata",
                "--format",
                "{{title}}|{{artist}}|{{album}}|{{status}}|{{position}}|{{mpris:length}}|{{volume}}|{{xesam:url}}",
            ])
            .output()
            .await
            .ok()?;

        if !output.status.success() {
            return None;
        }

        let raw = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
        let line = raw.lines().next()?;
        let parts: Vec<&str> = line.split('|').collect();

        if parts.len() < 7 {
            return None;
        }

        let title = parts[0].trim().to_string();
        let artist = parts[1].trim().to_string();
        let album = parts[2].trim().to_string();
        let status = parts[3].trim();
        let pos_us = parts[4].trim().parse::<f64>().unwrap_or(0.0);
        let len_us = parts[5].trim().parse::<f64>().unwrap_or(0.0);
        let vol = parts[6].trim().parse::<f64>().unwrap_or(1.0);
        let url = parts.get(7).map(|s| s.trim().to_string()).filter(|s| !s.is_empty());

        if title.is_empty() && artist.is_empty() {
            return None;
        }

        Some(MprisTrack {
            title,
            artist,
            album,
            is_playing: status.eq_ignore_ascii_case("Playing"),
            position_sec: pos_us / 1_000_000.0,
            length_sec: len_us / 1_000_000.0,
            volume_pct: (vol * 100.0).clamp(0.0, 100.0) as u8,
            url,
        })
    }

    pub async fn toggle_play_pause() {
        let _ = Command::new("playerctl").arg("play-pause").status().await;
    }

    pub async fn next() {
        let _ = Command::new("playerctl").arg("next").status().await;
    }

    pub async fn previous() {
        let _ = Command::new("playerctl").arg("previous").status().await;
    }

    pub async fn set_position(sec: f64) {
        let _ = Command::new("playerctl")
            .args(["position", &format!("{:.2}", sec)])
            .status()
            .await;
    }

    pub async fn set_volume(vol_pct: u8) {
        let frac = (vol_pct as f64) / 100.0;
        let _ = Command::new("playerctl")
            .args(["volume", &format!("{:.2}", frac)])
            .status()
            .await;
    }

    pub async fn open_track_folder(url_opt: Option<&str>) {
        if let Some(url) = url_opt {
            if let Some(clean_path) = url.strip_prefix("file://") {
                let path = PathBuf::from(clean_path);
                if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
                    let _ = Command::new("xdg-open").arg(parent).spawn();
                }
            }
        }
    }

    pub async fn trash_current_track(url_opt: Option<&str>) -> bool {
        if let Some(url) = url_opt {
            if let Some(clean_path) = url.strip_prefix("file://") {
                let status = Command::new("gio")
                    .args(["trash", clean_path])
                    .status()
                    .await;
                if status.map(|s| s.success()).unwrap_or(false) {
                    Self::next().await;
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }
        false
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/backend/weather.rs ===
use serde::Deserialize;
use tokio::process::Command;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum WeatherAlert {
    None,
    Upcoming(String),
    Danger(String),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct HourlyForecast {
    pub time_label: String,
    pub icon: &'static str,
    pub temp_f: i32,
    pub temp_c: i32,
    pub desc: &'static str,
    pub precip_prob: u8,
    pub is_danger: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct WeatherData {
    pub temp_f: i32,
    pub temp_c: i32,
    pub icon: &'static str,
    pub desc: &'static str,
    pub alert: WeatherAlert,
    pub hourly: Vec<HourlyForecast>,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct OpenMeteoResponse {
    current: Option<CurrentMeteo>,
    hourly: Option<HourlyMeteo>,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
struct CurrentMeteo {
    temperature_2m: Option<f32>,
    weather_code: Option<u16>,
    wind_speed_10m: Option<f32>,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct HourlyMeteo {
    time: Vec<String>,
    temperature_2m: Vec<f32>,
    weather_code: Vec<u16>,
    precipitation_probability: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    wind_speed_10m: Option<Vec<f32>>,
}

pub struct WeatherBackend;

impl WeatherBackend {
    pub async fn fetch() -> Result<WeatherData, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
        let (lat, lon) = Self::detect_coords().await;
        let url = format!(
            "https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast?latitude={}&longitude={}&current=temperature_2m,weather_code,wind_speed_10m&hourly=temperature_2m,precipitation_probability,weather_code,wind_speed_10m&forecast_days=2&timezone=auto",
            lat, lon
        );

        let output = Command::new("curl")
            .args(["-s", "--max-time", "6", &url])
            .output()
            .await?;

        let res: OpenMeteoResponse = serde_json::from_slice(&output.stdout)?;
        let cur = res.current.ok_or("No current weather data")?;
        let hourly = res.hourly.ok_or("No hourly data")?;

        let cur_c = cur.temperature_2m.unwrap_or(20.0).round() as i32;
        let cur_f = (cur_c as f32 * 9.0 / 5.0 + 32.0).round() as i32;
        let (cur_desc, cur_icon) = Self::wmo_info(cur.weather_code.unwrap_or(0));

        let mut alert = WeatherAlert::None;
        let mut timeline = Vec::new();
        let total = hourly.time.len().min(24);

        for i in 0..total {
            let tc = hourly.temperature_2m.get(i).copied().unwrap_or(20.0).round() as i32;
            let tf = (tc as f32 * 9.0 / 5.0 + 32.0).round() as i32;
            let code = hourly.weather_code.get(i).copied().unwrap_or(0);
            let (desc, icon) = Self::wmo_info(code);
            let prob = hourly.precipitation_probability.as_ref().and_then(|p| p.get(i).copied()).unwrap_or(0);
            let wind = hourly.wind_speed_10m.as_ref().and_then(|w| w.get(i).copied()).unwrap_or(0.0);

            let is_danger = tf >= 95 || tf <= 30 || code >= 95 || wind >= 60.0;
            if is_danger && i <= 3 && alert == WeatherAlert::None {
                alert = WeatherAlert::Danger(format!("Severe Weather ({}h)", i));
            } else if prob >= 60 && i <= 3 && alert == WeatherAlert::None {
                alert = WeatherAlert::Upcoming(format!("Rain in {}h", i));
            }

            let hour_raw = hourly.time[i].split('T').nth(1).unwrap_or("12:00");
            timeline.push(HourlyForecast {
                time_label: hour_raw.to_string(),
                icon,
                temp_f: tf,
                temp_c: tc,
                desc,
                precip_prob: prob,
                is_danger,
            });
        }

        Ok(WeatherData {
            temp_f: cur_f,
            temp_c: cur_c,
            icon: cur_icon,
            desc: cur_desc,
            alert,
            hourly: timeline,
        })
    }

    async fn detect_coords() -> (String, String) {
        if let Ok(out) = Command::new("curl")
            .args(["-s", "--max-time", "3", "http://ip-api.com/json"])
            .output()
            .await
        {
            if let Ok(val) = serde_json::from_slice::<serde_json::Value>(&out.stdout) {
                if let (Some(lat), Some(lon)) = (val["lat"].as_f64(), val["lon"].as_f64()) {
                    return (lat.to_string(), lon.to_string());
                }
            }
        }
        ("40.7128".into(), "-74.0060".into())
    }

    fn wmo_info(code: u16) -> (&'static str, &'static str) {
        match code {
            0 => ("Clear", "☀️"),
            1 => ("Mainly Clear", "🌤️"),
            2 => ("Partly Cloudy", "⛅"),
            3 => ("Overcast", "☁️"),
            45 | 48 => ("Fog", "🌫️"),
            51..=55 => ("Drizzle", "🌦️"),
            61..=65 => ("Rain", "🌧️"),
            71..=77 => ("Snow", "❄️"),
            80..=82 => ("Showers", "⛈️"),
            95..=99 => ("Thunderstorm", "⛈️"),
            _ => ("Fair", "🌤️"),
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/backend/clock.rs ===
use chrono::{Datelike, Local, NaiveDate, Utc};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct WorldZone {
    pub name: &'static str,
    pub code: &'static str,
    pub offset_hours: f32,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct WorldZoneColumn {
    pub title: &'static str,
    pub zones: Vec<(&'static str, &'static str, String, bool)>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct CalendarViewData {
    pub date_str: String,
    pub month_label: String,
    pub year: i32,
    pub month: u32,
    pub first_day_offset: usize,
    pub days_in_month: u32,
    pub today_day: u32,
}

pub struct ClockBackend;

impl ClockBackend {
    pub fn get_bar_time() -> String {
        Local::now().format("%I:%M %p").to_string()
    }

    pub fn get_world_matrix() -> Vec<WorldZoneColumn> {
        let now_utc = Utc::now();
        let local_code = Local::now().format("%Z").to_string();

        let americas = vec![
            ("Pacific Time", "US/PT", -7.0),
            ("Mountain Time", "US/MT", -6.0),
            ("Central Time", "US/CT", -5.0),
            ("Eastern Time", "US/ET", -4.0),
            ("São Paulo", "BRT", -3.0),
        ];

        let europe = vec![
            ("UTC / GMT", "UTC", 0.0),
            ("London", "BST", 1.0),
            ("Berlin / Paris", "CEST", 2.0),
            ("Cairo", "EEST", 3.0),
            ("Moscow", "MSK", 3.0),
        ];

        let asia = vec![
            ("Dubai", "GST", 4.0),
            ("India", "IST", 5.5),
            ("Singapore", "SGT", 8.0),
            ("Tokyo", "JST", 9.0),
            ("Sydney", "AEST", 10.0),
        ];

        let render_column = |title: &'static str, zones: Vec<(&'static str, &'static str, f32)>| {
            let rendered = zones
                .into_iter()
                .map(|(name, code, offset)| {
                    let zone_time = now_utc + chrono::Duration::minutes((offset * 60.0) as i64);
                    let formatted = zone_time.format("%I:%M %p").to_string();
                    let is_me = code == local_code;
                    (name, code, formatted, is_me)
                })
                .collect();

            WorldZoneColumn { title, zones: rendered }
        };

        vec![
            render_column("AMERICAS", americas),
            render_column("EUROPE & AFRICA", europe),
            render_column("ASIA & PACIFIC", asia),
        ]
    }

    pub fn compute_calendar(year: i32, month: u32) -> CalendarViewData {
        let now = Local::now();
        let y = if year > 0 { year } else { now.year() };
        let m = if (1..=12).contains(&month) { month } else { now.month() };

        let first_day = NaiveDate::from_ymd_opt(y, m, 1).unwrap_or_default();
        let first_day_offset = first_day.weekday().num_days_from_sunday() as usize;

        let days_in_month = if m == 12 {
            NaiveDate::from_ymd_opt(y + 1, 1, 1)
        } else {
            NaiveDate::from_ymd_opt(y, m + 1, 1)
        }
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .signed_duration_since(first_day)
        .num_days() as u32;

        let today_day = if y == now.year() && m == now.month() {
            now.day()
        } else {
            0
        };

        CalendarViewData {
            date_str: now.format("%a, %b %d").to_string(),
            month_label: first_day.format("%B %Y").to_string(),
            year: y,
            month: m,
            first_day_offset,
            days_in_month,
            today_day,
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/backend/podman_twitch.rs ===
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use tokio::process::Command;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, Default)]
pub struct WatcherDetails {
    pub name: String,
    pub is_running: bool,
    pub streamer: String,
    pub drops: String,
    pub game: String,
    pub is_limited: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ContainerInfo {
    pub name: String,
    pub status: String,
    pub is_running: bool,
    pub is_watcher: bool,
    pub purpose: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct PodmanStatus {
    pub is_ok: bool,
    pub is_rate_limited: bool,
    pub watchers: Vec<WatcherDetails>,
    pub containers: Vec<ContainerInfo>,
    pub summary_label: String,
}

pub struct PodmanBackend;

impl PodmanBackend {
    pub async fn poll() -> PodmanStatus {
        let engine = if Command::new("podman").arg("version").output().await.is_ok() {
            "podman"
        } else {
            "docker"
        };

        let Ok(output) = Command::new(engine)
            .args(["ps", "-a", "--format", "{{.Names}}\t{{.Status}}\t{{.Image}}\t{{.State}}"])
            .output()
            .await
        else {
            return PodmanStatus {
                summary_label: "Podman Offline".into(),
                ..Default::default()
            };
        };

        let raw = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
        let mut containers = Vec::new();
        let mut watchers = Vec::new();
        let mut any_limited = false;

        for line in raw.lines() {
            let parts: Vec<&str> = line.split('\t').collect();
            if parts.is_empty() || parts[0].trim().is_empty() {
                continue;
            }

            let name = parts[0].trim().to_string();
            let status_raw = parts.get(1).copied().unwrap_or("Unknown");
            let image = parts.get(2).copied().unwrap_or("");
            let state = parts.get(3).copied().unwrap_or("").to_lowercase();
            let is_running = state == "running" || status_raw.to_lowercase().starts_with("up");

            let is_watcher = name.contains("twitch") || name.contains("miner") || name.contains("berry");
            let mut watcher_details = WatcherDetails {
                name: name.clone(),
                is_running,
                ..Default::default()
            };

            if is_watcher && is_running {
                let (streamer, drops, game, limited) = Self::parse_logs(engine, &name).await;
                watcher_details.streamer = streamer;
                watcher_details.drops = drops;
                watcher_details.game = game;
                watcher_details.is_limited = limited;
                if limited {
                    any_limited = true;
                }
                watchers.push(watcher_details);
            }

            let purpose = Self::determine_purpose(&name, image);
            containers.push(ContainerInfo {
                name,
                status: status_raw.to_string(),
                is_running,
                is_watcher,
                purpose,
            });
        }

        let summary_label = if any_limited {
            "Miner: Limited!".to_string()
        } else if let Some(w) = watchers.iter().find(|w| w.is_running && !w.streamer.is_empty()) {
            format!("📺 {}", w.streamer)
        } else {
            let active_count = containers.iter().filter(|c| c.is_running).count();
            if active_count > 0 {
                format!("{} Active", active_count)
            } else {
                "Idle".into()
            }
        };

        PodmanStatus {
            is_ok: !containers.is_empty(),
            is_rate_limited: any_limited,
            watchers,
            containers,
            summary_label,
        }
    }

    async fn parse_logs(engine: &str, container_name: &str) -> (String, String, String, bool) {
        let Ok(out) = Command::new(engine)
            .args(["logs", "--tail", "60", container_name])
            .output()
            .await
        else {
            return (String::new(), String::new(), String::new(), false);
        };

        let logs = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
        let mut streamer = String::new();
        let mut drops = String::new();
        let mut game = String::new();
        let is_limited = logs.contains("429") || logs.contains("rate limit") || logs.contains("too many requests");

        for line in logs.lines().rev() {
            if streamer.is_empty() {
                if let Some(pos) = line.find("Watching:") {
                    streamer = line[pos + 9..].split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or("").to_string();
                } else if let Some(pos) = line.find("Channel:") {
                    streamer = line[pos + 8..].split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or("").to_string();
                }
            }

            if drops.is_empty() && line.contains("drop") {
                if let Some(pos) = line.find("Drop progress:") {
                    drops = line[pos..].trim().to_string();
                }
            }

            if game.is_empty() && line.contains("game:") {
                if let Some(start) = line.find("game:") {
                    let rest = &line[start + 5..];
                    if let Some(end) = rest.find(')') {
                        game = rest[..end].trim().to_string();
                    }
                }
            }

            if !streamer.is_empty() && !drops.is_empty() {
                break;
            }
        }

        (streamer, drops, game, is_limited)
    }

    fn determine_purpose(name: &str, img: &str) -> String {
        let low = format!("{} {}", name, img).to_lowercase();
        if low.contains("flaresolverr") {
            "Cloudflare Proxy (:8191)".into()
        } else if low.contains("twitch") || low.contains("miner") {
            "Twitch Drops Watcher".into()
        } else if low.contains("jellyfin") {
            "Media Server (:8096)".into()
        } else if low.contains("vaultwarden") {
            "Bitwarden Vault".into()
        } else {
            "Background Service".into()
        }
    }

    pub async fn restart(container: &str) {
        let _ = Command::new("podman")
            .args(["restart", container])
            .status()
            .await;
    }

    pub async fn restart_all() {
        let _ = Command::new("podman")
            .args(["restart", "twitch-miner", "twitchminer-berrydrop", "flaresolverr"])
            .status()
            .await;
    }

    pub async fn open_web_portal(port: u16) {
        let url = format!("http://localhost:{}", port);
        let _ = Command::new("xdg-open").arg(url).spawn();
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/backend/borg.rs ===
use serde::Deserialize;
use std::fs;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use tokio::process::Command;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct BorgStatus {
    pub state_label: String,
    pub is_active: bool,
    pub is_mounted: bool,
    pub percent: u8,
    pub speed: String,
    pub eta: String,
    pub details: Vec<String>,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
struct RawBorgPayload {
    status: Option<String>,
    percent: Option<u8>,
    archive_name: Option<String>,
    speed: Option<String>,
    eta: Option<String>,
    processed_files: Option<u64>,
    total_files: Option<u64>,
}

pub struct BorgBackend;

impl BorgBackend {
    pub async fn poll() -> BorgStatus {
        let mut payload_raw = None;
        let candidates = [
            PathBuf::from("/dev/shm/borg-offsite-status.json"),
            dirs::runtime_dir()
                .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/tmp"))
                .join("borg-offsite-status.json"),
        ];

        for path in &candidates {
            if let Ok(content) = fs::read_to_string(path) {
                if !content.trim().is_empty() {
                    payload_raw = Some(content);
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        let is_mounted = fs::read_to_string("/proc/mounts")
            .map(|s| s.contains("/tmp/borg-mount") || s.contains("/mnt/main_backup"))
            .unwrap_or(false);

        let Some(raw) = payload_raw else {
            return BorgStatus {
                state_label: if is_mounted { "Mounted".into() } else { "Idle".into() },
                is_active: false,
                is_mounted,
                percent: 0,
                speed: "".into(),
                eta: "".into(),
                details: vec![if is_mounted {
                    "Archive Mounted at /tmp/borg-mount".into()
                } else {
                    "Standing by (All repositories synced)".into()
                }],
            };
        };

        let parsed: RawBorgPayload = serde_json::from_str(&raw).unwrap_or(RawBorgPayload {
            status: Some("idle".into()),
            percent: None,
            archive_name: None,
            speed: None,
            eta: None,
            processed_files: None,
            total_files: None,
        });

        let st = parsed.status.unwrap_or_else(|| "idle".into());
        let pct = parsed.percent.unwrap_or(0);
        let speed = parsed.speed.unwrap_or_default();
        let eta = parsed.eta.unwrap_or_default();

        let mut details = Vec::new();
        let is_active = st == "running" || st == "syncing" || st == "indexing";

        match st.as_str() {
            "indexing" => {
                details.push("Indexing repository files...".into());
            }
            "running" => {
                details.push(format!("Active Backup Progress: {}%", pct));
                if let (Some(done), Some(tot)) = (parsed.processed_files, parsed.total_files) {
                    details.push(format!("Files: {} / {}", done, tot));
                }
            }
            "syncing" => {
                details.push(format!("Syncing to WebDAV: {}%", pct));
            }
            _ => {
                details.push("Repository Idle".into());
            }
        }

        if !speed.is_empty() {
            details.push(format!("Transfer Speed: {}", speed));
        }
        if !eta.is_empty() {
            details.push(format!("ETA: {}", eta));
        }

        let state_label = if is_active {
            if pct > 0 {
                format!("Borg: {}%", pct)
            } else {
                "Borg: Active".into()
            }
        } else if is_mounted {
            "Borg: Mounted".into()
        } else {
            "Borg: Idle".into()
        };

        BorgStatus {
            state_label,
            is_active,
            is_mounted,
            percent: pct,
            speed,
            eta,
            details,
        }
    }

    pub async fn toggle_backup() {
        let _ = Command::new("sudo")
            .arg("/run/current-system/sw/bin/borg-backup-start")
            .spawn();
    }

    pub async fn toggle_mount() {
        let is_mounted = fs::read_to_string("/proc/mounts")
            .map(|s| s.contains("/tmp/borg-mount"))
            .unwrap_or(false);

        let script = if is_mounted {
            "/run/current-system/sw/bin/borg-umount-browser"
        } else {
            "/run/current-system/sw/bin/borg-mount-browser"
        };

        let _ = Command::new("sudo").arg(script).spawn();
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/backend/notif_dbus.rs ===
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::mpsc::Sender;
use zbus::zvariant::OwnedValue;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct IncomingNotification {
    pub id: u32,
    pub app_name: String,
    pub summary: String,
    pub body: String,
    pub is_urgent: bool,
}

pub struct NotifServer {
    tx: Sender<IncomingNotification>,
}

fn next_id() -> u32 {
    use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU32, Ordering};
    static NEXT: AtomicU32 = AtomicU32::new(1);
    NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
}

#[zbus::interface(name = "org.freedesktop.Notifications")]
impl NotifServer {
    fn notify(
        &self,
        app_name: String,
        _replaces_id: u32,
        _app_icon: String,
        summary: String,
        body: String,
        _actions: Vec<String>,
        hints: HashMap<String, OwnedValue>,
        _expire_timeout: i32,
    ) -> u32 {
        let id = next_id();
        let is_urgent = hints
            .get("urgency")
            .and_then(|v| u8::try_from(v).ok())
            .map(|u| u == 2)
            .unwrap_or(false);
        let _ = self.tx.send(IncomingNotification {
            id, app_name, summary, body, is_urgent,
        });
        id
    }

    fn close_notification(&self, _id: u32) {}

    fn get_capabilities(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        vec!["body".into(), "actions".into()]
    }

    fn get_server_information(&self) -> (String, String, String, String) {
        (
            "MoonBeautyShell".into(),
            "MoonBeauty".into(),
            "1.0".into(),
            "1.2".into(),
        )
    }
}

pub fn spawn(tx: Sender<IncomingNotification>) {
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        let rt = match tokio::runtime::Builder::new_current_thread()
            .enable_all()
            .build()
        {
            Ok(r) => r,
            Err(e) => { eprintln!("[notif_dbus] rt build: {e}"); return; }
        };
        rt.block_on(async move {
            let server = NotifServer { tx };
            let conn = zbus::connection::Builder::session()
                .and_then(|b| b.name("org.freedesktop.Notifications"))
                .and_then(|b| b.serve_at("/org/freedesktop/Notifications", server))
                .expect("zbus builder")
                .build()
                .await;
            match conn {
                Ok(_c) => {
                    eprintln!("[notif_dbus] org.freedesktop.Notifications owned");
                    std::future::pending::<()>().await;
                    let _ = _c;
                }
                Err(e) => eprintln!("[notif_dbus] serve failed: {e}"),
            }
        });
    });
}

=== FILE: ./src/backend/annotator_ipc.rs ===
//! Persistent Unix-socket listener for `--annotator-surface`.
//! Pushes incoming AnnotatorCommand messages to an mpsc channel
//! consumed by the iced runtime via PollAnnotator subscription.

use std::sync::mpsc::Sender;

use crate::ipc::protocol::{get_annotator_socket_path, AnnotatorCommand};

pub fn spawn(tx: Sender<AnnotatorCommand>) {
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        use std::io::Read;
        use std::os::unix::net::UnixListener;

        let path = get_annotator_socket_path();
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        let listener = match UnixListener::bind(&path) {
            Ok(l) => {
                eprintln!("[annotator-ipc] listening on {:?}", path);
                l
            }
            Err(e) => {
                eprintln!("[annotator-ipc] bind failed: {e}");
                return;
            }
        };

        loop {
            match listener.accept() {
                Ok((mut stream, _)) => {
                    let _ = stream.set_read_timeout(Some(std::time::Duration::from_secs(3)));
                    let mut buf = Vec::new();
                    if stream.read_to_end(&mut buf).is_err() {
                        continue;
                    }
                    match serde_json::from_slice::<AnnotatorCommand>(&buf) {
                        Ok(cmd) => {
                            eprintln!("[annotator-ipc] received {:?}", cmd);
                            if tx.send(cmd).is_err() {
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                        Err(e) => eprintln!("[annotator-ipc] parse: {e}"),
                    }
                }
                Err(e) => eprintln!("[annotator-ipc] accept: {e}"),
            }
        }
    });
}
