=== FILE: ./src/backend/tray_watcher.rs ===
//! StatusNotifierWatcher host.
//!
//! Tray applications (Steam, CoreCtrl, music players, Discord, …) speak the
//! `org.kde.StatusNotifierItem` protocol. For their registration call to go
//! anywhere, something has to own `org.kde.StatusNotifierWatcher` on the
//! session bus. Waybar / Plasma do this; bare Sway+this-shell does not, which
//! is why SNI apps silently disappear without a host. This module is that host.
//!
//! It's a pure D-Bus background thread — no window, no layer surface. The
//! tray_popup overlay queries `RegisteredStatusNotifierItems` on startup.

use std::collections::HashSet;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct TrayWatcher {
    items: Arc<Mutex<HashSet<String>>>,
}

#[zbus::interface(name = "org.kde.StatusNotifierWatcher")]
impl TrayWatcher {
    fn register_status_notifier_item(
        &self,
        service: String,
        #[zbus(header)] hdr: zbus::message::Header<'_>,
    ) -> zbus::fdo::Result<()> {
        // Some clients (ayatana-style, e.g. Steam) send just the object path.
        // Fall back to the sender's unique bus name so the item is reachable.
        let entry = if service.starts_with('/') {
            let sender = hdr.sender().map(|s| s.as_str().to_string()).unwrap_or_default();
            format!("{}{}", sender, service)
        } else {
            normalize_service(&service)
        };
        let is_new = self.items.lock().unwrap().insert(entry.clone());
        if is_new {
            eprintln!("[tray-watcher] registered: {}", entry);
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    fn register_status_notifier_host(&self, service: String) -> zbus::fdo::Result<()> {
        eprintln!("[tray-watcher] host registered: {}", service);
        Ok(())
    }

    #[zbus(property)]
    fn registered_status_notifier_items(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        self.items.lock().unwrap().iter().cloned().collect()
    }

    #[zbus(property)]
    fn is_status_notifier_host_registered(&self) -> bool { true }

    #[zbus(property)]
    fn protocol_version(&self) -> i32 { 0 }
}

fn normalize_service(s: &str) -> String {
    // Registration strings are one of:
    //   ":1.42"                            (well-known bus name, default path)
    //   "org.kde.StatusNotifierItem-1234"  (well-known service name)
    //   ":1.42/org/ayatana/.../steam"      (explicit object path)
    if let Some(idx) = s.find('/') {
        format!("{}{}", &s[..idx], &s[idx..])
    } else {
        format!("{}/StatusNotifierItem", s)
    }
}

pub fn spawn() {
    std::thread::spawn(|| {
        let rt = match tokio::runtime::Builder::new_current_thread()
            .enable_all()
            .build()
        {
            Ok(r) => r,
            Err(e) => { eprintln!("[tray-watcher] rt build: {e}"); return; }
        };
        rt.block_on(async move {
            let watcher = TrayWatcher { items: Arc::new(Mutex::new(HashSet::new())) };
            let conn = zbus::connection::Builder::session()
                .and_then(|b| b.name("org.kde.StatusNotifierWatcher"))
                .and_then(|b| b.serve_at("/StatusNotifierWatcher", watcher))
                .expect("zbus builder")
                .build()
                .await;
            match conn {
                Ok(_c) => {
                    eprintln!("[tray-watcher] org.kde.StatusNotifierWatcher owned");
                    std::future::pending::<()>().await;
                    let _ = _c;
                }
                Err(e) => eprintln!("[tray-watcher] serve failed: {e}"),
            }
        });
    });
}

// ── SNI client side: enumeration, icons, activation ────────────────

use std::path::PathBuf;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct TrayItem {
    pub bus: String,
    pub path: String,
    pub id: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub icon_path: Option<String>,
    pub tooltip: String,
}

pub async fn fetch_tray_items() -> Vec<TrayItem> {
    let conn = match zbus::Connection::session().await {
        Ok(c) => c,
        Err(e) => { eprintln!("[tray-fetch] session bus: {e}"); return Vec::new(); }
    };
    let watcher = match zbus::Proxy::new(
        &conn,
        "org.kde.StatusNotifierWatcher",
        "/StatusNotifierWatcher",
        "org.kde.StatusNotifierWatcher",
    ).await {
        Ok(p) => p,
        Err(e) => { eprintln!("[tray-fetch] watcher proxy: {e}"); return Vec::new(); }
    };
    let raw_items: Vec<String> = match watcher.get_property("RegisteredStatusNotifierItems").await {
        Ok(v) => v,
        Err(e) => { eprintln!("[tray-fetch] RegisteredStatusNotifierItems: {e}"); return Vec::new(); }
    };

    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for raw in raw_items {
        let Some((bus, path)) = parse_registered(&raw) else { continue };
        let proxy = match zbus::Proxy::new(&conn, bus.as_str(), path.as_str(), "org.kde.StatusNotifierItem").await {
            Ok(p) => p,
            Err(e) => { eprintln!("[tray-fetch] {} proxy: {e}", raw); continue; }
        };
        let id: String = proxy.get_property("Id").await.unwrap_or_default();
        let title: String = proxy.get_property("Title").await.unwrap_or_default();
        let status: String = proxy.get_property("Status").await.unwrap_or_default();
        if status.eq_ignore_ascii_case("Passive") { continue; }

        let icon_name: String = proxy.get_property("IconName").await.unwrap_or_default();
        let mut icon_path = if !icon_name.is_empty() { resolve_icon(&icon_name) } else { None };
        if icon_path.is_none() {
            if let Ok(pixmaps) = proxy.get_property::<Vec<(i32, i32, Vec<u8>)>>("IconPixmap").await {
                if let Some((w, h, data)) = pixmaps.into_iter().max_by_key(|(w, h, _)| (*w, *h)) {
                    icon_path = save_pixmap(&id, w.max(0) as u32, h.max(0) as u32, &data);
                }
            }
        }
        let tooltip = match proxy
            .get_property::<(String, Vec<(i32, i32, Vec<u8>)>, String, String)>("ToolTip")
            .await
        {
            Ok((_icon, _pixmaps, ttl, sub)) => {
                let ttl = ttl.trim().to_string();
                let sub = sub.trim().to_string();
                match (ttl.is_empty(), sub.is_empty()) {
                    (true, true) => String::new(),
                    (false, true) => ttl,
                    (true, false) => sub,
                    (false, false) => format!("{} — {}", ttl, sub),
                }
            }
            Err(_) => String::new(),
        };
        out.push(TrayItem { bus, path, id, title, icon_path, tooltip });
    }
    out
}

pub async fn activate_tray_item(bus: &str, path: &str) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>> {
    let conn = zbus::Connection::session().await?;
    let proxy = zbus::Proxy::new(&conn, bus, path, "org.kde.StatusNotifierItem").await?;
    proxy.call_method("Activate", &(0i32, 0i32)).await?;
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn context_menu_tray_item(bus: &str, path: &str) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>> {
    let conn = zbus::Connection::session().await?;
    let proxy = zbus::Proxy::new(&conn, bus, path, "org.kde.StatusNotifierItem").await?;
    proxy.call_method("ContextMenu", &(0i32, 0i32)).await?;
    Ok(())
}

fn parse_registered(s: &str) -> Option<(String, String)> {
    if let Some(idx) = s.find('/') {
        let (bus, path) = (&s[..idx], &s[idx..]);
        if bus.is_empty() { None } else { Some((bus.to_string(), path.to_string())) }
    } else if !s.is_empty() {
        Some((s.to_string(), "/StatusNotifierItem".to_string()))
    } else {
        None
    }
}

fn resolve_icon(icon_name: &str) -> Option<String> {
    use std::path::Path;
    if icon_name.is_empty() { return None; }
    if icon_name.starts_with('/') {
        return if Path::new(icon_name).exists() { Some(icon_name.to_string()) } else { None };
    }
    let home = dirs::home_dir().unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/tmp"));
    let roots = [
        PathBuf::from("/run/current-system/sw/share/icons/hicolor"),
        PathBuf::from("/usr/share/icons/hicolor"),
        PathBuf::from("/usr/local/share/icons/hicolor"),
        home.join(".local/share/icons/hicolor"),
        home.join(".local/share/flatpak/exports/share/icons/hicolor"),
        home.join(".nix-profile/share/icons/hicolor"),
    ];
    let sizes = ["48x48", "64x64", "128x128", "256x256", "512x512", "scalable", "symbolic"];
    let exts = ["png", "svg", "xpm"];
    for root in &roots {
        for size in &sizes {
            for ext in &exts {
                let p = root.join(size).join("apps").join(format!("{}.{}", icon_name, ext));
                if p.exists() { return Some(p.to_string_lossy().into_owned()); }
            }
        }
    }
    for pix in ["/run/current-system/sw/share/pixmaps", "/usr/share/pixmaps"] {
        for ext in &exts {
            let p = PathBuf::from(pix).join(format!("{}.{}", icon_name, ext));
            if p.exists() { return Some(p.to_string_lossy().into_owned()); }
        }
    }
    None
}

fn save_pixmap(id: &str, w: u32, h: u32, data: &[u8]) -> Option<String> {
    if w == 0 || h == 0 { return None; }
    if data.len() != (w as usize * h as usize * 4) { return None; }
    let mut rgba = Vec::with_capacity(data.len());
    // SNI IconPixmap is ARGB32 network order: [A, R, G, B]
    for chunk in data.as_chunks::<4>().0 {
        rgba.push(chunk[1]);
        rgba.push(chunk[2]);
        rgba.push(chunk[3]);
        rgba.push(chunk[0]);
    }
    let img: image::RgbaImage = image::ImageBuffer::from_raw(w, h, rgba)?;
    let safe: String = id.chars().map(|c| if c.is_alphanumeric() { c } else { '_' }).collect();
    let icon_dir = std::env::var("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR")
        .map(std::path::PathBuf::from)
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| std::env::temp_dir())
        .join("mbs-tray");
    let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(&icon_dir);
    let path = icon_dir.join(format!("mbs-tray-{}.png", safe));
    img.save(&path).ok()?;
    Some(path.to_string_lossy().into_owned())
}
\n
=== FILE: ./src/bar/capsules.rs ===
use iced::widget::{button, container, text, tooltip, MouseArea, Row, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length};

use crate::backend::UnifiedTelemetry;
use crate::config::theme::{hex_to_color, ShellTheme};
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType, SlantSide};
use crate::style::shell_font;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum CapsuleAction {
    OpenOverlay(String),
    TogglePlayPause,
    NextTrack,
    PreviousTrack,
    CycleAudioSink,
    ToggleSinkMute,
    ToggleSourceMute,
    CycleGpu,
    ToggleNotificationDnd,
    ToggleCustomMode,
    ToggleTrayExpanded,
    TrayActivate(crate::backend::TrayItem),
    TrayContextMenu(crate::backend::TrayItem),
}

thread_local! {
    static LABEL_W: std::cell::Cell<f32> = const { std::cell::Cell::new(0.0) };
}

fn est_text_width(txt: &str, size: u16) -> f32 {
    let em = size as f32;
    txt.chars()
        .map(|c| if c.is_ascii() { em * 0.6 } else { em * 1.3 })
        .sum()
}

fn label<'a, Message: 'a>(
    segments: &[(String, Color)],
    size: u16,
) -> Element<'a, Message> {
    let mut r: Row<'a, Message, iced::Theme, iced::Renderer> = Row::new();
    r = r.spacing(0).align_y(Alignment::Center);
    let mut total_w = 0.0;
    for (txt, col) in segments {
        total_w += est_text_width(txt, size);
        if txt.is_empty() {
            continue;
        }
        r = r.push(
            text(txt.clone())
                .size(size)
                .wrapping(iced::widget::text::Wrapping::None)
                .font(shell_font(iced::font::Weight::Bold))
                .color(*col),
        );
    }
    LABEL_W.with(|w| w.set(total_w));
    r.into()
}

pub struct CapsuleRenderer;

impl CapsuleRenderer {
    pub fn render<'a, Message: 'static + Clone>(
        id: &str,
        telemetry: &'a UnifiedTelemetry,
        theme: &ShellTheme,
        slant_left: SlantSide,
        slant_right: SlantSide,
        module_shape: &str,
        region: &str,
        on_action: impl Fn(CapsuleAction) -> Message + 'static + Copy,
    ) -> Element<'a, Message> {
        LABEL_W.with(|w| w.set(0.0));
        let (content, action, border_color): (
            Element<'a, Message>,
            CapsuleAction,
            iced::Color,
        ) = match id {
            "calendar" => {
                let date_str = chrono::Local::now().format("%a, %b %d").to_string();
                let elem = label::<Message>(&[(date_str, theme.text)], theme.font_size_normal);
                (elem, CapsuleAction::OpenOverlay("calendar".into()), theme.border)
            }
            "music" => {
                let display = match &telemetry.mpris {
                    Some(m) if !m.title.is_empty() && !m.artist.is_empty() => {
                        format!("{} - {}", m.artist, m.title)
                    }
                    Some(m) if !m.title.is_empty() => m.title.clone(),
                    Some(m) if !m.artist.is_empty() => m.artist.clone(),
                    _ => "No Track".into(),
                };
                let truncated: String = if display.chars().count() > 20 {
                    let s: String = display.chars().take(20).collect();
                    format!("{}…", s.trim())
                } else {
                    display
                };
                let elem = label::<Message>(&[(truncated, theme.text)], theme.font_size_normal);
                (elem, CapsuleAction::TogglePlayPause, theme.border)
            }
            "alarm" => {
                let entries = crate::overlays::reminders::RemindersOverlay::load();
                let now = chrono::Local::now().timestamp();
                let active: Vec<_> = entries.iter().filter(|e| e.target_epoch > now).collect();
                let (txt, col) = if let Some(first) = active.iter().min_by_key(|e| e.target_epoch) {
                    let diff = (first.target_epoch - now).max(0);
                    let label = if diff >= 3600 {
                        format!("⏰ {}h{}m", diff / 3600, (diff % 3600) / 60)
                    } else if diff >= 60 {
                        format!("⏰ {}m", diff / 60)
                    } else {
                        format!("⏰ {}s", diff)
                    };
                    (label, theme.warning)
                } else {
                    ("No Alarm".to_string(), theme.text)
                };
                let elem = label::<Message>(&[(txt, col)], theme.font_size_normal);
                (elem, CapsuleAction::OpenOverlay("alarm".into()), theme.border)
            }
            "weather" => {
                let (txt, col) = match &telemetry.weather {
                    Some(w) => {
                        let (prefix, col) = match &w.alert {
                            crate::backend::WeatherAlert::Danger(_) => ("🚨 ", theme.danger),
                            crate::backend::WeatherAlert::Upcoming(_) => ("⚠️ ", theme.warning),
                            crate::backend::WeatherAlert::None => ("", theme.text),
                        };
                        (format!("{}{}°F/{}°C", prefix, w.temp_f, w.temp_c), col)
                    }
                    None => ("55°F/13°C".into(), theme.text),
                };
                let elem = label::<Message>(&[(txt, col)], theme.font_size_normal);
                (elem, CapsuleAction::OpenOverlay("weather".into()), col)
            }
            "unified" => {
                let (txt, col) = if telemetry.podman.is_rate_limited {
                    ("Miner: Limited!".to_string(), theme.warning)
                } else if telemetry.borg.is_active && !telemetry.borg.state_label.is_empty() {
                    (telemetry.borg.state_label.clone(), theme.success)
                } else if let Some(w) = telemetry.podman.watchers.iter().find(|w| w.is_running && !w.streamer.is_empty()) {
                    (format!("📺 {}", w.streamer), theme.success)
                } else if !telemetry.podman.summary_label.is_empty() {
                    (telemetry.podman.summary_label.clone(), theme.success)
                } else {
                    ("Sys OK".to_string(), theme.success)
                };
                let elem = label::<Message>(&[(txt, col)], theme.font_size_normal);
                (elem, CapsuleAction::OpenOverlay("unified".into()), theme.border)
            }
            "notify" | "notifications" => {
                let paused = std::path::Path::new("/dev/shm/mbs-dnd").exists();
                let held_count = crate::overlays::notifications::held_notifs_count();
                let base08_col = hex_to_color(&theme.palette.base08);
                let text_col = theme.text;

                let (segments, border_col) = if paused {
                    let status_str = if held_count > 0 {
                        format!(" Paused ({})", held_count)
                    } else {
                        " Paused".to_string()
                    };
                    (
                        vec![
                            ("Notifications:".to_string(), base08_col),
                            (status_str, base08_col),
                        ],
                        base08_col,
                    )
                } else {
                    let status_str = if held_count > 0 {
                        format!(" ({})", held_count)
                    } else {
                        " 0".to_string()
                    };
                    (
                        vec![
                            ("Notifications:".to_string(), text_col),
                            (status_str, text_col),
                        ],
                        theme.border,
                    )
                };
                let elem = label::<Message>(&segments, theme.font_size_normal);
                (elem, CapsuleAction::ToggleNotificationDnd, border_col)
            }
            "clock" => {
                let t = telemetry.bar_time.clone();
                let elem = label::<Message>(&[(t, theme.text)], theme.font_size_normal);
                (elem, CapsuleAction::OpenOverlay("clock".into()), theme.border)
            }
            "audio" => {
                let val_col = if telemetry.audio.is_muted { theme.danger } else { theme.text };
                let val_str = if telemetry.audio.is_muted { "Mute".to_string() } else { format!("{}%", telemetry.audio.volume_pct) };
                let elem = label::<Message>(&[
                    ("Audio:".to_string(), theme.success),
                    (" ".to_string(), theme.success),
                    (val_str, val_col),
                ], theme.font_size_normal);
                let border = if telemetry.audio.is_muted { theme.danger } else { theme.border };
                (elem, CapsuleAction::ToggleSinkMute, border)
            }
            "mic" => {
                let muted = telemetry.mic.is_muted;
                let val_col = if muted { theme.danger } else { theme.text };
                let val_str = if muted { "MUTED".to_string() } else { format!("{}%", telemetry.mic.volume_pct) };
                let elem = label::<Message>(&[
                    ("Mic:".to_string(), theme.success),
                    (" ".to_string(), theme.success),
                    (val_str, val_col),
                ], theme.font_size_normal);
                let border = if muted { theme.danger } else { theme.border };
                (elem, CapsuleAction::ToggleSourceMute, border)
            }
            "net" => {
                let down = if telemetry.net.down_str.is_empty() { "0.0Mb" } else { &telemetry.net.down_str };
                let up = if telemetry.net.up_str.is_empty() { "0.0Mb" } else { &telemetry.net.up_str };
                let ping = if telemetry.net.ping_str.is_empty() { "--" } else { &telemetry.net.ping_str };
                let ping_col = if ping.contains("OFFLIN") { theme.danger } else { theme.text };
                let elem = label::<Message>(&[
                    ("NET:".to_string(), theme.success),
                    (" ▼".to_string(), theme.text),
                    (down.to_string(), theme.text),
                    (" ▲".to_string(), theme.text),
                    (up.to_string(), theme.text),
                    (" ".to_string(), theme.text),
                    (ping.to_string(), ping_col),
                ], theme.font_size_normal);
                (elem, CapsuleAction::OpenOverlay("net".into()), theme.border)
            }
            "cpu" => {
                let border_col = if telemetry.cpu.temp_c >= 80 || telemetry.cpu.usage_pct >= 90 {
                    theme.danger
                } else if telemetry.cpu.temp_c >= 70 || telemetry.cpu.usage_pct >= 75 {
                    theme.warning
                } else {
                    theme.border
                };
                let elem = label::<Message>(&[
                    ("CPU:".to_string(), theme.success),
                    (" ".to_string(), theme.success),
                    (format!("{}% {}°C", telemetry.cpu.usage_pct, telemetry.cpu.temp_c), theme.text),
                ], theme.font_size_normal);
                (elem, CapsuleAction::OpenOverlay("cpu".into()), border_col)
            }
            "gpu" => {
                let temp_col = if telemetry.gpu.temp_c >= theme.gpu0_temp_danger { theme.danger } else if telemetry.gpu.temp_c >= theme.gpu0_temp_warn { theme.warning } else { theme.text };
                let vram_col = if telemetry.gpu.vram_free_gib <= theme.gpu0_vram_danger { theme.danger } else if telemetry.gpu.vram_free_gib <= theme.gpu0_vram_warn { theme.warning } else { theme.text };
                let name = if telemetry.gpu.name.is_empty() { "GPU".to_string() } else { telemetry.gpu.name.clone() };
                let elem = label::<Message>(&[
                    (format!("{}:", name), theme.success),
                    (" ".to_string(), theme.text),
                    (format!("{}%", telemetry.gpu.usage_pct), theme.text),
                    (" ".to_string(), theme.text),
                    (format!("{}°C", telemetry.gpu.temp_c), temp_col),
                    (" ".to_string(), theme.text),
                    (format!("{}W", telemetry.gpu.power_w), theme.text),
                    (" ".to_string(), theme.text),
                    (format!("{}GiB", telemetry.gpu.vram_free_gib), vram_col),
                ], theme.font_size_normal);
                let border_col = if telemetry.gpu.temp_c >= theme.gpu0_temp_danger || telemetry.gpu.vram_free_gib <= theme.gpu0_vram_danger { theme.danger } else if telemetry.gpu.temp_c >= theme.gpu0_temp_warn || telemetry.gpu.vram_free_gib <= theme.gpu0_vram_warn { theme.warning } else { theme.border };
                (elem, CapsuleAction::OpenOverlay("gpu".into()), border_col)
            }
            "ram" => {
                let (display, total) = if telemetry.ram.effective_avail_gib > 0.0 {
                    (telemetry.ram.effective_avail_gib, telemetry.ram.total_gib + telemetry.ram.zram_saved_gib)
                } else {
                    (telemetry.ram.available_gib, telemetry.ram.total_gib)
                };
                let usage_ratio = if total > 0.0 { ((total - display).max(0.0)) / total } else { 0.0 };
                let val_col = if usage_ratio >= 0.85 { theme.danger } else if usage_ratio >= 0.50 { theme.warning } else { theme.text };
                let val_str = if display == 0.0 { " -- GiB".to_string() } else { format!(" {:.1} GiB", display) };
                let elem = label::<Message>(&[
                    ("RAM:".to_string(), theme.success),
                    (val_str, val_col),
                ], theme.font_size_normal);
                let border_col = if usage_ratio >= 0.85 { theme.danger } else if usage_ratio >= 0.50 { theme.warning } else { theme.border };
                (elem, CapsuleAction::OpenOverlay("ram".into()), border_col)
            }
            "battery" => {
                if !telemetry.battery.has_battery {
                    let elem = label::<Message>(&[
                        ("⚡ AC:".to_string(), theme.text),
                        (" Online".to_string(), theme.success),
                    ], theme.font_size_normal);
                    (elem, CapsuleAction::OpenOverlay("power".into()), theme.border)
                } else {
                    let pct = telemetry.battery.percent;
                    let status = telemetry.battery.status.to_lowercase();
                    let icon = if status.contains("charging") { "⚡ " } else if pct <= 20 { "🪫 " } else { "🔋 " };
                    let val_col = if status.contains("charging") || status.contains("full") || status.contains("mains") { theme.success } else if pct <= 20 { theme.danger } else if pct <= 40 { theme.warning } else { theme.text };
                    let elem = label::<Message>(&[
                        (format!("{}BAT:", icon), theme.text),
                        (format!(" {}% ({:.1}W)", pct, telemetry.battery.power_w), val_col),
                    ], theme.font_size_normal);
                    (elem, CapsuleAction::OpenOverlay("power".into()), val_col)
                }
            }
            "custom" => {
                let col = theme.text;
                let elem = label::<Message>(&[("Custom".to_string(), col)], theme.font_size_normal);
                (elem, CapsuleAction::ToggleCustomMode, col)
            }
            "tray" => {
                let arrow = match region {
                    "left"  => "\u{25B6}",
                    "right" => "\u{25C0}",
                    _       => "\u{25BC}",
                };
                let elem = label::<Message>(&[(arrow.to_string(), theme.text)], theme.font_size_normal);
                (elem, CapsuleAction::ToggleTrayExpanded, theme.text)
            }
            _ => {
                let elem = label::<Message>(&[(id.to_uppercase(), theme.text)], theme.font_size_normal);
                (elem, CapsuleAction::OpenOverlay(id.to_string()), theme.border)
            }
        };

        let text_w = LABEL_W.with(|w| w.get());
        let min_width = text_w + theme.slant_width * 2.0 + 24.0;

        let border_color = if border_color == theme.border { theme.text } else { border_color };

        let shape_type = match module_shape {
            "hexagon" => ShapeType::Hexagon { cut: (theme.slant_width * 1.5) as u16 },
            "rounded" => ShapeType::Rounded(((theme.slant_width * 1.5) as u16).max(4)),
            "flat" => ShapeType::Flat,
            _ => ShapeType::Slant { left: slant_left, right: slant_right },
        };

        let background_shape = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(shape_type)
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(border_color, theme.control_border_width)
            .slant_width(theme.slant_width)
            .size(Length::Fixed(min_width), Length::Fill);

        let button_content = container(content)
            .width(Length::Fixed(min_width))
            .height(Length::Fill)
            .align_x(Alignment::Center)
            .align_y(Alignment::Center);

        button(Stack::new().push(background_shape.view()).push(button_content))
            .on_press(on_action(action))
            .padding(0)
            .width(Length::Fixed(min_width))
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: None,
                ..Default::default()
            })
            .into()
    }

    pub fn render_tray_expanded<'a, Message: 'static + Clone>(
        items: &[crate::backend::TrayItem],
        theme: &ShellTheme,
        slant_left: SlantSide,
        slant_right: SlantSide,
        region: &str,
        on_action: impl Fn(CapsuleAction) -> Message + 'static + Copy,
    ) -> Element<'a, Message> {
        let icon_size = 20.0_f32;
        let icon_pad = 4.0_f32;
        let icon_slot = icon_size + icon_pad * 2.0;
        let close_slot = 24.0_f32;
        let spacing = 4.0_f32;

        let shown = items.len().min(8);
        let empty_w = if items.is_empty() { 150.0 } else { 0.0 };
        let content_w = close_slot
            + if shown > 0 { spacing } else { 0.0 }
            + (icon_slot + spacing) * shown as f32
            + empty_w;
        let min_width = content_w + theme.slant_width * 2.0 + 32.0;

        let hover_bg = theme.surface_alt;
        let fg = theme.text;

        let close_btn = button(text("\u{2715}").size(14).color(theme.muted))
            .on_press(on_action(CapsuleAction::ToggleTrayExpanded))
            .padding(0)
            .width(Length::Fixed(close_slot))
            .height(Length::Fixed(close_slot))
            .style(move |_t, status| {
                let bg = match status {
                    button::Status::Hovered | button::Status::Pressed =>
                        Some(iced::Background::Color(hover_bg)),
                    _ => None,
                };
                button::Style {
                    background: bg,
                    border: iced::Border { radius: 4.0.into(), ..Default::default() },
                    text_color: fg,
                    ..Default::default()
                }
            });

        let mut icons_row = Row::new().spacing(spacing).align_y(Alignment::Center);
        for item in items.iter().take(shown) {
            let icon_elem: Element<'a, Message> = if let Some(p) = &item.icon_path {
                iced::widget::image(iced::widget::image::Handle::from_path(p.clone()))
                    .width(Length::Fixed(icon_size))
                    .height(Length::Fixed(icon_size))
                    .into()
            } else {
                text("\u{25CF}").size(icon_size * 0.7).color(theme.success).into()
            };

            let activate_item = item.clone();
            let ctx_item = item.clone();

            let icon_box = container(icon_elem)
                .padding(icon_pad)
                .center_x(Length::Fixed(icon_slot))
                .center_y(Length::Fixed(icon_slot));

            let clickable = MouseArea::new(icon_box)
                .on_press(on_action(CapsuleAction::TrayActivate(activate_item)))
                .on_right_press(on_action(CapsuleAction::TrayContextMenu(ctx_item)));

            let interactive: Element<'a, Message> = if !item.tooltip.is_empty() {
                let tt_text = item.tooltip.clone();
                let tt_bg = theme.surface_alt;
                let tt_fg = theme.text;
                let tt_border = theme.border;
                tooltip(
                    clickable,
                    container(text(tt_text).size(11).color(tt_fg))
                        .padding(6)
                        .style(move |_t| container::Style {
                            background: Some(iced::Background::Color(tt_bg)),
                            border: iced::Border {
                                color: tt_border,
                                width: 1.0,
                                radius: 4.0.into(),
                            },
                            ..Default::default()
                        }),
                    tooltip::Position::Bottom,
                )
                .into()
            } else {
                clickable.into()
            };

            let _ = hover_bg; // kept for signature compat; tooltip handles hover styling
            icons_row = icons_row.push(interactive);
        }

        let pad_x = theme.slant_width.round() as u16;
        let mut content = Row::new()
            .push(close_btn)
            .push(icons_row)
            .spacing(spacing)
            .align_y(Alignment::Center)
            .padding([0, pad_x]);
        if items.is_empty() {
            content = content.push(
                text("No tray apps — restart Steam/CoreCtrl/etc.")
                    .size(12)
                    .color(theme.muted),
            );
        }

        let shape_type = ShapeType::Slant { left: slant_left, right: slant_right };
        let _ = region; // region inferred from sl/sr now

        let background_shape = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(shape_type)
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.text, theme.control_border_width)
            .slant_width(theme.slant_width)
            .size(Length::Fixed(min_width), Length::Fill);

        Stack::new()
            .push(background_shape.view())
            .push(
                container(content)
                    .width(Length::Fixed(min_width))
                    .height(Length::Fill)
                    .align_x(Alignment::Center)
                    .align_y(Alignment::Center),
            )
            .into()
    }

}
\n
=== FILE: ./src/bar/top_bar.rs ===
use iced::widget::{container, row, Row, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Element, Length};

use super::capsules::{CapsuleAction, CapsuleRenderer};
use crate::backend::TrayItem;
use crate::backend::UnifiedTelemetry;
use crate::config::settings::ShellSettings;
use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType, SlantSide};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum TopBarMessage {
    Action(CapsuleAction),
    Noop,
}

pub struct TopBar;



impl TopBar {
    pub fn view<'a>(
        settings: &ShellSettings,
        theme: &ShellTheme,
        telemetry: &'a UnifiedTelemetry,
        tray_expanded: bool,
        tray_items: &'a [TrayItem],
    ) -> Element<'a, TopBarMessage> {
        let spacing_px = settings.capsule_spacing as f32;

        let mut left_row = Row::new().spacing(spacing_px).align_y(Alignment::Center);
        for id in &settings.bar_left {
            if settings.visible_capsules.get(id).copied().unwrap_or(true) {
                let (sl, sr) = match settings.slant_mode.as_str() {
                    "left" => (SlantSide::Left, SlantSide::Left),
                    "right" => (SlantSide::Right, SlantSide::Right),
                    _ => (SlantSide::Left, SlantSide::Left), // Slant towards center
                };
                if id == "tray" && tray_expanded {
                    left_row = left_row.push(CapsuleRenderer::render_tray_expanded(
                        tray_items, theme, sl, sr, "left", TopBarMessage::Action,
                    ));
                } else {
                    left_row = left_row.push(CapsuleRenderer::render(
                        id,
                        telemetry,
                        theme,
                        sl,
                        sr,
                        &settings.module_shape,
                        "left",
                        TopBarMessage::Action,
                    ));
                }
            }
        }

        let mut center_row = Row::new().spacing(spacing_px).align_y(Alignment::Center);
        let visible_center: Vec<&String> = settings.bar_center.iter()
            .filter(|id| settings.visible_capsules.get(*id).copied().unwrap_or(true))
            .collect();
        let keystone_idx = visible_center.len() / 2;

        for (i, id) in visible_center.iter().enumerate() {
            let (sl, sr) = match settings.slant_mode.as_str() {
                "left" => (SlantSide::Left, SlantSide::Left),
                "right" => (SlantSide::Right, SlantSide::Right),
                _ => {
                    if i == keystone_idx {
                        (SlantSide::Left, SlantSide::Right) // Keystone /\
                    } else if i < keystone_idx {
                        (SlantSide::Left, SlantSide::Left) // Left of keystone //
                    } else {
                        (SlantSide::Right, SlantSide::Right) // Right of keystone \\
                    }
                }
            };
            if id.as_str() == "tray" && tray_expanded {
                center_row = center_row.push(CapsuleRenderer::render_tray_expanded(
                    tray_items, theme, sl, sr, "center", TopBarMessage::Action,
                ));
            } else {
                center_row = center_row.push(CapsuleRenderer::render(
                    id,
                    telemetry,
                    theme,
                    sl,
                    sr,
                    &settings.module_shape,
                    "center",
                    TopBarMessage::Action,
                ));
            }
        }

        let mut right_row = Row::new().spacing(spacing_px).align_y(Alignment::Center);
        for id in &settings.bar_right {
            if settings.visible_capsules.get(id).copied().unwrap_or(true) {
                let (sl, sr) = match settings.slant_mode.as_str() {
                    "left" => (SlantSide::Left, SlantSide::Left),
                    "right" => (SlantSide::Right, SlantSide::Right),
                    _ => (SlantSide::Right, SlantSide::Right), // Slant towards center
                };
                if id == "tray" && tray_expanded {
                    right_row = right_row.push(CapsuleRenderer::render_tray_expanded(
                        tray_items, theme, sl, sr, "right", TopBarMessage::Action,
                    ));
                } else {
                    right_row = right_row.push(CapsuleRenderer::render(
                        id,
                        telemetry,
                        theme,
                        sl,
                        sr,
                        &settings.module_shape,
                        "right",
                        TopBarMessage::Action,
                    ));
                }
            }
        }

        let bar_shape = match settings.bar_shape.as_str() {
            "hexagon" => ShapeType::Hexagon {
                cut: (theme.slant_width * 1.5) as u16,
            },
            "rounded" => ShapeType::Rounded(((theme.slant_width * 1.5) as u16).max(4)),
            "flat" => ShapeType::Flat,
            _ => ShapeType::Slant {
                left: SlantSide::Left,
                right: SlantSide::Right,
            },
        };

        let bar_background = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(bar_shape)
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.border, theme.border_width)
            .slant_width(theme.slant_width * 1.5);

        let content = row![
            left_row,
            row![].width(Length::Fill),
            center_row,
            row![].width(Length::Fill),
            right_row,
        ]
        .padding([4, 16])
        .align_y(Alignment::Center)
        .width(Length::Fill)
        .height(Length::Fixed(settings.bar_height as f32));

        container(Stack::new().push(bar_background.view()).push(content))
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .height(Length::Fixed(settings.bar_height as f32))
            .into()
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/overlays/calc.rs ===
use iced::widget::{button, column, container, text, text_input, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length, Padding};
use tokio::process::Command;

use crate::config::settings::ShellSettings;
use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum CalcMessage {
    InputChanged(String),
    CopyResult(String),
    Dismiss,
}

pub struct CalculatorOverlay {
    pub input_text: String,
    pub result_text: String,
}

impl Default for CalculatorOverlay {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl CalculatorOverlay {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            input_text: String::new(),
            result_text: String::new(),
        }
    }

    pub fn update(&mut self, message: CalcMessage) {
        if let CalcMessage::InputChanged(val) = message {
            self.input_text = val.clone();
            self.result_text = Self::evaluate(&val);
        }
    }

    pub fn evaluate(expr: &str) -> String {
        let clean = expr.trim().trim_start_matches('=').trim();
        if clean.is_empty() {
            return String::new();
        }

        if let Some(res) = Self::eval_units(clean) {
            return res;
        }

        match Self::eval_math(clean) {
            Ok(v) => format!("{:.6}", v)
                .trim_end_matches('0')
                .trim_end_matches('.')
                .to_string(),
            Err(_) => "Syntax Error".to_string(),
        }
    }

    fn eval_math(expr: &str) -> Result<f64, ()> {
        let mut tokens = Vec::new();
        let mut num_buf = String::new();

        for c in expr.chars() {
            if c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '.' {
                num_buf.push(c);
            } else {
                if !num_buf.is_empty() {
                    tokens.push(num_buf.clone());
                    num_buf.clear();
                }
                if !c.is_whitespace() {
                    tokens.push(c.to_string());
                }
            }
        }
        if !num_buf.is_empty() {
            tokens.push(num_buf);
        }

        let mut it = tokens.iter().peekable();
        Self::parse_expr(&mut it)
    }

    fn parse_expr<'a>(it: &mut std::iter::Peekable<std::slice::Iter<'a, String>>) -> Result<f64, ()> {
        let mut left = Self::parse_term(it)?;
        while let Some(&op) = it.peek() {
            if op == "+" {
                it.next();
                left += Self::parse_term(it)?;
            } else if op == "-" {
                it.next();
                left -= Self::parse_term(it)?;
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
        Ok(left)
    }

    fn parse_term<'a>(it: &mut std::iter::Peekable<std::slice::Iter<'a, String>>) -> Result<f64, ()> {
        let mut left = Self::parse_factor(it)?;
        while let Some(&op) = it.peek() {
            if op == "*" {
                it.next();
                left *= Self::parse_factor(it)?;
            } else if op == "/" {
                it.next();
                let denom = Self::parse_factor(it)?;
                if denom == 0.0 {
                    return Err(());
                }
                left /= denom;
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
        Ok(left)
    }

    fn parse_factor<'a>(it: &mut std::iter::Peekable<std::slice::Iter<'a, String>>) -> Result<f64, ()> {
        let token = it.next().ok_or(())?;
        if token == "(" {
            let val = Self::parse_expr(it)?;
            if it.next().map(|s| s.as_str()) != Some(")") {
                return Err(());
            }
            Ok(val)
        } else if token == "-" {
            Ok(-Self::parse_factor(it)?)
        } else {
            token.parse::<f64>().map_err(|_| ())
        }
    }

    fn eval_units(expr: &str) -> Option<String> {
        let parts: Vec<&str> = expr.split_whitespace().collect();
        if parts.len() == 4 && (parts[2].eq_ignore_ascii_case("in") || parts[2].eq_ignore_ascii_case("to")) {
            let val = parts[0].parse::<f64>().ok()?;
            let from = parts[1].to_lowercase();
            let to = parts[3].to_lowercase();

            if from == "c" && to == "f" {
                return Some(format!("{:.2} °F", val * 9.0 / 5.0 + 32.0));
            } else if from == "f" && to == "c" {
                return Some(format!("{:.2} °C", (val - 32.0) * 5.0 / 9.0));
            } else if from == "kg" && to == "lbs" {
                return Some(format!("{:.2} lbs", val * 2.20462));
            } else if from == "lbs" && to == "kg" {
                return Some(format!("{:.2} kg", val / 2.20462));
            } else if from == "km" && to == "mi" {
                return Some(format!("{:.2} mi", val * 0.621371));
            } else if from == "mi" && to == "km" {
                return Some(format!("{:.2} km", val / 0.621371));
            }
        }
        None
    }

    pub async fn copy_to_clipboard(val: &str) {
        if val.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        let _ = Command::new("wl-copy").arg(val).status().await;
        let _ = Command::new("notify-send")
            .args(["-a", "Calculator", "Copied to Clipboard", val])
            .spawn();
    }

    pub fn view<'a>(&'a self, theme: &ShellTheme, settings: &ShellSettings) -> Element<'a, CalcMessage> {
        let (win_w, win_h) = settings.get_window_size("calc", 820, 580);

        let cut_f = theme.overlay_cut;
        let angle_deg = theme.overlay_angle;
        let rad = angle_deg.to_radians();
        let sqrt2 = std::f32::consts::SQRT_2;
        let dx = (cut_f * rad.cos() * sqrt2).min(win_w * 0.45).max(0.0);
        let dy = (cut_f * rad.sin() * sqrt2).min(win_h * 0.45).max(0.0);

        let header_inset = if dy > 22.0 {
            (dx * (1.0 - (22.0 / dy))).max(0.0) + 16.0
        } else {
            16.0
        };
        let footer_inset = if dy > 20.0 {
            (dx * (1.0 - (20.0 / dy))).max(0.0) + 16.0
        } else {
            16.0
        };

        let input = text_input("Type math or unit conversion (e.g. 50 * 12, 100 kg in lbs)...", &self.input_text)
            .on_input(CalcMessage::InputChanged)
            .on_submit(CalcMessage::CopyResult(self.result_text.clone()))
            .padding(14)
            .size(theme.font_size_overlay);

        let input_row = container(input)
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .padding(Padding {
                top: 0.0,
                right: header_inset,
                bottom: 0.0,
                left: header_inset,
            });

        let result_display = text(&self.result_text)
            .size(theme.font_size_overlay + 16)
            .color(theme.success);

        let card_bg = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::AngledHexagon { cut: cut_f, angle_deg })
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.border, theme.border_width)
            .size(Length::Fixed(win_w), Length::Fixed(win_h));

        let header_row = container(
            text("🧮 CALCULATOR & UNIT CONVERSION")
                .size(theme.font_size_overlay)
                .color(theme.text)
        )
        .width(Length::Fill)
        .padding(Padding {
            top: 0.0,
            right: header_inset,
            bottom: 0.0,
            left: header_inset,
        });

        let footer_row = container(
            text("Press [Enter] to copy result  •  [ESC] to dismiss")
                .size(11)
                .color(theme.muted)
                .align_x(Alignment::Center)
        )
        .width(Length::Fill)
        .padding(Padding {
            top: 0.0,
            right: footer_inset,
            bottom: 0.0,
            left: footer_inset,
        });

        let card_content = container(
            column![
                header_row,
                input_row,
                container(result_display).padding(20).center_x(Length::Fill),
                footer_row,
            ]
            .spacing(16)
            .padding(20),
        )
        .width(Length::Fixed(win_w))
        .height(Length::Fixed(win_h));

        let card = Stack::new().push(card_bg.view()).push(card_content);

        let backdrop = button(container(column![]).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fill))
            .on_press(CalcMessage::Dismiss)
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgba8(0, 0, 0, 0.65))),
                ..Default::default()
            });

        Stack::new()
            .push(backdrop)
            .push(container(card).center_x(Length::Fill).center_y(Length::Fill))
            .into()
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/overlays/clipboard.rs ===
use iced::widget::{button, column, container, row, scrollable, text, text_input, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length, Padding};
use std::path::PathBuf;
use tokio::process::Command;

use crate::config::settings::ShellSettings;
use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ClipItem {
    pub id: u64,
    pub text: String,
    pub is_image: bool,
    pub thumb_path: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum ClipboardMessage {
    SearchChanged(String),
    SelectEntry(u64),
    SelectNext,
    SelectPrev,
    CopySelected,
    CopyEntry(u64),
    DeleteEntry(u64),
    DeleteSelected,
    WipeAll,
    RunOcr(u64),
    Reloaded(Vec<ClipItem>),
    PreviewLoaded { text: String, image_path: Option<String> },
    Dismiss,
}

pub struct ClipboardOverlay {
    pub search_query: String,
    pub entries: Vec<ClipItem>,
    pub selected_id: Option<u64>,
    pub preview_text: String,
    pub preview_image_path: Option<String>,
}

impl Default for ClipboardOverlay {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl ClipboardOverlay {
    pub fn filtered_ids(&self) -> Vec<u64> {
        self.entries.iter()
            .filter(|e| self.search_query.is_empty() || e.text.to_lowercase().contains(&self.search_query.to_lowercase()))
            .map(|e| e.id)
            .collect()
    }

    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            search_query: String::new(),
            entries: Vec::new(),
            selected_id: None,
            preview_text: String::new(),
            preview_image_path: None,
        }
    }

    pub async fn reload() -> Vec<ClipItem> {
        let cache_dir = dirs::cache_dir().unwrap_or_else(std::env::temp_dir);
        let map_path = cache_dir.join("mbs-clip-names.txt");
        let mut name_map = std::collections::HashMap::new();
        
        if let Ok(map_content) = std::fs::read_to_string(&map_path) {
            for line in map_content.lines() {
                if let Some((id_str, name)) = line.split_once('\t') {
                    if let Ok(id) = id_str.parse::<u64>() {
                        name_map.insert(id, name.to_string());
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        let Ok(out) = Command::new("cliphist").arg("list").output().await else {
            return Vec::new();
        };

        let raw = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
        let mut list = Vec::new();
        let tmp_dir = std::env::temp_dir();

        for line in raw.lines() {
            if let Some((id_str, content)) = line.split_once('\t') {
                if let Ok(id) = id_str.trim().parse::<u64>() {
                    let is_image = content.contains("[[ binary data") || content.contains("binary data");
                    let thumb_path = if is_image {
                        let thumb = tmp_dir.join(format!("mbs-clip-thumb-{}.png", id));
                        let decode = Command::new("cliphist")
                            .args(["decode", &id.to_string()])
                            .output()
                            .await;
                        match decode {
                            Ok(d) if !d.stdout.is_empty() => {
                                if std::fs::write(&thumb, &d.stdout).is_ok() {
                                    Some(thumb.to_string_lossy().into_owned())
                                } else { None }
                            }
                            _ => None,
                        }
                    } else { None };

                    // Use mapped filename if it exists
                    let text_val = if is_image {
                        if let Some(name) = name_map.get(&id) {
                            format!("📸 {}", name)
                        } else {
                            let clean = content.replace("[[ binary data ", "").replace(" ]]", "");
                            format!("📸 Screenshot {}", clean.trim())
                        }
                    } else {
                        content.to_string()
                    };

                    list.push(ClipItem {
                        id,
                        text: text_val,
                        is_image,
                        thumb_path,
                    });
                }
            }
        }
        list
    }

    pub async fn load_preview(id: u64) -> (String, Option<String>) {
        let out = match Command::new("cliphist")
            .args(["decode", &id.to_string()])
            .output()
            .await
        {
            Ok(o) => o,
            Err(_) => return (String::new(), None),
        };
        if out.stdout.is_empty() { return (String::new(), None); }
        if out.stdout.len() > 8 * 1024 * 1024 {
            return ("[entry too large to preview]".into(), None);
        }
        match String::from_utf8(out.stdout.clone()) {
            Ok(text) => (text, None),
            Err(_) => {
                let tmp = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("mbs-clip-preview-{}.png", id));
                if std::fs::write(&tmp, &out.stdout).is_ok() {
                    (String::new(), Some(tmp.to_string_lossy().into_owned()))
                } else {
                    (String::new(), None)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    pub async fn copy(id: u64) {
        let decode = match Command::new("cliphist")
            .args(["decode", &id.to_string()])
            .output()
            .await
        {
            Ok(d) => d,
            Err(e) => { eprintln!("[clipboard] cliphist decode failed: {e}"); return; }
        };
        if decode.stdout.is_empty() { return; }
        let mut child = match Command::new("wl-copy")
            .stdin(std::process::Stdio::piped())
            .spawn()
        {
            Ok(c) => c,
            Err(e) => { eprintln!("[clipboard] wl-copy spawn failed: {e}"); return; }
        };
        if let Some(mut stdin) = child.stdin.take() {
            use tokio::io::AsyncWriteExt;
            let _ = stdin.write_all(&decode.stdout).await;
            drop(stdin);
        }
        let _ = child.wait().await;
    }

    pub async fn delete(id: u64) {
        let child = Command::new("cliphist")
            .arg("delete")
            .stdin(std::process::Stdio::piped())
            .spawn()
            .ok();
        if let Some(mut c) = child {
            use tokio::io::AsyncWriteExt;
            if let Some(mut stdin) = c.stdin.take() {
                let _ = stdin.write_all(format!("{}\t\n", id).as_bytes()).await;
            }
        }
    }

    pub async fn wipe() {
        let _ = Command::new("cliphist").arg("wipe").status().await;
    }

    pub async fn ocr(id: u64) -> String {
        let runtime = std::env::var("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR").unwrap_or_else(|_| "/tmp".into());
        let tmp_img = PathBuf::from(runtime).join(format!("qs_ocr_{}.png", id));

        if let Ok(decode) = Command::new("cliphist")
            .args(["decode", &id.to_string()])
            .output()
            .await
        {
            if std::fs::write(&tmp_img, &decode.stdout).is_ok() {
                if let Ok(tess) = Command::new("tesseract")
                    .args([tmp_img.to_str().unwrap(), "stdout"])
                    .output()
                    .await
                {
                    let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&tmp_img);
                    return String::from_utf8_lossy(&tess.stdout).trim().to_string();
                }
            }
        }
        String::new()
    }

    pub fn view<'a>(&'a self, theme: &'a ShellTheme, settings: &ShellSettings) -> Element<'a, ClipboardMessage> {
        let is_image_selected = self.selected_id.and_then(|id| self.entries.iter().find(|e| e.id == id)).map(|e| e.is_image).unwrap_or(false);

        let (win_w, win_h) = settings.get_window_size("clipboard", 1000, 700);
        let cut = theme.overlay_cut as u16;

        let cut_f = theme.overlay_cut;
        let angle_deg = theme.overlay_angle;
        let rad = angle_deg.to_radians();
        let sqrt2 = std::f32::consts::SQRT_2;
        let dx = (cut_f * rad.cos() * sqrt2).min(win_w * 0.45).max(0.0);
        let dy = (cut_f * rad.sin() * sqrt2).min(win_h * 0.45).max(0.0);

        let pad_top = 18.0_f32;
        let pad_bottom = 16.0_f32;

        let search_inward = if dy > pad_top {
            (dx * (1.0 - (pad_top / dy))).max(0.0) + 16.0
        } else {
            16.0
        };

        let list_top_inset = if dy > 72.0 {
            (dx * (1.0 - (72.0 / dy))).max(0.0) + 16.0
        } else {
            16.0
        };
        let list_bottom_inset = if dy > 45.0 {
            (dx * (1.0 - (45.0 / dy))).max(0.0) + 16.0
        } else {
            16.0
        };
        let list_side_inset = list_top_inset.max(list_bottom_inset).max(20.0);

        // Header
        let search_input = text_input("Search clipboard...", &self.search_query)
            .on_input(ClipboardMessage::SearchChanged)
            .padding(Padding { top: 12.0, right: 24.0, bottom: 12.0, left: 24.0 })
            .size(theme.font_size_overlay)
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .style(move |_t, _s| text_input::Style {
                background: iced::Background::Color(Color::TRANSPARENT),
                border: iced::Border::default(),
                icon: theme.muted,
                placeholder: theme.muted,
                value: theme.text,
                selection: theme.surface_alt,
            });

        let search_bg = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Hexagon { cut })
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.border, theme.control_border_width)
            .size(Length::Fill, Length::Fixed(48.0));

        let search_bar = container(
            Stack::new()
                .push(search_bg.view())
                .push(search_input)
        ).height(Length::Fixed(48.0));

        let wipe_btn = button(
            container(text("Wipe All").size(theme.font_size_overlay).color(theme.danger))
                .center_y(Length::Fill)
                .center_x(Length::Fill)
        )
        .on_press(ClipboardMessage::WipeAll)
        .height(Length::Fixed(48.0))
        .width(Length::Fixed(100.0))
        .style(move |_t, _s| button::Style {
            background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::TRANSPARENT)),
            border: iced::Border::default(),
            ..Default::default()
        });

        let wipe_bg = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Hexagon { cut })
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.danger, theme.control_border_width)
            .size(Length::Fixed(100.0), Length::Fixed(48.0));

        let ocr_btn = button(
            container(text("OCR").size(theme.font_size_overlay).color(if is_image_selected { theme.text } else { theme.muted }))
                .center_y(Length::Fill)
                .center_x(Length::Fill)
        )
        .on_press_maybe(Some(ClipboardMessage::RunOcr(
            self.selected_id.unwrap_or(0)
        )))
        .height(Length::Fixed(48.0))
        .width(Length::Fixed(80.0))
        .style(move |_t, _s| button::Style {
            background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::TRANSPARENT)),
            border: iced::Border::default(),
            ..Default::default()
        });

        let ocr_bg = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Hexagon { cut })
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(if is_image_selected { theme.text } else { theme.border }, theme.control_border_width)
            .size(Length::Fixed(80.0), Length::Fixed(48.0));

        let ocr_bar = container(
            Stack::new()
                .push(ocr_bg.view())
                .push(ocr_btn)
        ).height(Length::Fixed(48.0)).width(Length::Fixed(80.0));

        let wipe_bar = container(
            Stack::new()
                .push(wipe_bg.view())
                .push(wipe_btn)
        ).height(Length::Fixed(48.0)).width(Length::Fixed(100.0));

        let input_row = container(
            row![search_bar, ocr_bar, wipe_bar].spacing(12).align_y(Alignment::Center)
        )
        .width(Length::Fill)
        .padding(Padding { top: 0.0, right: search_inward, bottom: 0.0, left: search_inward });

        // Left Pane (List)
        let mut list_col = column![].spacing(8);
        for item in &self.entries {
            if !self.search_query.is_empty() && !item.text.to_lowercase().contains(&self.search_query.to_lowercase()) {
                continue;
            }
            let is_sel = self.selected_id == Some(item.id);
            let border_color = if is_sel { theme.danger } else { theme.border }; // Base08 outline
            let text_color = if is_sel { theme.text } else { theme.text }; // Keep text normal

            let raw_text = item.text.lines().next().unwrap_or("").to_string();
            
            let display_text = if raw_text.chars().count() > 30 {
                let truncated: String = raw_text.chars().take(30).collect();
                format!("{}...", truncated)
            } else {
                raw_text
            };

            let mut item_row = row![].spacing(12).align_y(Alignment::Center);
            if let Some(thumb) = &item.thumb_path {
                item_row = item_row.push(
                    iced::widget::image(iced::widget::image::Handle::from_path(thumb.clone()))
                        .width(Length::Fixed(40.0))
                        .height(Length::Fixed(40.0))
                        .content_fit(iced::ContentFit::Contain)
                );
            } else {
                item_row = item_row.push(text("■").size(10).color(theme.muted));
            }
            
            // Force the text to stay strictly within the box
            let text_container = container(text(display_text).size(theme.font_size_overlay).color(text_color))
                .width(Length::Fill)
                .clip(true);
            item_row = item_row.push(text_container);

            let btn_content = container(item_row)
                .padding(Padding { top: 0.0, right: 24.0, bottom: 0.0, left: 24.0 })
                .width(Length::Fill)
                .height(Length::Fixed(56.0))
                .align_y(Alignment::Center);

            let item_bg = ShapeBox::new()
                .shape(ShapeType::Hexagon { cut })
                .background(theme.background) // Always dark background
                .border(border_color, theme.control_border_width)
                .size(Length::Fill, Length::Fixed(56.0));

            let item_btn = button(
                Stack::new()
                    .push(item_bg.view())
                    .push(btn_content)
            )
            .on_press(ClipboardMessage::SelectEntry(item.id))
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .height(Length::Fixed(56.0))
            .style(move |_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::TRANSPARENT)),
                border: iced::Border::default(),
                ..Default::default()
            });

            list_col = list_col.push(item_btn);
        }

        let left_pane = container(
            scrollable(list_col).id(iced::widget::scrollable::Id::new("clip_list")).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fill)
        )
        .width(Length::FillPortion(1))
        .height(Length::Fill)
        .clip(true)
        .padding(Padding {
            top: list_top_inset,
            right: 24.0, // Increased to keep scrollbar away from right pane
            bottom: list_bottom_inset,
            left: list_side_inset,
        });

        // Right Pane (Preview)
        // Dynamic padding based on the chamfer cut to prevent spilling out of the octagon
        let inner_pad = cut_f + 4.0; // Tight padding: just enough to clear the chamfer
        let inner_padding = Padding { top: inner_pad, right: inner_pad, bottom: inner_pad, left: inner_pad };

        let preview_content: Element<'a, ClipboardMessage> = if let Some(img_path) = &self.preview_image_path {
            container(
                container(
                    iced::widget::image(iced::widget::image::Handle::from_path(img_path.clone()))
                        .width(Length::Fill)
                        .content_fit(iced::ContentFit::Contain)
                )
                .padding(inner_padding)
                .center_x(Length::Fill).center_y(Length::Fill).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fill)
            ).into()
        } else if !self.preview_text.is_empty() {
            container(
                scrollable(
                    container(
                        text(&self.preview_text)
                            .size(theme.font_size_overlay)
                            .font(iced::Font::MONOSPACE)
                            .color(theme.text)
                            .wrapping(iced::widget::text::Wrapping::WordOrGlyph)
                    ).width(Length::Fill)
                ).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fill)
            ).padding(inner_padding).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fill).into()
        } else {
            container(text("Select an entry to preview").size(theme.font_size_overlay).color(theme.muted))
                .center_x(Length::Fill)
                .center_y(Length::Fill)
                .width(Length::Fill)
                .height(Length::Fill)
                .into()
        };

        let right_bg = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Hexagon { cut })
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.border, theme.control_border_width)
            .size(Length::Fill, Length::Fill);

        // Wrap the Stack in a container with .clip(true) so the text inside the right pane doesn't bleed out
        let right_pane = container(
            Stack::new()
                .push(right_bg.view())
                .push(preview_content)
        )
        .width(Length::FillPortion(2))
        .clip(true)
        .height(Length::Fill)
        .clip(true);

        let content = column![
            input_row,
            row![left_pane, right_pane].spacing(16).height(Length::Fill).width(Length::Fill)
        ]
        .spacing(16)
        .padding(Padding { top: pad_top, right: 0.0, bottom: pad_bottom, left: 0.0 })
        .width(Length::Fixed(win_w))
        .height(Length::Fixed(win_h));

        let bg_shape = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Hexagon { cut })
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.border, theme.control_border_width)
            .size(Length::Fixed(win_w), Length::Fixed(win_h));

        container(
            Stack::new()
                .push(bg_shape.view())
                .push(content)
        )
        .width(Length::Fill)
        .height(Length::Fill)
        .center_x(Length::Fill)
        .center_y(Length::Fill)
        .into()
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/overlays/notes.rs ===
use chrono::Local;
use iced::widget::{button, column, container, row, scrollable, text, text_input, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::fs;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use tokio::process::Command;

use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct NoteItem {
    pub id: u64,
    pub content: String,
    pub ts: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum NotesMessage {
    SearchOrNewChanged(String),
    AddNote,
    DeleteNote(u64),
    CopyNote(String),
    Dismiss,
}

pub struct NotesOverlay {
    pub input_text: String,
    pub notes: Vec<NoteItem>,
}

impl Default for NotesOverlay {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl NotesOverlay {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            input_text: String::new(),
            notes: Self::load(),
        }
    }

    fn storage_path() -> PathBuf {
        let base = dirs::data_dir().unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/tmp"));
        base.join("moon-beauty-shell").join("notes.json")
    }

    pub fn load() -> Vec<NoteItem> {
        let path = Self::storage_path();
        if path.exists() {
            if let Ok(raw) = fs::read_to_string(&path) {
                if let Ok(items) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<NoteItem>>(&raw) {
                    return items;
                }
            }
        }
        Vec::new()
    }

    pub fn save(&self) {
        let path = Self::storage_path();
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
            let _ = fs::create_dir_all(parent);
        }
        if let Ok(data) = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&self.notes) {
            let tmp = path.with_extension("tmp");
            if fs::write(&tmp, data).is_ok() {
                let _ = fs::rename(tmp, path);
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn update(&mut self, message: NotesMessage) {
        match message {
            NotesMessage::SearchOrNewChanged(val) => {
                self.input_text = val;
            }
            NotesMessage::AddNote => {
                let clean = self.input_text.trim();
                if !clean.is_empty() {
                    let max_id = self.notes.iter().map(|n| n.id).max().unwrap_or(0);
                    let ts = Local::now().format("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M").to_string();
                    self.notes.insert(
                        0,
                        NoteItem {
                            id: max_id + 1,
                            content: clean.to_string(),
                            ts,
                        },
                    );
                    self.input_text.clear();
                    self.save();
                }
            }
            NotesMessage::DeleteNote(id) => {
                self.notes.retain(|n| n.id != id);
                self.save();
            }
            NotesMessage::CopyNote(content) => {
                tokio::spawn(async move {
                    let _ = Command::new("wl-copy").arg(&content).status().await;
                    let _ = Command::new("notify-send")
                        .args(["-a", "Notes", "Note Copied", &content])
                        .spawn();
                });
            }
            NotesMessage::Dismiss => {}
        }
    }

    pub fn view<'a>(&'a self, theme: &ShellTheme) -> Element<'a, NotesMessage> {
        let input = text_input("Type note to save [Enter to add]...", &self.input_text)
            .on_input(NotesMessage::SearchOrNewChanged)
            .on_submit(NotesMessage::AddNote)
            .padding(14)
            .size(theme.font_size_overlay);

        let query = self.input_text.trim().to_lowercase();
        let mut list_col = column![].spacing(8);

        for note in self.notes.iter().filter(|n| {
            query.is_empty()
                || n.content.to_lowercase().contains(&query)
                || n.ts.contains(&query)
        }) {
            let id = note.id;
            let content_clone = note.content.clone();

            let row_item = row![
                text("📌").size(14),
                column![
                    text(&note.ts)
                        .size(theme.font_size_normal - 4)
                        .color(theme.warning),
                    text(&note.content)
                        .size(theme.font_size_normal)
                        .color(theme.text),
                ]
                .width(Length::Fill)
                .spacing(2),
                button(text("✕").size(12).color(theme.danger))
                    .on_press(NotesMessage::DeleteNote(id))
                    .padding(6)
                    .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                        background: None,
                        ..Default::default()
                    }),
            ]
            .spacing(12)
            .align_y(Alignment::Center);

            let note_card = button(container(row_item).padding(10))
                .on_press(NotesMessage::CopyNote(content_clone))
                .width(Length::Fill)
                .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                    background: None,
                    ..Default::default()
                });

            list_col = list_col.push(note_card);
        }

        let card_bg = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Rounded(12))
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.border, theme.border_width);

        let card_content = container(
            column![
                text("📝 QUICK NOTES")
                    .size(theme.font_size_overlay)
                    .color(theme.text),
                input,
                scrollable(list_col).height(Length::Fill),
                text("Click note to copy  •  ✕ to delete  •  ESC to dismiss")
                    .size(11)
                    .color(theme.muted)
                    .align_x(Alignment::Center),
            ]
            .spacing(14)
            .padding(20),
        )
        .width(Length::Fixed(840.0))
        .height(Length::Fixed(650.0));

        let card = Stack::new().push(card_bg.view()).push(card_content);

        let backdrop = button(container(column![]).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fill))
            .on_press(NotesMessage::Dismiss)
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgba8(0, 0, 0, 0.6))),
                ..Default::default()
            });

        Stack::new()
            .push(backdrop)
            .push(container(card).center_x(Length::Fill).center_y(Length::Fill))
            .into()
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/overlays/pass.rs ===
use iced::widget::{button, column, container, row, scrollable, text, text_input, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length};
use std::path::PathBuf;
use tokio::process::Command;
use walkdir::WalkDir;

use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum PassMessage {
    SearchChanged(String),
    CopyKey(String),
    Dismiss,
}

pub struct PassOverlay {
    pub search_query: String,
    pub all_keys: Vec<String>,
    pub filtered_keys: Vec<String>,
}

impl Default for PassOverlay {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl PassOverlay {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        let all_keys = Self::scan_passwords();
        Self {
            search_query: String::new(),
            filtered_keys: all_keys.clone(),
            all_keys,
        }
    }

    pub fn scan_passwords() -> Vec<String> {
        let store_dir = std::env::var("PASSWORD_STORE_DIR")
            .map(PathBuf::from)
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| {
                dirs::home_dir()
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/tmp"))
                    .join(".password-store")
            });

        let mut keys = Vec::new();
        if !store_dir.exists() {
            return keys;
        }

        for entry in WalkDir::new(&store_dir).into_iter().flatten() {
            let path = entry.path();
            if path.is_file() && path.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) == Some("gpg") {
                if let Ok(rel) = path.strip_prefix(&store_dir) {
                    let mut s = rel.to_string_lossy().to_string();
                    if s.ends_with(".gpg") {
                        s.truncate(s.len() - 4);
                        if !s.starts_with(".git") {
                            keys.push(s);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        keys.sort();
        keys
    }

    pub fn update(&mut self, message: PassMessage) {
        if let PassMessage::SearchChanged(q) = message {
            self.search_query = q.clone();
            let lower = q.trim().to_lowercase();
            if lower.is_empty() {
                self.filtered_keys = self.all_keys.clone();
            } else {
                self.filtered_keys = self
                    .all_keys
                    .iter()
                    .filter(|k| k.to_lowercase().contains(&lower))
                    .cloned()
                    .collect();
            }
        }
    }

    pub async fn copy_password(key: &str) {
        let clean = key.trim();
        if clean.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        let _ = Command::new("pass").args(["-c", clean]).status().await;
        let _ = Command::new("notify-send")
            .args(["-a", "Password Store", "Credential Copied", clean])
            .spawn();
    }

    pub fn view<'a>(&'a self, theme: &ShellTheme) -> Element<'a, PassMessage> {
        let input = text_input("Search password store... [Enter to copy]", &self.search_query)
            .on_input(PassMessage::SearchChanged)
            .on_submit(
                if let Some(first) = self.filtered_keys.first() {
                    PassMessage::CopyKey(first.clone())
                } else {
                    PassMessage::Dismiss
                },
            )
            .padding(14)
            .size(theme.font_size_overlay);

        let mut list_col = column![].spacing(6);
        for key in self.filtered_keys.iter().take(40) {
            let k_clone = key.clone();
            let row_item = button(
                row![
                    text("🔒").size(16),
                    text(key).size(theme.font_size_overlay).color(theme.text),
                ]
                .spacing(12)
                .align_y(Alignment::Center),
            )
            .on_press(PassMessage::CopyKey(k_clone))
            .padding([8, 14])
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: None,
                ..Default::default()
            });

            list_col = list_col.push(row_item);
        }

        let card_bg = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Rounded(12))
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.border, theme.border_width);

        let card_content = container(
            column![
                text("🔑 PASSWORD STORE")
                    .size(theme.font_size_overlay)
                    .color(theme.text),
                input,
                scrollable(list_col).height(Length::Fill),
                text("Click or Enter to copy credential  •  ESC to dismiss")
                    .size(11)
                    .color(theme.muted)
                    .align_x(Alignment::Center),
            ]
            .spacing(14)
            .padding(20),
        )
        .width(Length::Fixed(820.0))
        .height(Length::Fixed(600.0));

        let card = Stack::new().push(card_bg.view()).push(card_content);

        let backdrop = button(container(column![]).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fill))
            .on_press(PassMessage::Dismiss)
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgba8(0, 0, 0, 0.6))),
                ..Default::default()
            });

        Stack::new()
            .push(backdrop)
            .push(container(card).center_x(Length::Fill).center_y(Length::Fill))
            .into()
    }
}
