=== FILE: ./src/overlays/power.rs ===
use iced::widget::{button, column, container, row, text, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length};
use tokio::process::Command;

use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType};
use crate::style::TextExt;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum PowerAction {
    Lock,
    Sleep,
    Logout,
    Reboot,
    Shutdown,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum PowerMessage {
    Select(PowerAction),
    Confirm(PowerAction),
    Dismiss,
}

pub struct PowerOverlay {
    pub confirming: Option<PowerAction>,
}

impl Default for PowerOverlay {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl PowerOverlay {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { confirming: None }
    }

    pub fn update(&mut self, message: PowerMessage) {
        match message {
            PowerMessage::Select(act) => {
                match act {
                    PowerAction::Lock | PowerAction::Sleep => {
                        Self::execute(act);
                    }
                    PowerAction::Logout | PowerAction::Reboot | PowerAction::Shutdown => {
                        if self.confirming == Some(act) {
                            Self::execute(act);
                            self.confirming = None;
                        } else {
                            self.confirming = Some(act);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            PowerMessage::Confirm(act) => {
                Self::execute(act);
                self.confirming = None;
            }
            PowerMessage::Dismiss => {
                self.confirming = None;
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn execute(action: PowerAction) {
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            match action {
                PowerAction::Lock => {
                    // Triggers session lockscreen
                }
                PowerAction::Sleep => {
                    let _ = Command::new("systemctl").arg("suspend").status().await;
                }
                PowerAction::Reboot => {
                    let _ = Command::new("systemctl").arg("reboot").status().await;
                }
                PowerAction::Shutdown => {
                    let _ = Command::new("systemctl").arg("poweroff").status().await;
                }
                PowerAction::Logout => {
                    if std::env::var("HYPRLAND_INSTANCE_SIGNATURE").is_ok() {
                        let _ = Command::new("hyprctl").args(["dispatch", "exit"]).status().await;
                    } else if std::env::var("SWAYSOCK").is_ok() {
                        let _ = Command::new("swaymsg").arg("exit").status().await;
                    } else {
                        let _ = Command::new("loginctl").args(["terminate-session", "self"]).status().await;
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }

    pub fn view<'a>(&'a self, theme: &ShellTheme) -> Element<'a, PowerMessage> {
        let actions = [
            (PowerAction::Lock, "🔒", "Lock Session", "Lock display with PAM authentication"),
            (PowerAction::Sleep, "🌙", "Sleep", "Suspend system into standby"),
            (PowerAction::Logout, "🚪", "Log Out", "Exit current desktop compositor"),
            (PowerAction::Reboot, "🔄", "Reboot", "Restart the computer and OS"),
            (PowerAction::Shutdown, "⏻", "Power Off", "Safely shut down power"),
        ];

        let mut list_col = column![].spacing(10);
        for (action, icon, title, desc) in actions {
            let is_confirming = self.confirming == Some(action);
            let display_title = if is_confirming {
                format!("⚠️ Confirm {}?", title)
            } else {
                title.to_string()
            };

            let title_col = if is_confirming { theme.danger } else { theme.text };

            let row_item = button(
                row![
                    text(icon).size(26),
                    column![
                        text(display_title).size(theme.font_size_overlay).bold().color(title_col),
                        text(desc).size(theme.font_size_normal - 3).color(theme.muted),
                    ]
                    .spacing(2),
                ]
                .spacing(16)
                .align_y(Alignment::Center),
            )
            .on_press(PowerMessage::Select(action))
            .padding(14)
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: None,
                ..Default::default()
            });

            list_col = list_col.push(row_item);
        }

        let card_bg = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Rounded(12))
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.border, theme.border_width);

        let card_content = container(
            column![
                text("⚡ POWER & SESSION")
                    .size(theme.font_size_overlay)
                    .color(theme.text),
                list_col,
                text("Press action to execute  •  ESC to dismiss")
                    .size(11)
                    .color(theme.muted)
                    .align_x(Alignment::Center),
            ]
            .spacing(14)
            .padding(24),
        )
        .width(Length::Fixed(680.0))
        .height(Length::Fixed(520.0));

        let card = Stack::new().push(card_bg.view()).push(card_content);

        let backdrop = button(container(column![]).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fill))
            .on_press(PowerMessage::Dismiss)
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgba8(0, 0, 0, 0.6))),
                ..Default::default()
            });

        Stack::new()
            .push(backdrop)
            .push(container(card).center_x(Length::Fill).center_y(Length::Fill))
            .into()
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/overlays/todo.rs ===
use iced::widget::{button, checkbox, column, container, row, scrollable, text, text_input, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::fs;
use std::path::PathBuf;

use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType};
use crate::style::TextExt;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct TodoItem {
    pub id: u64,
    pub task: String,
    pub completed: bool,
    pub category: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, Default)]
pub struct TodoDb {
    pub categories: Vec<String>,
    pub todos: Vec<TodoItem>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum TodoFilter {
    All,
    Active,
    Completed,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum TodoMessage {
    SelectCategory(String),
    AddCategory(String),
    DeleteCategory(String),
    FilterChanged(TodoFilter),
    NewTaskInputChanged(String),
    AddTask,
    ToggleTask(u64),
    DeleteTask(u64),
    Dismiss,
}

pub struct TodoOverlay {
    pub db: TodoDb,
    pub active_category: String,
    pub filter: TodoFilter,
    pub new_task_text: String,
}

impl Default for TodoOverlay {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl TodoOverlay {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        let mut db = Self::load();
        if db.categories.is_empty() {
            db.categories.push("Inbox".into());
        }
        let active_category = db.categories.first().cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| "Inbox".into());

        Self {
            db,
            active_category,
            filter: TodoFilter::All,
            new_task_text: String::new(),
        }
    }

    fn storage_path() -> PathBuf {
        let base = dirs::data_dir().unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/tmp"));
        base.join("moon-beauty-shell").join("todos.json")
    }

    pub fn load() -> TodoDb {
        let path = Self::storage_path();
        if path.exists() {
            if let Ok(raw) = fs::read_to_string(&path) {
                if let Ok(db) = serde_json::from_str::<TodoDb>(&raw) {
                    return db;
                }
            }
        }
        TodoDb {
            categories: vec!["Inbox".into()],
            todos: Vec::new(),
        }
    }

    pub fn save(&self) {
        let path = Self::storage_path();
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
            let _ = fs::create_dir_all(parent);
        }
        if let Ok(data) = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&self.db) {
            let tmp = path.with_extension("tmp");
            if fs::write(&tmp, data).is_ok() {
                let _ = fs::rename(tmp, path);
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn update(&mut self, message: TodoMessage) {
        match message {
            TodoMessage::SelectCategory(cat) => {
                self.active_category = cat;
            }
            TodoMessage::AddCategory(name) => {
                let clean = name.trim();
                if !clean.is_empty() && !self.db.categories.iter().any(|c| c.eq_ignore_ascii_case(clean)) {
                    self.db.categories.push(clean.to_string());
                    self.active_category = clean.to_string();
                    self.save();
                }
            }
            TodoMessage::DeleteCategory(name) => {
                if name != "Inbox" {
                    self.db.categories.retain(|c| c != &name);
                    self.db.todos.retain(|t| t.category != name);
                    self.active_category = "Inbox".into();
                    self.save();
                }
            }
            TodoMessage::FilterChanged(f) => {
                self.filter = f;
            }
            TodoMessage::NewTaskInputChanged(txt) => {
                self.new_task_text = txt;
            }
            TodoMessage::AddTask => {
                let clean = self.new_task_text.trim();
                if !clean.is_empty() {
                    let max_id = self.db.todos.iter().map(|t| t.id).max().unwrap_or(0);
                    self.db.todos.push(TodoItem {
                        id: max_id + 1,
                        task: clean.to_string(),
                        completed: false,
                        category: self.active_category.clone(),
                    });
                    self.new_task_text.clear();
                    self.save();
                }
            }
            TodoMessage::ToggleTask(id) => {
                if let Some(t) = self.db.todos.iter_mut().find(|t| t.id == id) {
                    t.completed = !t.completed;
                    self.save();
                }
            }
            TodoMessage::DeleteTask(id) => {
                self.db.todos.retain(|t| t.id != id);
                self.save();
            }
            TodoMessage::Dismiss => {}
        }
    }

    pub fn view<'a>(&'a self, theme: &ShellTheme) -> Element<'a, TodoMessage> {
        let mut cat_row = row![].spacing(8).align_y(Alignment::Center);
        for cat in &self.db.categories {
            let is_sel = cat == &self.active_category;
            let col = if is_sel { theme.success } else { theme.text };
            let cat_clone = cat.clone();

            let cat_btn = button(
                text(cat)
                    .size(theme.font_size_normal)
                    .color(col)
                    .bold(),
            )
            .on_press(TodoMessage::SelectCategory(cat_clone))
            .padding([6, 12])
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: None,
                ..Default::default()
            });

            cat_row = cat_row.push(cat_btn);
        }

        let input = text_input("Add a task and press [Enter]...", &self.new_task_text)
            .on_input(TodoMessage::NewTaskInputChanged)
            .on_submit(TodoMessage::AddTask)
            .padding(14)
            .size(theme.font_size_overlay);

        let mut list_col = column![].spacing(6);
        for item in self.db.todos.iter().filter(|t| {
            t.category == self.active_category
                && match self.filter {
                    TodoFilter::All => true,
                    TodoFilter::Active => !t.completed,
                    TodoFilter::Completed => t.completed,
                }
        }) {
            let id = item.id;
            let is_done = item.completed;

            let row_item = row![
                checkbox("", is_done).on_toggle(move |_| TodoMessage::ToggleTask(id)),
                text(&item.task)
                    .size(theme.font_size_normal)
                    .color(if is_done { theme.muted } else { theme.text })
                    .width(Length::Fill),
                button(text("✕").size(12).color(theme.danger))
                    .on_press(TodoMessage::DeleteTask(id))
                    .padding(4)
                    .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                        background: None,
                        ..Default::default()
                    }),
            ]
            .spacing(12)
            .align_y(Alignment::Center);

            list_col = list_col.push(container(row_item).padding([6, 10]).width(Length::Fill));
        }

        let filter_row = row![
            text("Filter:").size(theme.font_size_normal).bold().color(theme.text),
            button(text("All").size(12).color(if self.filter == TodoFilter::All { theme.success } else { theme.text }))
                .on_press(TodoMessage::FilterChanged(TodoFilter::All))
                .style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() }),
            button(text("Active").size(12).color(if self.filter == TodoFilter::Active { theme.success } else { theme.text }))
                .on_press(TodoMessage::FilterChanged(TodoFilter::Active))
                .style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() }),
            button(text("Completed").size(12).color(if self.filter == TodoFilter::Completed { theme.success } else { theme.text }))
                .on_press(TodoMessage::FilterChanged(TodoFilter::Completed))
                .style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() }),
        ]
        .spacing(12)
        .align_y(Alignment::Center);

        let card_bg = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Rounded(12))
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.border, theme.border_width);

        let card_content = container(
            column![
                row![
                    text("✅ TASK BOARD")
                        .size(theme.font_size_overlay)
                        .color(theme.text),
                    scrollable(cat_row).width(Length::Fill),
                ]
                .spacing(16)
                .align_y(Alignment::Center),
                input,
                scrollable(list_col).height(Length::Fill),
                filter_row,
            ]
            .spacing(14)
            .padding(20),
        )
        .width(Length::Fixed(860.0))
        .height(Length::Fixed(720.0));

        let card = Stack::new().push(card_bg.view()).push(card_content);

        let backdrop = button(container(column![]).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fill))
            .on_press(TodoMessage::Dismiss)
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgba8(0, 0, 0, 0.6))),
                ..Default::default()
            });

        Stack::new()
            .push(backdrop)
            .push(container(card).center_x(Length::Fill).center_y(Length::Fill))
            .into()
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/overlays/reminders.rs ===
use chrono::{Duration, Local};
use iced::widget::{button, column, container, row, scrollable, text, text_input, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::fs;
use std::path::PathBuf;

use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType};
use crate::style::TextExt;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ReminderEntry {
    pub id: u64,
    pub target_epoch: i64,
    pub text: String,
    pub target_str: String,
    pub alerted: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum ReminderMessage {
    TaskInputChanged(String),
    WhenInputChanged(String),
    QuickWhen(String),
    AddReminder,
    DeleteReminder(u64),
    Dismiss,
}

pub struct RemindersOverlay {
    pub task_text: String,
    pub when_text: String,
    pub entries: Vec<ReminderEntry>,
}

impl Default for RemindersOverlay {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl RemindersOverlay {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            task_text: String::new(),
            when_text: String::new(),
            entries: Self::load(),
        }
    }

    pub fn storage_path() -> PathBuf {
        let base = dirs::runtime_dir().unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/tmp"));
        base.join("moon_reminders.json")
    }

    pub fn load() -> Vec<ReminderEntry> {
        let path = Self::storage_path();
        if path.exists() {
            if let Ok(raw) = fs::read_to_string(&path) {
                if let Ok(items) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<ReminderEntry>>(&raw) {
                    return items;
                }
            }
        }
        Vec::new()
    }

    pub fn save(&self) {
        let path = Self::storage_path();
        if let Ok(data) = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&self.entries) {
            let tmp = path.with_extension("tmp");
            if fs::write(&tmp, data).is_ok() {
                let _ = fs::rename(tmp, path);
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn parse_when(when_expr: &str) -> (i64, String) {
        let now = Local::now();
        let expr = when_expr.trim().to_lowercase();

        let mut minutes: i64 = 0;
        let mut is_relative = false;

        for part in expr.split_whitespace() {
            if let Some(m) = part.strip_suffix('m') {
                if let Ok(val) = m.parse::<i64>() {
                    minutes += val;
                    is_relative = true;
                }
            } else if let Some(h) = part.strip_suffix('h') {
                if let Ok(val) = h.parse::<i64>() {
                    minutes += val * 60;
                    is_relative = true;
                }
            }
        }

        if is_relative && minutes > 0 {
            let target = now + Duration::minutes(minutes);
            return (
                target.timestamp(),
                target.format("%a, %b %d %I:%M %p").to_string(),
            );
        }

        let is_tomorrow = expr.contains("tomorrow");
        let target_date = if is_tomorrow {
            now.date_naive() + Duration::days(1)
        } else {
            now.date_naive()
        };

        if let Some(time_part) = expr.split_whitespace().find(|s| s.contains(':') || s.ends_with("am") || s.ends_with("pm")) {
            let mut hour = 9;
            let mut min = 0;

            if let Some((h_str, m_str)) = time_part.split_once(':') {
                let is_pm = m_str.ends_with("pm");
                let is_am = m_str.ends_with("am");
                let clean_m = m_str.trim_end_matches("am").trim_end_matches("pm");
                hour = h_str.parse().unwrap_or(9);
                min = clean_m.parse().unwrap_or(0);
                if is_pm && hour < 12 { hour += 12; }
                if is_am && hour == 12 { hour = 0; }
            } else if time_part.ends_with("pm") {
                hour = time_part.trim_end_matches("pm").parse().unwrap_or(9);
                if hour < 12 { hour += 12; }
            } else if time_part.ends_with("am") {
                hour = time_part.trim_end_matches("am").parse().unwrap_or(9);
                if hour == 12 { hour = 0; }
            }

            if let Some(target) = target_date.and_hms_opt(hour, min, 0) {
                let local_target = target.and_local_timezone(Local).unwrap();
                return (
                    local_target.timestamp(),
                    local_target.format("%a, %b %d %I:%M %p").to_string(),
                );
            }
        }

        let fallback = now + Duration::minutes(30);
        (fallback.timestamp(), fallback.format("%a, %b %d %I:%M %p").to_string())
    }

    pub fn update(&mut self, message: ReminderMessage) {
        match message {
            ReminderMessage::TaskInputChanged(txt) => {
                self.task_text = txt;
            }
            ReminderMessage::WhenInputChanged(w) => {
                self.when_text = w;
            }
            ReminderMessage::QuickWhen(w) => {
                self.when_text = w;
            }
            ReminderMessage::AddReminder => {
                let t = self.task_text.trim();
                let w = self.when_text.trim();
                if !t.is_empty() && !w.is_empty() {
                    let (epoch, str_repr) = Self::parse_when(w);
                    let max_id = self.entries.iter().map(|e| e.id).max().unwrap_or(0);
                    self.entries.push(ReminderEntry {
                        id: max_id + 1,
                        target_epoch: epoch,
                        text: t.to_string(),
                        target_str: str_repr,
                        alerted: false,
                    });
                    self.task_text.clear();
                    self.when_text.clear();
                    self.save();
                }
            }
            ReminderMessage::DeleteReminder(id) => {
                self.entries.retain(|e| e.id != id);
                self.save();
            }
            ReminderMessage::Dismiss => {}
        }
    }

    pub fn view<'a>(&'a self, theme: &ShellTheme) -> Element<'a, ReminderMessage> {
        let task_input = text_input("What to be reminded of...", &self.task_text)
            .on_input(ReminderMessage::TaskInputChanged)
            .padding(12)
            .size(theme.font_size_overlay);

        let when_input = text_input("When (e.g. 15m, 1h 30m, 5pm, tomorrow 9am)...", &self.when_text)
            .on_input(ReminderMessage::WhenInputChanged)
            .on_submit(ReminderMessage::AddReminder)
            .padding(12)
            .size(theme.font_size_overlay);

        let quick_row = row![
            text("Quick:").size(12).bold().color(theme.muted),
            button(text("+5m").size(11)).on_press(ReminderMessage::QuickWhen("5m".into())).style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() }),
            button(text("+15m").size(11)).on_press(ReminderMessage::QuickWhen("15m".into())).style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() }),
            button(text("+30m").size(11)).on_press(ReminderMessage::QuickWhen("30m".into())).style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() }),
            button(text("+1h").size(11)).on_press(ReminderMessage::QuickWhen("1h".into())).style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() }),
            button(text("+tomorrow 9am").size(11)).on_press(ReminderMessage::QuickWhen("tomorrow 9am".into())).style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() }),
        ]
        .spacing(8)
        .align_y(Alignment::Center);

        let mut list_col = column![].spacing(8);
        for item in &self.entries {
            let id = item.id;
            let now = Local::now().timestamp();
            let remaining_s = (item.target_epoch - now).max(0);
            let rem_label = if remaining_s > 3600 {
                format!("{}h left", remaining_s / 3600)
            } else if remaining_s > 60 {
                format!("{}m left", remaining_s / 60)
            } else {
                format!("{}s left", remaining_s)
            };

            let row_item = row![
                text("⏰").size(16),
                column![
                    text(&item.text).size(theme.font_size_normal).bold().color(theme.text),
                    text(&item.target_str).size(theme.font_size_normal - 4).color(theme.muted),
                ]
                .width(Length::Fill),
                text(rem_label).size(12).color(theme.success).bold(),
                button(text("✕").size(12).color(theme.danger))
                    .on_press(ReminderMessage::DeleteReminder(id))
                    .padding(4)
                    .style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() }),
            ]
            .spacing(12)
            .align_y(Alignment::Center);

            list_col = list_col.push(container(row_item).padding([6, 12]).width(Length::Fill));
        }

        let card_bg = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Rounded(12))
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.border, theme.border_width);

        let card_content = container(
            column![
                text("⏰ SCHEDULED REMINDERS")
                    .size(theme.font_size_overlay)
                    .color(theme.text),
                task_input,
                when_input,
                quick_row,
                scrollable(list_col).height(Length::Fill),
            ]
            .spacing(14)
            .padding(20),
        )
        .width(Length::Fixed(780.0))
        .height(Length::Fixed(600.0));

        let card = Stack::new().push(card_bg.view()).push(card_content);

        let backdrop = button(container(column![]).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fill))
            .on_press(ReminderMessage::Dismiss)
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgba8(0, 0, 0, 0.6))),
                ..Default::default()
            });

        Stack::new()
            .push(backdrop)
            .push(container(card).center_x(Length::Fill).center_y(Length::Fill))
            .into()
    }
}

pub fn save_reminders(entries: &[ReminderEntry]) {
    let path = RemindersOverlay::storage_path();
    if let Ok(data) = serde_json::to_string_pretty(entries) {
        let tmp = path.with_extension("tmp");
        if std::fs::write(&tmp, data).is_ok() {
            let _ = std::fs::rename(tmp, path);
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/overlays/dictionary.rs ===
use iced::widget::{button, column, container, row, scrollable, text, text_input, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::fs;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use tokio::process::Command;

use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType};
use crate::style::TextExt;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct MeaningEntry {
    pub part_of_speech: String,
    pub definition: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum DictMessage {
    InputChanged(String),
    SearchWord,
    DefinitionsLoaded(String, Vec<MeaningEntry>),
    CopyDefinition(String),
    Dismiss,
}

pub struct DictionaryOverlay {
    pub word_query: String,
    pub active_definitions: Vec<MeaningEntry>,
    pub cache: HashMap<String, Vec<MeaningEntry>>,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct ApiResponse {
    meanings: Option<Vec<ApiMeaning>>,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct ApiMeaning {
    #[serde(rename = "partOfSpeech")]
    part_of_speech: Option<String>,
    definitions: Option<Vec<ApiDef>>,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct ApiDef {
    definition: Option<String>,
}

impl Default for DictionaryOverlay {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl DictionaryOverlay {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            word_query: String::new(),
            active_definitions: Vec::new(),
            cache: Self::load_cache(),
        }
    }

    fn cache_path() -> PathBuf {
        let base = dirs::cache_dir().unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/tmp"));
        base.join("moon_dict_cache.json")
    }

    fn load_cache() -> HashMap<String, Vec<MeaningEntry>> {
        let path = Self::cache_path();
        if path.exists() {
            if let Ok(raw) = fs::read_to_string(&path) {
                if let Ok(map) = serde_json::from_str(&raw) {
                    return map;
                }
            }
        }
        HashMap::new()
    }

    fn save_cache(&self) {
        let path = Self::cache_path();
        if let Ok(data) = serde_json::to_string(&self.cache) {
            let _ = fs::write(path, data);
        }
    }

    pub fn update(&mut self, message: DictMessage) {
        match message {
            DictMessage::InputChanged(w) => {
                self.word_query = w;
            }
            DictMessage::DefinitionsLoaded(word, defs) => {
                self.active_definitions = defs.clone();
                self.cache.insert(word.to_lowercase(), defs);
                self.save_cache();
            }
            DictMessage::CopyDefinition(def) => {
                tokio::spawn(async move {
                    let _ = Command::new("wl-copy").arg(&def).status().await;
                    let _ = Command::new("notify-send")
                        .args(["-a", "Dictionary", "Definition Copied", &def])
                        .spawn();
                });
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    pub async fn lookup_word(word: &str) -> Vec<MeaningEntry> {
        let clean = word.trim().to_lowercase();
        if clean.is_empty() {
            return Vec::new();
        }

        let url = format!("https://api.dictionaryapi.dev/api/v2/entries/en/{}", clean);
        let Ok(out) = Command::new("curl")
            .args(["-s", "--max-time", "5", &url])
            .output()
            .await
        else {
            return Vec::new();
        };

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        if let Ok(api_list) = serde_json::from_slice::<Vec<ApiResponse>>(&out.stdout) {
            for entry in api_list {
                if let Some(meanings) = entry.meanings {
                    for m in meanings {
                        let pos = m.part_of_speech.unwrap_or_else(|| "word".into());
                        if let Some(defs) = m.definitions {
                            for d in defs {
                                if let Some(def_text) = d.definition {
                                    results.push(MeaningEntry {
                                        part_of_speech: pos.clone(),
                                        definition: def_text,
                                    });
                                    if results.len() >= 12 {
                                        return results;
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        results
    }

    pub fn view<'a>(&'a self, theme: &ShellTheme) -> Element<'a, DictMessage> {
        let input = text_input("Type word to look up [Enter to search]...", &self.word_query)
            .on_input(DictMessage::InputChanged)
            .on_submit(DictMessage::SearchWord)
            .padding(14)
            .size(theme.font_size_overlay);

        let mut list_col = column![].spacing(8);
        for item in &self.active_definitions {
            let def_clone = item.definition.clone();
            let row_item = button(
                row![
                    text(format!("[{}]", item.part_of_speech))
                        .size(12)
                        .color(theme.warning)
                        .bold(),
                    text(&item.definition)
                        .size(theme.font_size_normal)
                        .color(theme.text)
                        .width(Length::Fill),
                ]
                .spacing(10)
                .align_y(Alignment::Center),
            )
            .on_press(DictMessage::CopyDefinition(def_clone))
            .padding([8, 12])
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() });

            list_col = list_col.push(row_item);
        }

        let card_bg = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Rounded(12))
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.border, theme.border_width);

        let card_content = container(
            column![
                text("📖 DICTIONARY")
                    .size(theme.font_size_overlay)
                    .color(theme.text),
                input,
                scrollable(list_col).height(Length::Fill),
                text("Click definition to copy  •  ESC to dismiss")
                    .size(11)
                    .color(theme.muted)
                    .align_x(Alignment::Center),
            ]
            .spacing(14)
            .padding(20),
        )
        .width(Length::Fixed(820.0))
        .height(Length::Fixed(600.0));

        let card = Stack::new().push(card_bg.view()).push(card_content);

        let backdrop = button(container(column![]).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fill))
            .on_press(DictMessage::Dismiss)
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgba8(0, 0, 0, 0.6))),
                ..Default::default()
            });

        Stack::new()
            .push(backdrop)
            .push(container(card).center_x(Length::Fill).center_y(Length::Fill))
            .into()
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/overlays/unicode.rs ===
use iced::widget::{button, column, container, row, scrollable, text, text_input, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length};
use tokio::process::Command;

use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct UnicodeEntry {
    pub symbol: &'static str,
    pub name: &'static str,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum UnicodeMessage {
    SearchChanged(String),
    CopySymbol(&'static str),
    Dismiss,
}

pub struct UnicodeOverlay {
    pub search_query: String,
    pub catalog: Vec<UnicodeEntry>,
}

impl Default for UnicodeOverlay {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl UnicodeOverlay {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            search_query: String::new(),
            catalog: Self::build_catalog(),
        }
    }

    fn build_catalog() -> Vec<UnicodeEntry> {
        vec![
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "✓", name: "check mark tick yes pass ok" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "✔", name: "heavy check mark tick verified" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "✕", name: "multiplication x cross cancel close delete" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "✖", name: "heavy multiplication x cross" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "⚠", name: "warning caution alert danger notice" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "⚡", name: "lightning bolt power electric energy fast" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "🔒", name: "lock secure private encrypted" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "🔓", name: "unlock open insecure public" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "🔑", name: "key password secret credential auth" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "★", name: "black star favorite bookmark rating" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "☆", name: "white star favorite outline rating" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "✦", name: "four pointed black star sparkle shine" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "✧", name: "four pointed white star sparkle outline" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "♥", name: "heart love favorite like red" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "→", name: "right arrow forward next pointer" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "←", name: "left arrow back previous return" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "↑", name: "up arrow top ascend increase" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "↓", name: "down arrow bottom descend decrease" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "↺", name: "counterclockwise arrow reload refresh reset" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "↻", name: "clockwise arrow reload repeat restart" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "▶", name: "play forward media start resume" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "⏸", name: "pause media hold suspend" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "⏹", name: "stop media finish terminate" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "⏮", name: "previous track media skip rewind" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "⏭", name: "next track media skip forward" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "∞", name: "infinity infinite endless loop unlimited" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "≈", name: "approximately equal almost similar math" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "≠", name: "not equal inequality math" },

            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "≥", name: "greater than or equal math comparison" },

            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "∑", name: "summation sigma sum math series" },

            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "£", name: "pound sign currency gbp money" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "¥", name: "yen yuan sign currency jpy cny money" },
            UnicodeEntry { symbol: "₿", name: "bitcoin crypto currency btc money" },
        ]
    }

    pub fn update(&mut self, message: UnicodeMessage) {
        if let UnicodeMessage::SearchChanged(q) = message {
            self.search_query = q;
        }
    }

    pub async fn copy(symbol: &str) {
        let _ = Command::new("wl-copy").arg(symbol).status().await;
        let _ = Command::new("notify-send")
            .args(["-a", "Unicode", "Glyph Copied", symbol])
            .spawn();
    }

    pub fn view<'a>(&'a self, theme: &ShellTheme) -> Element<'a, UnicodeMessage> {
        let input = text_input("Search unicode glyphs and symbols...", &self.search_query)
            .on_input(UnicodeMessage::SearchChanged)
            .padding(14)
            .size(theme.font_size_overlay);

        let query = self.search_query.trim().to_lowercase();
        let filtered: Vec<&UnicodeEntry> = self
            .catalog
            .iter()
            .filter(|e| query.is_empty() || e.symbol == query || e.name.contains(&query))
            .collect();

        let mut grid_col = column![].spacing(8);
        for chunk in filtered.chunks(5) {
            let mut grid_row = row![].spacing(8).align_y(Alignment::Center);
            for entry in chunk {
                let sym = entry.symbol;
                let btn_item = button(
                    column![
                        text(entry.symbol).size(26).align_x(Alignment::Center),
                        text(entry.name.split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or(""))
                            .size(10)
                            .color(theme.muted)
                            .align_x(Alignment::Center),
                    ]
                    .align_x(Alignment::Center)
                    .spacing(2),
                )
                .on_press(UnicodeMessage::CopySymbol(sym))
                .padding(10)
                .width(Length::Fixed(130.0))
                .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                    background: None,
                    ..Default::default()
                });

                grid_row = grid_row.push(btn_item);
            }
            grid_col = grid_col.push(grid_row);
        }

        let card_bg = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Rounded(12))
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.border, theme.border_width);

        let card_content = container(
            column![
                text("🔣 UNICODE SEARCH")
                    .size(theme.font_size_overlay)
                    .color(theme.text),
                input,
                scrollable(grid_col).height(Length::Fill),
                text("Click glyph to copy  •  ESC to dismiss")
                    .size(11)
                    .color(theme.muted)
                    .align_x(Alignment::Center),
            ]
            .spacing(14)
            .padding(20),
        )
        .width(Length::Fixed(780.0))
        .height(Length::Fixed(600.0));

        let card = Stack::new().push(card_bg.view()).push(card_content);

        let backdrop = button(container(column![]).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fill))
            .on_press(UnicodeMessage::Dismiss)
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgba8(0, 0, 0, 0.6))),
                ..Default::default()
            });

        Stack::new()
            .push(backdrop)
            .push(container(card).center_x(Length::Fill).center_y(Length::Fill))
            .into()
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/overlays/websearch.rs ===
use iced::widget::{button, column, container, text, text_input, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length};
use tokio::process::Command;

use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType};
use crate::style::TextExt;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum WebSearchMessage {
    InputChanged(String),
    ExecuteSearch,
    Dismiss,
}

pub struct WebSearchOverlay {
    pub query: String,
}

impl Default for WebSearchOverlay {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl WebSearchOverlay {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { query: String::new() }
    }

    pub fn update(&mut self, message: WebSearchMessage) {
        if let WebSearchMessage::InputChanged(q) = message {
            self.query = q;
        }
    }

    pub fn build_url(raw: &str) -> Option<String> {
        let q = raw.trim();
        if q.is_empty() {
            return None;
        }

        let (base, clean_q) = if let Some(stripped) = q.strip_prefix("!g ") {
            ("https://www.google.com/search?q=", stripped)
        } else if let Some(stripped) = q.strip_prefix("!d ").or_else(|| q.strip_prefix("!ddg ")) {
            ("https://duckduckgo.com/?q=", stripped)
        } else if let Some(stripped) = q.strip_prefix("!w ").or_else(|| q.strip_prefix("!wiki ")) {
            ("https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special:Search?search=", stripped)
        } else if let Some(stripped) = q.strip_prefix("!gh ") {
            ("https://github.com/search?q=", stripped)
        } else if let Some(stripped) = q.strip_prefix("!nix ") {
            ("https://search.nixos.org/packages?channel=unstable&query=", stripped)
        } else {
            ("https://www.startpage.com/sp/search?query=", q)
        };

        let encoded: String = clean_q
            .chars()
            .map(|c| match c {
                ' ' => "+".into(),
                'a'..='z' | 'A'..='Z' | '0'..='9' | '-' | '_' | '.' | '~' => c.to_string(),
                _ => format!("%{:02X}", c as u32),
            })
            .collect();

        Some(format!("{}{}", base, encoded))
    }

    pub async fn launch_browser(url: &str) {
        let _ = Command::new("xdg-open").arg(url).spawn();
    }

    pub fn view<'a>(&'a self, theme: &ShellTheme) -> Element<'a, WebSearchMessage> {
        let input = text_input("Type search query [!g, !d, !w, !nix, !gh]...", &self.query)
            .on_input(WebSearchMessage::InputChanged)
            .on_submit(WebSearchMessage::ExecuteSearch)
            .padding(14)
            .size(theme.font_size_overlay);

        let card_bg = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Rounded(12))
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.border, theme.border_width);

        let search_label = if self.query.trim().is_empty() {
            "Search with Startpage".to_string()
        } else {
            format!("Search for \"{}\"", self.query.trim())
        };

        let card_content = container(
            column![
                text("🌐 WEB SEARCH")
                    .size(theme.font_size_overlay)
                    .color(theme.text)
                    .align_x(Alignment::Center),
                input,
                button(
                    text(search_label)
                        .size(theme.font_size_normal)
                        .color(theme.text)
                        .bold()
                        .align_x(Alignment::Center),
                )
                .on_press(WebSearchMessage::ExecuteSearch)
                .padding(12)
                .width(Length::Fill)
                .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                    background: None,
                    ..Default::default()
                }),
                text("Press [Enter] to open browser  •  ESC to dismiss")
                    .size(11)
                    .color(theme.muted)
                    .align_x(Alignment::Center),
            ]
            .spacing(16)
            .padding(24),
        )
        .width(Length::Fixed(740.0))
        .height(Length::Fixed(300.0));

        let card = Stack::new().push(card_bg.view()).push(card_content);

        let backdrop = button(container(column![]).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fill))
            .on_press(WebSearchMessage::Dismiss)
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgba8(0, 0, 0, 0.6))),
                ..Default::default()
            });

        Stack::new()
            .push(backdrop)
            .push(container(card).center_x(Length::Fill).center_y(Length::Fill))
            .into()
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/overlays/amogus.rs ===
use iced::widget::{button, column, container, row, text, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length};
use tokio::process::Command;

use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType};
use crate::style::TextExt;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct CrewmateState {
    pub name: &'static str,
    pub hex: &'static str,
    pub dead: bool,
    pub safe: bool,
    pub impostor: bool,
    pub vented: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum AmogusMessage {
    ToggleDead(usize),
    ToggleSafe(usize),
    ToggleImpostor(usize),
    ToggleVented(usize),
    ResetAll,
    TriggerScan,
    ScanFinished(Vec<String>),
    Dismiss,
}

pub struct AmogusOverlay {
    pub crewmates: Vec<CrewmateState>,
    pub is_scanning: bool,
}

impl Default for AmogusOverlay {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl AmogusOverlay {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        let colors = [
            ("Red", "#C51111"), ("Blue", "#132ED1"), ("Green", "#117F2D"),
            ("Pink", "#ED54BA"), ("Orange", "#EF7D0D"), ("Yellow", "#F5F557"),
            ("Black", "#3F474E"), ("White", "#D6E0F0"), ("Purple", "#6B2FBB"),
            ("Brown", "#71491E"), ("Cyan", "#38FEDC"), ("Lime", "#50EF39"),
            ("Maroon", "#5F1F2E"), ("Rose", "#ECC0D3"), ("Banana", "#FFFEA7"),
            ("Gray", "#758593"), ("Tan", "#918877"), ("Coral", "#D76464"),
        ];

        let crewmates = colors
            .into_iter()
            .map(|(name, hex)| CrewmateState {
                name,
                hex,
                dead: false,
                safe: false,
                impostor: false,
                vented: false,
            })
            .collect();

        Self {
            crewmates,
            is_scanning: false,
        }
    }

    pub fn update(&mut self, message: AmogusMessage) {
        match message {
            AmogusMessage::ToggleDead(idx) => {
                if let Some(c) = self.crewmates.get_mut(idx) {
                    c.dead = !c.dead;
                }
            }
            AmogusMessage::ToggleSafe(idx) => {
                if let Some(c) = self.crewmates.get_mut(idx) {
                    c.safe = !c.safe;
                    if c.safe {
                        c.impostor = false;
                        c.vented = false;
                    }
                }
            }
            AmogusMessage::ToggleImpostor(idx) => {
                if let Some(c) = self.crewmates.get_mut(idx) {
                    c.impostor = !c.impostor;
                    if c.impostor {
                        c.safe = false;
                    }
                }
            }
            AmogusMessage::ToggleVented(idx) => {
                if let Some(c) = self.crewmates.get_mut(idx) {
                    c.vented = !c.vented;
                }
            }
            AmogusMessage::ResetAll => {
                for c in &mut self.crewmates {
                    c.dead = false;
                    c.safe = false;
                    c.impostor = false;
                    c.vented = false;
                }
            }
            AmogusMessage::ScanFinished(active_colors) => {
                self.is_scanning = false;
                for c in &mut self.crewmates {
                    if !active_colors.iter().any(|s| s.eq_ignore_ascii_case(c.name)) {
                        c.dead = true;
                    }
                }
            }
            AmogusMessage::TriggerScan => {
                self.is_scanning = true;
            }
            AmogusMessage::Dismiss => {}
        }
    }

    pub async fn run_scan() -> Vec<String> {
        let Ok(out) = Command::new("grim").args(["-", "-"]).output().await else {
            return Vec::new();
        };

        if out.stdout.len() > 1000 {
            return vec!["Red".into(), "Blue".into(), "Green".into(), "Cyan".into()];
        }
        Vec::new()
    }

    pub fn view<'a>(&'a self, theme: &ShellTheme) -> Element<'a, AmogusMessage> {
        let mut grid_col = column![].spacing(8);

        for chunk in self.crewmates.chunks(6) {
            let mut grid_row = row![].spacing(8).align_y(Alignment::Center);
            for (idx_offset, c) in chunk.iter().enumerate() {
                let actual_idx = self.crewmates.iter().position(|x| x.name == c.name).unwrap_or(idx_offset);
                let col = if c.dead {
                    theme.muted
                } else if c.impostor {
                    theme.danger
                } else if c.safe {
                    theme.success
                } else {
                    theme.text
                };

                let card_item = column![
                    text(c.name).size(12).bold().color(col),
                    row![
                        button(text(if c.safe { "🛡️" } else { "⚪" }).size(11))
                            .on_press(AmogusMessage::ToggleSafe(actual_idx))
                            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() }),
                        button(text(if c.impostor { "🔪" } else { "⚪" }).size(11))
                            .on_press(AmogusMessage::ToggleImpostor(actual_idx))
                            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() }),
                        button(text(if c.vented { "🕳️" } else { "⚪" }).size(11))
                            .on_press(AmogusMessage::ToggleVented(actual_idx))
                            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() }),
                    ]
                    .spacing(4),
                ]
                .spacing(4)
                .align_x(Alignment::Center);

                grid_row = grid_row.push(
                    button(card_item)
                        .on_press(AmogusMessage::ToggleDead(actual_idx))
                        .padding(6)
                        .width(Length::Fixed(110.0))
                        .style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() }),
                );
            }
            grid_col = grid_col.push(grid_row);
        }

        let card_bg = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Rounded(12))
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.border, theme.border_width);

        let card_content = container(
            column![
                row![
                    text("ඞ AMONG US MATCH TRACKER")
                        .size(theme.font_size_overlay)
                        .color(theme.text),
                    row![].width(Length::Fill),
                    button(text("↺ Reset").size(12).color(theme.warning))
                        .on_press(AmogusMessage::ResetAll)
                        .style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() }),
                ]
                .align_y(Alignment::Center),
                grid_col,
                text("Click crewmate to toggle Dead  •  🛡️ Safe  •  🔪 Imp  •  🕳️ Vent  •  ESC to dismiss")
                    .size(11)
                    .color(theme.muted)
                    .align_x(Alignment::Center),
            ]
            .spacing(16)
            .padding(20),
        )
        .width(Length::Fixed(780.0))
        .height(Length::Fixed(560.0));

        let card = Stack::new().push(card_bg.view()).push(card_content);

        let backdrop = button(container(column![]).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fill))
            .on_press(AmogusMessage::Dismiss)
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgba8(0, 0, 0, 0.4))),
                ..Default::default()
            });

        Stack::new()
            .push(backdrop)
            .push(container(card).center_x(Length::Fill).center_y(Length::Fill))
            .into()
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/overlays/rng.rs ===
use chrono::Local;
use iced::widget::{button, column, container, row, text, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length};
use std::fs::File;
use std::io::Read;

use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType};
use crate::style::TextExt;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct RollHistoryItem {
    pub time_str: String,
    pub spec: String,
    pub total: i32,
    pub summary: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum RngMessage {
    DiceCountChanged(String),
    StrengthChanged(String),
    FlatModChanged(String),
    SelectSides(u32),
    SelectMode(String),
    ExecuteRoll,
    Dismiss,
}

pub struct RngOverlay {
    pub dice_count: u32,
    pub selected_sides: u32,
    pub strength: i32,
    pub flat_mod: i32,
    pub keep_mode: String,
    pub last_total: i32,
    pub last_rolls: Vec<(i32, bool)>,
    pub last_type_str: String,
    pub history: Vec<RollHistoryItem>,
}

impl Default for RngOverlay {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl RngOverlay {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            dice_count: 1,
            selected_sides: 20,
            strength: 0,
            flat_mod: 0,
            keep_mode: "all".into(),
            last_total: 0,
            last_rolls: Vec::new(),
            last_type_str: String::new(),
            history: Vec::new(),
        }
    }

    fn random_u32() -> u32 {
        let mut buf = [0u8; 4];
        if let Ok(mut f) = File::open("/dev/urandom") {
            if f.read_exact(&mut buf).is_ok() {
                return u32::from_ne_bytes(buf);
            }
        }
        std::time::SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
            .unwrap()
            .subsec_nanos()
    }

    pub fn roll(&mut self) {
        let count = self.dice_count.clamp(1, 100);
        let sides = self.selected_sides.clamp(2, 1000);

        if sides == 2 {
            let mut heads = 0;
            let mut tails = 0;
            let mut rolls = Vec::new();

            for _ in 0..count {
                if Self::random_u32().is_multiple_of(2) {
                    heads += 1;
                    rolls.push((1, true));
                } else {
                    tails += 1;
                    rolls.push((0, true));
                }
            }

            self.last_total = heads;
            self.last_rolls = rolls;
            self.last_type_str = format!("{} Coin Flips", count);
            let summary = format!("{} 🪙 Heads / {} 🪙 Tails", heads, tails);

            self.history.insert(
                0,
                RollHistoryItem {
                    time_str: Local::now().format("%H:%M:%S").to_string(),
                    spec: self.last_type_str.clone(),
                    total: heads,
                    summary,
                },
            );
            return;
        }

        let mut rolls: Vec<(i32, bool)> = (0..count)
            .map(|_| {
                let r = ((Self::random_u32() % sides) + 1) as i32;
                let adjusted = (r + self.strength).clamp(1, sides as i32) + self.flat_mod;
                (adjusted, true)
            })
            .collect();

        if self.keep_mode == "kh" {
            rolls.sort_by_key(|r| std::cmp::Reverse(r.0));
            for r in rolls.iter_mut().skip(1) {
                r.1 = false;
            }
        } else if self.keep_mode == "kl" {
            rolls.sort_by_key(|a| a.0);
            for r in rolls.iter_mut().skip(1) {
                r.1 = false;
            }
        }

        let total: i32 = rolls.iter().filter(|r| r.1).map(|r| r.0).sum();
        let summary: Vec<String> = rolls
            .iter()
            .map(|r| {
                if r.1 {
                    r.0.to_string()
                } else {
                    format!("({})", r.0)
                }
            })
            .collect();

        self.last_total = total;
        self.last_rolls = rolls;
        self.last_type_str = format!("{}d{}", count, sides);

        self.history.insert(
            0,
            RollHistoryItem {
                time_str: Local::now().format("%H:%M:%S").to_string(),
                spec: self.last_type_str.clone(),
                total,
                summary: summary.join(", "),
            },
        );
    }

    pub fn update(&mut self, message: RngMessage) {
        match message {
            RngMessage::DiceCountChanged(val) => {
                self.dice_count = val.parse().unwrap_or(1).clamp(1, 100);
            }
            RngMessage::StrengthChanged(val) => {
                self.strength = val.parse().unwrap_or(0);
            }
            RngMessage::FlatModChanged(val) => {
                self.flat_mod = val.parse().unwrap_or(0);
            }
            RngMessage::SelectSides(s) => {
                self.selected_sides = s;
            }
            RngMessage::SelectMode(m) => {
                self.keep_mode = m;
            }
            RngMessage::ExecuteRoll => {
                self.roll();
            }
            RngMessage::Dismiss => {}
        }
    }

    pub fn view<'a>(&'a self, theme: &ShellTheme) -> Element<'a, RngMessage> {
        let dice_types = [
            ("🪙 Coin", 2),
            ("d4", 4),
            ("d6", 6),
            ("d8", 8),
            ("d10", 10),
            ("d12", 12),
            ("d20", 20),
            ("d100", 100),
        ];

        let mut dice_buttons_row = row![].spacing(8);
        for (label, sides) in dice_types {
            let is_sel = self.selected_sides == sides;
            let col = if is_sel { theme.success } else { theme.text };
            let btn_item = button(text(label).size(14).bold().color(col))
                .on_press(RngMessage::SelectSides(sides))
                .padding([8, 14])
                .style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() });
            dice_buttons_row = dice_buttons_row.push(btn_item);
        }

        let mode_row = row![
            text("Mode:").size(theme.font_size_normal).bold().color(theme.text),
            button(text("Keep All").size(12).color(if self.keep_mode == "all" { theme.success } else { theme.text }))
                .on_press(RngMessage::SelectMode("all".into()))
                .style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() }),
            button(text("Advantage").size(12).color(if self.keep_mode == "kh" { theme.success } else { theme.text }))
                .on_press(RngMessage::SelectMode("kh".into()))
                .style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() }),
            button(text("Disadvantage").size(12).color(if self.keep_mode == "kl" { theme.success } else { theme.text }))
                .on_press(RngMessage::SelectMode("kl".into()))
                .style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() }),
        ]
        .spacing(12)
        .align_y(Alignment::Center);

        let roll_btn = button(
            text("🎲 ROLL OUTCOME")
                .size(theme.font_size_overlay)
                .bold()
                .color(theme.success)
                .align_x(Alignment::Center),
        )
        .on_press(RngMessage::ExecuteRoll)
        .padding(14)
        .width(Length::Fill)
        .style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() });

        let result_box = row![
            text(format!("{}: ", self.last_type_str)).size(16).color(theme.muted),
            text(format!("TOTAL: {}", self.last_total)).size(22).bold().color(theme.success),
        ]
        .spacing(12)
        .align_y(Alignment::Center);

        let card_bg = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Rounded(12))
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.border, theme.border_width);

        let card_content = container(
            column![
                text("🎲 DICE, COIN & RNG ROLLER")
                    .size(theme.font_size_overlay)
                    .color(theme.text),
                dice_buttons_row,
                mode_row,
                roll_btn,
                result_box,
                text("ESC to dismiss  •  Enter to roll")
                    .size(11)
                    .color(theme.muted)
                    .align_x(Alignment::Center),
            ]
            .spacing(16)
            .padding(24),
        )
        .width(Length::Fixed(680.0))
        .height(Length::Fixed(420.0));

        let card = Stack::new().push(card_bg.view()).push(card_content);

        let backdrop = button(container(column![]).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fill))
            .on_press(RngMessage::Dismiss)
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgba8(0, 0, 0, 0.6))),
                ..Default::default()
            });

        Stack::new()
            .push(backdrop)
            .push(container(card).center_x(Length::Fill).center_y(Length::Fill))
            .into()
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/overlays/email.rs ===
use iced::widget::{button, column, container, row, scrollable, text, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use tokio::process::Command;

use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType};
use crate::style::TextExt;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct EnvelopeItem {
    pub id: String,
    pub subject: Option<String>,
    pub from: Option<SenderInfo>,
    pub flags: Option<Vec<String>>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SenderInfo {
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub addr: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum EmailMessage {
    SelectFolder(String),
    SelectMail(String),
    MailBodyLoaded(String),
    OpenComposer,
    ToChanged(String),
    SubjectChanged(String),
    BodyChanged(String),
    SendMail,
    Dismiss,
}

pub struct EmailOverlay {
    pub folders: Vec<&'static str>,
    pub active_folder: String,
    pub envelopes: Vec<EnvelopeItem>,
    pub active_mail_id: Option<String>,
    pub mail_body: String,
    pub is_composing: bool,
    pub compose_to: String,
    pub compose_subject: String,
    pub compose_body: String,
}

impl Default for EmailOverlay {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl EmailOverlay {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            folders: vec!["INBOX", "Starred", "Sent", "Drafts", "Trash", "Spam"],
            active_folder: "INBOX".into(),
            envelopes: Vec::new(),
            active_mail_id: None,
            mail_body: String::new(),
            is_composing: false,
            compose_to: String::new(),
            compose_subject: String::new(),
            compose_body: String::new(),
        }
    }

    pub async fn fetch_envelopes(folder: &str) -> Vec<EnvelopeItem> {
        let Ok(out) = Command::new("himalaya")
            .args(["envelope", "list", "--folder", folder, "--page-size", "40", "--output", "json"])
            .output()
            .await
        else {
            return Vec::new();
        };

        serde_json::from_slice(&out.stdout).unwrap_or_default()
    }

    pub async fn read_message(id: &str, folder: &str) -> String {
        let Ok(out) = Command::new("himalaya")
            .args(["message", "read", id, "--folder", folder])
            .output()
            .await
        else {
            return "Failed to load email message.".into();
        };

        String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).to_string()
    }

    pub async fn send_message(to: &str, subject: &str, body: &str) {
        let raw = format!("To: {}\nSubject: {}\n\n{}", to, subject, body);
        let child = Command::new("himalaya")
            .args(["template", "send"])
            .stdin(std::process::Stdio::piped())
            .spawn()
            .ok();

        if let Some(mut c) = child {
            use tokio::io::AsyncWriteExt;
            if let Some(mut stdin) = c.stdin.take() {
                let _ = stdin.write_all(raw.as_bytes()).await;
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn update(&mut self, message: EmailMessage) {
        match message {
            EmailMessage::SelectFolder(f) => {
                self.active_folder = f;
            }
            EmailMessage::SelectMail(id) => {
                self.active_mail_id = Some(id);
            }
            EmailMessage::MailBodyLoaded(b) => {
                self.mail_body = b;
            }
            EmailMessage::OpenComposer => {
                self.is_composing = true;
            }
            EmailMessage::ToChanged(t) => {
                self.compose_to = t;
            }
            EmailMessage::SubjectChanged(s) => {
                self.compose_subject = s;
            }
            EmailMessage::BodyChanged(b) => {
                self.compose_body = b;
            }
            EmailMessage::SendMail => {
                let to = self.compose_to.clone();
                let sub = self.compose_subject.clone();
                let b = self.compose_body.clone();
                self.is_composing = false;
                self.compose_to.clear();
                self.compose_subject.clear();
                self.compose_body.clear();
                tokio::spawn(async move {
                    Self::send_message(&to, &sub, &b).await;
                });
            }
            EmailMessage::Dismiss => {
                self.is_composing = false;
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn view<'a>(&'a self, theme: &ShellTheme) -> Element<'a, EmailMessage> {
        let mut folder_col = column![].spacing(6);
        for f in &self.folders {
            let is_sel = &self.active_folder == f;
            let col = if is_sel { theme.success } else { theme.text };
            let f_clone = f.to_string();

            let f_btn = button(text(*f).size(theme.font_size_normal).bold().color(col))
                .on_press(EmailMessage::SelectFolder(f_clone))
                .padding([8, 12])
                .style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() });

            folder_col = folder_col.push(f_btn);
        }

        let mut mail_col = column![].spacing(6);
        for mail in &self.envelopes {
            let id = mail.id.clone();
            let sender = mail.from.as_ref().and_then(|f| f.name.clone().or_else(|| f.addr.clone())).unwrap_or_else(|| "Unknown".into());
            let subject = mail.subject.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "(no subject)".into());

            let row_item = button(
                column![
                    text(sender).size(theme.font_size_normal).bold().color(theme.text),
                    text(subject).size(theme.font_size_normal - 2).color(theme.muted),
                ]
                .spacing(2),
            )
            .on_press(EmailMessage::SelectMail(id))
            .padding(10)
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() });

            mail_col = mail_col.push(row_item);
        }

        let preview_content = scrollable(
            text(&self.mail_body)
                .size(theme.font_size_normal)
                .color(theme.text),
        )
        .height(Length::Fill);

        let main_row = row![
            container(folder_col).width(Length::Fixed(180.0)).padding(10),
            container(scrollable(mail_col)).width(Length::Fixed(360.0)).padding(10),
            container(preview_content).width(Length::Fill).padding(14),
        ]
        .spacing(12);

        let card_bg = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Rounded(12))
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.border, theme.border_width);

        let card_content = container(
            column![
                row![
                    text("✉ EMAIL CLIENT (HIMALAYA)")
                        .size(theme.font_size_overlay)
                        .color(theme.text),
                    row![].width(Length::Fill),
                    button(text("✏ Compose").size(12).bold().color(theme.success))
                        .on_press(EmailMessage::OpenComposer)
                        .style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() }),
                ]
                .align_y(Alignment::Center),
                main_row,
                text("ESC to dismiss  •  Ctrl+Enter to send in composer")
                    .size(11)
                    .color(theme.muted)
                    .align_x(Alignment::Center),
            ]
            .spacing(14)
            .padding(20),
        )
        .width(Length::Fixed(1280.0))
        .height(Length::Fixed(800.0));

        let card = Stack::new().push(card_bg.view()).push(card_content);

        let backdrop = button(container(column![]).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fill))
            .on_press(EmailMessage::Dismiss)
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgba8(0, 0, 0, 0.6))),
                ..Default::default()
            });

        Stack::new()
            .push(backdrop)
            .push(container(card).center_x(Length::Fill).center_y(Length::Fill))
            .into()
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/overlays/lockscreen.rs ===
use chrono::Local;
use iced::widget::{column, container, text, text_input, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length};
use tokio::process::Command;

use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType};
use crate::style::TextExt;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum LockMessage {
    PasswordInputChanged(String),
    SubmitUnlock,
    UnlockSuccess,
    UnlockFailed,
}

pub struct LockscreenOverlay {
    pub password_buffer: String,
    pub is_locked: bool,
    pub auth_failed: bool,
    pub is_caps_lock: bool,
}

impl Default for LockscreenOverlay {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl LockscreenOverlay {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            password_buffer: String::new(),
            is_locked: false,
            auth_failed: false,
            is_caps_lock: Self::check_caps_lock(),
        }
    }

    pub fn check_caps_lock() -> bool {
        for i in 0..6 {
            let p = format!("/sys/class/leds/input{}::capslock/brightness", i);
            if let Ok(content) = std::fs::read_to_string(p) {
                if content.trim() == "1" {
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }
        false
    }

    pub async fn authenticate_pam(password: &str) -> bool {
        let user = std::env::var("USER").unwrap_or_else(|_| "root".into());
        let clean_pass = password.trim();
        if clean_pass.is_empty() {
            return false;
        }

        let child = Command::new("su")
            .args(["-c", "true", &user])
            .stdin(std::process::Stdio::piped())
            .stdout(std::process::Stdio::null())
            .stderr(std::process::Stdio::null())
            .spawn()
            .ok();

        if let Some(mut c) = child {
            use tokio::io::AsyncWriteExt;
            if let Some(mut stdin) = c.stdin.take() {
                let _ = stdin.write_all(format!("{}\n", clean_pass).as_bytes()).await;
            }
            if let Ok(st) = c.wait().await {
                return st.success();
            }
        }
        false
    }

    pub async fn set_dpms(on: bool) {
        let state = if on { "on" } else { "off" };
        if std::env::var("HYPRLAND_INSTANCE_SIGNATURE").is_ok() {
            let _ = Command::new("hyprctl").args(["dispatch", "dpms", state]).status().await;
        } else if std::env::var("SWAYSOCK").is_ok() {
            let _ = Command::new("swaymsg").args(["output", "*", "dpms", state]).status().await;
        }
    }

    pub fn update(&mut self, message: LockMessage) {
        match message {
            LockMessage::PasswordInputChanged(p) => {
                self.password_buffer = p;
                self.auth_failed = false;
                self.is_caps_lock = Self::check_caps_lock();
            }
            LockMessage::UnlockSuccess => {
                self.is_locked = false;
                self.password_buffer.clear();
                self.auth_failed = false;
                tokio::spawn(async {
                    Self::set_dpms(true).await;
                });
            }
            LockMessage::UnlockFailed => {
                self.password_buffer.clear();
                self.auth_failed = true;
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    pub fn view<'a>(&'a self, theme: &ShellTheme) -> Element<'a, LockMessage> {
        let time_str = Local::now().format("%H:%M").to_string();
        let date_str = Local::now().format("%A, %B %d").to_string();

        let placeholder = if self.auth_failed {
            "Authentication Failed..."
        } else {
            "Enter password..."
        };

        let input = text_input(placeholder, &self.password_buffer)
            .on_input(LockMessage::PasswordInputChanged)
            .on_submit(LockMessage::SubmitUnlock)
            .secure(true)
            .padding(14)
            .size(theme.font_size_overlay)
            .width(Length::Fixed(400.0));

        let card_bg = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Rounded(12))
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(if self.auth_failed { theme.danger } else { theme.border }, theme.border_width);

        let content = container(
            column![
                text(time_str).size(56).bold().color(theme.text).align_x(Alignment::Center),
                text(date_str).size(theme.font_size_overlay).color(theme.muted).align_x(Alignment::Center),
                input,
                if self.is_caps_lock {
                    text("⇪ CAPS LOCK ACTIVE").size(12).bold().color(theme.warning).align_x(Alignment::Center)
                } else {
                    text("").size(12)
                },
                text("Press [Enter] to unlock")
                    .size(11)
                    .color(theme.muted)
                    .align_x(Alignment::Center),
            ]
            .spacing(18)
            .padding(32)
            .align_x(Alignment::Center),
        )
        .width(Length::Fixed(480.0));

        let card = Stack::new().push(card_bg.view()).push(content);

        container(card)
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .height(Length::Fill)
            .center_x(Length::Fill)
            .center_y(Length::Fill)
            .style(|_t| container::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgb8(10, 10, 12))),
                ..Default::default()
            })
            .into()
    }
}
