=== FILE: ./src/overlays/annotator.rs ===
//! Annotator overlay.
//! - Watermark: LSB steganography — 1 bit per RGB channel, imperceptible.
//! - Reveal: decodes LSBs in-memory, shows recovered text as a UI overlay.
//!   No image capture, no temp files, no ImageMagick for reveal.
//! - Save: composes annotations via ImageMagick, then LSB-embeds watermark
//!   per recipient, writes `annotated_STAMP_{name}.png`.
//! - Enter: save (annotated+watermarked) then close. Always.

use iced::widget::canvas::{self, Canvas, Frame, Geometry};
use iced::widget::{button, container, text, text_input, Column, Row, Stack};
use iced::{event, mouse, Alignment, Color, Element, Length, Point, Rectangle, Size};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Tool { Rect, Ellipse, Arrow, Pen, Text, Counter, Redact }
impl Tool {
    fn glyph(self) -> &'static str {
        match self { Self::Rect=>"Rect", Self::Ellipse=>"Circle", Self::Arrow=>"Arrow", Self::Pen=>"Pen", Self::Text=>"Text", Self::Counter=>"Num", Self::Redact=>"Redact" }
    }
    pub fn all() -> [Self; 7] { [Self::Rect, Self::Ellipse, Self::Arrow, Self::Pen, Self::Text, Self::Counter, Self::Redact] }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Annotation {
    pub tool: Tool,
    pub start: Point,
    pub end: Point,
    pub points: Vec<Point>,
    pub color: Color,
    pub width: f32,
    pub number: u32,
    pub text: String,
    pub font_size: f32,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum AnnotatorMessage {
    Pressed(Point),
    Moved(Point),
    Released(Point),
    SetTool(Tool),
    SetColor(Color),
    Undo,
    ClearAll,
    Save,
    Copy,
    Cancel,
    Loaded(PathBuf, Vec<u8>),
    Saved(Result<Vec<PathBuf>, String>),
    WatermarkInputChanged(String),
    ToggleReveal,
    RevealLoaded(Result<Vec<u8>, String>),
    TextEditingChanged(String),
    TextCommitted,
    CharacterInput(String),
    BackspacePressed,
    CancelTextEditing,
    ConfirmText,
    CancelText,
    FontSizeUp,
    FontSizeDown,
    NewLinePressed,
}

pub const PALETTE: [Color; 8] = [
    Color::from_rgb(1.0, 0.27, 0.23), Color::from_rgb(1.0, 0.62, 0.04),
    Color::from_rgb(1.0, 0.84, 0.04), Color::from_rgb(0.20, 0.84, 0.29),
    Color::from_rgb(0.04, 0.52, 1.0), Color::from_rgb(0.37, 0.36, 0.90),
    Color::from_rgb(1.0, 1.0, 1.0), Color::from_rgb(0.11, 0.11, 0.12),
];

pub struct AnnotatorOverlay {
    pub handle: Option<iced::widget::image::Handle>,
    pub text_editing: Option<usize>,
    pub text_buffer: String,
    pub text_dragging: bool,
    pub text_drag_offset: Point,
    pub text_edit_original: Option<String>,
    pub text_font_size: f32,
    pub image_size: Size,
    pub annotations: Vec<Annotation>,
    in_progress: Option<Annotation>,
    pub tool: Tool,
    pub color: Color,
    pub stroke_width: f32,
    pub status: String,
    pub source_path: Option<PathBuf>,
    pub watermark_input: String,
    pub watermark_cli: Option<String>,
    drawing: bool,
    pub reveal_mode: bool,
    pub reveal_text: Option<String>,
    pub reveal_handle: Option<iced::widget::image::Handle>,
    pub reveal_bytes: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    pub reveal_computing: bool,
}

impl Default for AnnotatorOverlay {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl AnnotatorOverlay {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            handle: None,
            text_editing: None,
            text_buffer: String::new(),
            text_dragging: false,
            text_drag_offset: Point::ORIGIN,
            text_edit_original: None,
            text_font_size: 20.0,
            image_size: Size::new(0.0, 0.0),
            annotations: Vec::new(),
            in_progress: None,
            tool: Tool::Rect,
            color: PALETTE[0],
            stroke_width: 4.0,
            status: "Draw · Enter saves & closes · ESC cancels · 👁 reveals watermark".into(),
            source_path: None,
            watermark_input: String::new(),
            watermark_cli: None,
            drawing: false,
            reveal_mode: false,
            reveal_text: None,
            reveal_handle: None,
            reveal_bytes: None,
            reveal_computing: false,
        }
    }

    pub fn load_image(&mut self, path: PathBuf, bytes: Vec<u8>) {
        match ::image::load_from_memory(&bytes) {
            Ok(img) => {
                self.image_size = Size::new(img.width() as f32, img.height() as f32);
                self.handle = Some(iced::widget::image::Handle::from_bytes(bytes));
                self.source_path = Some(path);
                self.status = format!("{}×{} · draw · Enter saves & closes", img.width(), img.height());
            }
            Err(e) => self.status = format!("decode failed: {e}"),
        }
    }

    pub fn update(&mut self, msg: AnnotatorMessage) {
        match msg {
            AnnotatorMessage::Pressed(p) if !self.reveal_mode => self.on_press(p),
            AnnotatorMessage::Moved(p) if !self.reveal_mode => self.on_move(p),
            AnnotatorMessage::Released(p) if !self.reveal_mode => self.on_release(p),
            AnnotatorMessage::SetTool(t) => self.tool = t,
            AnnotatorMessage::SetColor(c) => self.color = c,
            AnnotatorMessage::Undo => { self.annotations.pop(); }
            AnnotatorMessage::ClearAll => self.annotations.clear(),
            AnnotatorMessage::WatermarkInputChanged(s) => self.watermark_input = s,

            AnnotatorMessage::CharacterInput(s) => {
                if let Some(idx) = self.text_editing {
                    self.text_buffer.push_str(&s);
                    if let Some(a) = self.annotations.get_mut(idx) {
                        a.text = self.text_buffer.clone();
                    }
                }
            }
            AnnotatorMessage::BackspacePressed => {
                if let Some(idx) = self.text_editing {
                    self.text_buffer.pop();
                    if let Some(a) = self.annotations.get_mut(idx) {
                        a.text = self.text_buffer.clone();
                    }
                }
            }
            AnnotatorMessage::NewLinePressed => {
                if let Some(idx) = self.text_editing {
                    self.text_buffer.push('\n');
                    if let Some(a) = self.annotations.get_mut(idx) {
                        a.text = self.text_buffer.clone();
                    }
                }
            }
            AnnotatorMessage::ConfirmText => {
                if let Some(idx) = self.text_editing.take() {
                    if self.text_buffer.trim().is_empty() {
                        self.annotations.remove(idx);
                    } else if let Some(a) = self.annotations.get_mut(idx) {
                        a.text = self.text_buffer.clone();
                        a.font_size = self.text_font_size;
                    }
                }
                self.text_edit_original = None;
            }
            AnnotatorMessage::CancelText => {
                if let Some(idx) = self.text_editing.take() {
                    match self.text_edit_original.take() {
                        Some(original) => {
                            // Resume-cancel: restore original text
                            if let Some(a) = self.annotations.get_mut(idx) {
                                a.text = original;
                            }
                        }
                        None => {
                            // New-cancel: remove the empty annotation
                            self.annotations.remove(idx);
                        }
                    }
                }
                self.text_buffer.clear();
            }
            AnnotatorMessage::FontSizeUp => {
                self.text_font_size = (self.text_font_size + 2.0).min(96.0);
                if let Some(idx) = self.text_editing {
                    if let Some(a) = self.annotations.get_mut(idx) {
                        a.font_size = self.text_font_size;
                    }
                }
            }
            AnnotatorMessage::FontSizeDown => {
                self.text_font_size = (self.text_font_size - 2.0).max(10.0);
                if let Some(idx) = self.text_editing {
                    if let Some(a) = self.annotations.get_mut(idx) {
                        a.font_size = self.text_font_size;
                    }
                }
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    fn on_press(&mut self, p: Point) {
        eprintln!("[mbs-child] on_press ({:.0},{:.0}) tool={:?}", p.x, p.y, self.tool);
        
        // If already editing text, check if click is inside the box (for drag)
        if let Some(idx) = self.text_editing {
            let in_box = if let Some(a) = self.annotations.get(idx) {
                let font_size = a.font_size.max(10.0);
                let line_height = font_size * 1.2;
                let max_chars = a.text.lines().map(|l| l.chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0).max(4);
                let pad = 24.0;
                let bw = (max_chars as f32 * font_size * 0.55) + pad * 2.0;
                let bh = (a.text.lines().count() as f32).max(1.0) * line_height + pad * 2.0;
                let bx = a.start.x - pad;
                let by = a.start.y - pad;
                p.x >= bx && p.x <= bx + bw && p.y >= by && p.y <= by + bh
            } else {
                false
            };

            if in_box {
                self.text_dragging = true;
                if let Some(a) = self.annotations.get(idx) {
                    self.text_drag_offset = Point::new(p.x - a.start.x, p.y - a.start.y);
                }
                eprintln!("[mbs-child] Text drag started, offset=({:.0},{:.0})", self.text_drag_offset.x, self.text_drag_offset.y);
                return;
            }

            // Click outside → commit current text and start new
            if let Some(a) = self.annotations.get(idx) {
                if a.text.trim().is_empty() {
                    self.annotations.remove(idx);
                }
            }
            self.text_editing = None;
            self.text_buffer.clear();
            self.text_edit_original = None;
        }

        // Text tool doesn't use drag; it opens a multiline editor at the click position
        if self.tool == Tool::Text {
            eprintln!("[mbs-child] Text tool pressed at ({:.0},{:.0})", p.x, p.y);
                        // Hit-test existing text annotations (newest first = topmost)
            for idx in (0..self.annotations.len()).rev() {
                let a = &self.annotations[idx];
                if a.tool != Tool::Text { continue; }
                let font_size = a.font_size.max(10.0);
                let line_height = font_size * 1.2;
                let max_chars = a.text.lines().map(|l| l.chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0).max(4);
                let pad = 24.0;
                let bw = (max_chars as f32 * font_size * 0.55) + pad * 2.0;
                let bh = (a.text.lines().count() as f32).max(1.0) * line_height + pad * 2.0;
                let bx = a.start.x - pad;
                let by = a.start.y - pad;
                if p.x >= bx && p.x <= bx + bw && p.y >= by && p.y <= by + bh {
                    eprintln!("[mbs-child] Resuming text annotation idx={}", idx);
                    self.text_buffer = a.text.clone();
                    self.text_font_size = a.font_size;
                    self.text_edit_original = Some(a.text.clone());
                    self.text_editing = Some(idx);
                    return;
                }
            }

            // No existing hit — start a new text annotation
            self.text_edit_original = None;
            self.text_buffer = String::new();
            self.text_font_size = 20.0;
            let idx = self.annotations.len();
            self.annotations.push(Annotation {
                tool: Tool::Text,
                start: p, end: p,
                points: Vec::new(),
                color: self.color, width: self.stroke_width,
                number: 0,
                text: String::new(),
                font_size: self.text_font_size,
            });
            self.text_buffer = String::new();
            self.text_font_size = 20.0;
            self.text_editing = Some(idx);
            eprintln!("[mbs-child] Text editing started, idx={}", idx);
            return;
        }
        
        self.drawing = true;
        let counter_num = self.annotations.iter().filter(|a| a.tool == Tool::Counter).count() as u32 + 1;
        self.in_progress = Some(Annotation {
            tool: self.tool, start: p, end: p,
            points: if self.tool == Tool::Pen { vec![p] } else { Vec::new() },
            color: self.color, width: self.stroke_width,
            number: if self.tool == Tool::Counter { counter_num } else { 0 },
            text: String::new(),
            font_size: 0.0, // only used by Text tool
        });
    }
    fn on_move(&mut self, p: Point) {
        if self.text_dragging {
            if let Some(idx) = self.text_editing {
                if let Some(a) = self.annotations.get_mut(idx) {
                    a.start = Point::new(p.x - self.text_drag_offset.x, p.y - self.text_drag_offset.y);
                }
            }
            return;
        }
        if !self.drawing { return; }
        if let Some(ref mut ann) = self.in_progress {
            if ann.tool == Tool::Pen {
                ann.points.push(p); // Accumulate points for pen
            } else {
                ann.end = p; // Update end point for shapes
            }
        }
    }
    fn on_release(&mut self, p: Point) {
        if self.text_dragging {
            self.text_dragging = false;
            return;
        }
        eprintln!("[mbs-child] on_release ({:.0},{:.0}) in_progress={}", p.x, p.y, self.in_progress.is_some());
        eprintln!("[mbs-child] on_release at ({:.0},{:.0}) — in_progress={}",
            p.x, p.y, self.in_progress.is_some());
        self.drawing = false;
        if let Some(mut a) = self.in_progress.take() {
            if a.tool != Tool::Pen { a.end = p; }
            let ok = match a.tool {
                Tool::Pen => a.points.len() > 2,
                Tool::Counter => true, // single-click is valid for counters
                _ => (a.end.x - a.start.x).abs() > 0.5 || (a.end.y - a.start.y).abs() > 0.5,
            };
            if ok {
                eprintln!("[mbs-child] annotation ACCEPTED: {} total", self.annotations.len() + 1);
                self.annotations.push(a);
            } else {
                eprintln!("[mbs-child] annotation REJECTED (too small: dx={:.2} dy={:.2})",
                    (a.end.x - a.start.x).abs(), (a.end.y - a.start.y).abs());
            }
        }
    }

            pub fn view<'a>(&'a self) -> Element<'a, AnnotatorMessage> {
        if self.handle.is_none() {
            return container(
                text(self.status.clone()).size(18).color(Color::WHITE)
            )
            .width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fill)
            .center_x(Length::Fill).center_y(Length::Fill)
            .style(|_t| container::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgb(0.05, 0.02, 0.02))),
                ..Default::default()
            }).into();
        }

        // Image widget (below) + canvas overlay (above)
                let active_handle = if self.reveal_mode {
            self.reveal_handle.clone().or_else(|| self.handle.clone())
        } else {
            self.handle.clone()
        };

        // ContentFit::Contain preserves 1:1 pixel mapping so the auto-leveled
        // high-pass reveal isn't blurred by upsampling. Source is also shown
        // at 1:1 this way (matches how QML displays the reveal overlay).
        let image_widget = iced::widget::image(active_handle.unwrap())
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .height(Length::Fill)
            .content_fit(iced::ContentFit::Contain);

        let canvas = Canvas::new(OverlayCanvas {
            handle: None,
            image_size: self.image_size,
            annotations: if self.reveal_mode { Vec::new() } else { self.annotations.clone() },
            in_progress: if self.reveal_mode { None } else { self.in_progress.clone() },
            reveal_mode: self.reveal_mode,
            text_editing: self.text_editing.is_some(),
            editing_idx: self.text_editing,
        })
        .width(Length::Fill)
        .height(Length::Fill);

        let reveal_banner: Element<'a, AnnotatorMessage> = if self.reveal_mode && self.reveal_text.is_some() {
            let txt = self.reveal_text.clone().unwrap_or_default();
            container(
                text(txt).size(56).color(Color::from_rgb(0.2, 1.0, 0.3)).font(iced::Font {
                    weight: iced::font::Weight::Bold,
                    ..Default::default()
                })
            )
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .padding([32, 20])
            .center_x(Length::Fill)
            .style(|_t| container::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgba(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.88))),
                border: iced::Border { color: Color::WHITE, width: 2.0, radius: 8.0.into() },
                ..Default::default()
            })
            .into()
        } else if self.reveal_mode {
            // Plate case: amplified image is the reveal. No banner.
            container(text("")).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fixed(0.0)).into()
        } else {
            container(text("")).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fixed(0.0)).into()
        };

        let canvas_stack = container(
            Stack::new()
                .push(image_widget)
                .push(canvas)
                .push(
                    container(reveal_banner)
                        .width(Length::Fill)
                        .height(Length::Fill)
                        .align_y(Alignment::End)
                        .padding(iced::Padding {
                            top: 0.0,
                            right: 0.0,
                            bottom: 40.0,
                            left: 0.0,
                        })
                )
        )
        .width(Length::Fill)
        .height(Length::Fill)
        .style(|_t| container::Style {
            background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgb(0.02, 0.02, 0.03))),
            ..Default::default()
        });

        // Toolbar
        let mut tool_row = Row::new().spacing(6).align_y(Alignment::Center);
        for t in Tool::all() {
            let active = self.tool == t;
            tool_row = tool_row.push(
                button(text(t.glyph()).size(20))
                    .on_press(AnnotatorMessage::SetTool(t))
                    .padding([6, 12])
                    .style(move |_t, _s| button::Style {
                        background: Some(iced::Background::Color(if active {
                            Color::from_rgb(0.04, 0.52, 1.0)
                        } else {
                            Color::from_rgba(0.15, 0.15, 0.20, 0.9)
                        })),
                        text_color: Color::WHITE,
                        border: iced::Border { color: Color::WHITE, width: 1.0, radius: 4.0.into() },
                        ..Default::default()
                    })
            );
        }

        let mut color_row = Row::new().spacing(4).align_y(Alignment::Center);
        for c in PALETTE {
            let active = self.color == c;
            color_row = color_row.push(
                button(text("").size(1))
                    .on_press(AnnotatorMessage::SetColor(c))
                    .width(Length::Fixed(24.0))
                    .height(Length::Fixed(24.0))
                    .padding(0)
                    .style(move |_t, _s| button::Style {
                        background: Some(iced::Background::Color(c)),
                        border: iced::Border {
                            color: if active { Color::WHITE } else { Color::from_rgb(0.3, 0.3, 0.4) },
                            width: if active { 2.5 } else { 1.0 },
                            radius: 12.0.into(),
                        },
                        ..Default::default()
                    })
            );
        }

        let wm_input = text_input("recipient(s) for LSB watermark", &self.watermark_input)
            .on_input(AnnotatorMessage::WatermarkInputChanged)
            .on_submit(AnnotatorMessage::Save)
            .padding([6, 10])
            .size(13)
            .width(Length::Fixed(220.0));

        let reveal_label = if self.reveal_mode { "◀ Exit Reveal" } else { "👁 Reveal" };
        let reveal_btn = button(text(reveal_label).size(13))
            .on_press(AnnotatorMessage::ToggleReveal)
            .padding([6, 12])
            .style(move |_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(if self.reveal_mode {
                    Color::from_rgb(0.45, 0.25, 0.85)
                } else {
                    Color::from_rgb(0.04, 0.52, 1.0)
                })),
                text_color: Color::WHITE,
                border: iced::Border { color: Color::WHITE, width: 1.0, radius: 4.0.into() },
                ..Default::default()
            });

        let mk = |lbl: &'static str, msg: AnnotatorMessage| {
            button(text(lbl).size(13))
                .on_press(msg)
                .padding([6, 12])
                .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                    background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgba(0.15, 0.15, 0.20, 0.9))),
                    text_color: Color::WHITE,
                    border: iced::Border { color: Color::WHITE, width: 1.0, radius: 4.0.into() },
                    ..Default::default()
                })
        };

        let mut font_controls = Row::new().spacing(6).align_y(Alignment::Center);
        if self.text_editing.is_some() {
            font_controls = font_controls
                .push(text(" | Font:").size(13).color(Color::WHITE))
                .push(button(text("-").size(16)).on_press(AnnotatorMessage::FontSizeDown).padding([2, 8]))
                .push(text(format!("{:.0}px", self.text_font_size)).size(14).color(Color::WHITE).width(Length::Fixed(45.0)))
                .push(button(text("+").size(16)).on_press(AnnotatorMessage::FontSizeUp).padding([2, 8]));
        }

        let toolbar = container(
            Column::new()
                .push(Row::new()
                    .push(text("Tools:").size(13).color(Color::WHITE))
                    .push(tool_row)
                    .push(text(" ").size(13))
                    .push(color_row)
                    .push(font_controls) // <-- Added font controls here
                    .spacing(10).align_y(Alignment::Center))
                .push(Row::new()
                    .push(text("Watermark:").size(13).color(Color::WHITE))
                    .push(wm_input)
                    .push(reveal_btn)
                    .push(mk("Undo", AnnotatorMessage::Undo))
                    .push(mk("Clear", AnnotatorMessage::ClearAll))
                    .push(mk("Cancel", AnnotatorMessage::Cancel))
                    .spacing(8).align_y(Alignment::Center))
                .spacing(6)
        )
        .width(Length::Fill)
        .padding([10, 12])
        .style(|_t| container::Style {
            background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgb(0.05, 0.05, 0.08))),
            ..Default::default()
        });

        let status_bar = if self.text_editing.is_some() {
            let hint = format!("[TEXT MODE] Drag the box to reposition · F2=place · Esc=cancel · [ ]=font ({:.0}px)", self.text_font_size);
            container(text(hint).size(12).color(Color::WHITE))
                .width(Length::Fill)
                .padding([6, 14])
                .style(|_t| container::Style {
                    background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgb(0.2, 0.2, 0.25))),
                    ..Default::default()
                })
        } else {
            container(text(self.status.clone()).size(12).color(Color::from_rgb(0.7, 0.7, 0.75)))
                .width(Length::Fill)
                .padding([6, 14])
                .style(|_t| container::Style {
                    background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgb(0.05, 0.05, 0.08))),
                    ..Default::default()
                })
        };

        Column::new().push(toolbar).push(canvas_stack).push(status_bar).into()
    }
}

// ── Canvas ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

#[derive(Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
struct OverlayCanvas {
    handle: Option<iced::widget::image::Handle>,
    image_size: Size,
    annotations: Vec<Annotation>,
    in_progress: Option<Annotation>,
    reveal_mode: bool,
    text_editing: bool,
    editing_idx: Option<usize>,
}

fn compute_display_rect(bounds: Rectangle, image: Size) -> Option<Rectangle> {
    if image.width <= 0.0 || image.height <= 0.0 { return None; }
    let fit = (bounds.width / image.width).min(bounds.height / image.height);
    let scale = fit;
    let w = image.width * scale;
    let h = image.height * scale;
    Some(Rectangle::new(
        Point::new((bounds.width - w) / 2.0, (bounds.height - h) / 2.0),
        Size::new(w, h),
    ))
}

impl canvas::Program<AnnotatorMessage> for OverlayCanvas {
    type State = ();
                fn draw(&self, _s: &(), renderer: &iced::Renderer, _t: &iced::Theme, bounds: Rectangle, _c: mouse::Cursor) -> Vec<Geometry> {
        let mut frame = Frame::new(renderer, bounds.size());

        let dr_opt = compute_display_rect(bounds, self.image_size);

        if let Some(dr) = dr_opt {

            if !self.reveal_mode {
                let scale = dr.width / self.image_size.width;
                let to_disp = |p: Point| Point::new(dr.x + p.x * scale, dr.y + p.y * scale);
                let redacts: Vec<(usize, Point, Point)> = self.annotations.iter()
                    .enumerate()
                    .filter(|(_, a)| a.tool == Tool::Redact)
                    .map(|(i, a)| (i, to_disp(a.start), to_disp(a.end)))
                    .collect();
                for (idx, ann) in self.annotations.iter().enumerate() {
                    draw_ann(&mut frame, ann, &to_disp, scale, &redacts, idx);
                    if self.editing_idx == Some(idx) && ann.tool == Tool::Text {
                        let p = to_disp(ann.start);
                        let font_size = (ann.font_size * scale).max(8.0);
                        let line_height = font_size * 1.2;
                        let max_chars = ann.text.lines().map(|l| l.chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(1);
                        let est_w = max_chars as f32 * font_size * 0.55 + 12.0;
                        let est_h = (ann.text.lines().count() as f32).max(1.0) * line_height + 8.0;
                        let bx = p.x - 6.0;
                        let by = p.y - 4.0;
                        let bc = Color::from_rgba(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.6);
                        let sw = 1.5;
                        frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(bx, by), Size::new(est_w, sw), bc);
                        frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(bx, by + est_h - sw), Size::new(est_w, sw), bc);
                        frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(bx, by), Size::new(sw, est_h), bc);
                        frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(bx + est_w - sw, by), Size::new(sw, est_h), bc);
                    }
                }
                if let Some(a) = &self.in_progress {
                    draw_ann(&mut frame, a, &to_disp, scale, &redacts, self.annotations.len());
                }
            }
        }

        vec![frame.into_geometry()]
    }

    fn update(&self, _s: &mut (), event: canvas::Event, bounds: Rectangle, cursor: mouse::Cursor)
        -> (event::Status, Option<AnnotatorMessage>)
    {
        if self.reveal_mode { return (event::Status::Ignored, None); }
        let Some(dr) = compute_display_rect(bounds, self.image_size) else {
            return (event::Status::Ignored, None);
        };
        let scale = (dr.width / self.image_size.width).max(0.0001);
        let to_img = |p: Point| Point::new((p.x - dr.x) / scale, (p.y - dr.y) / scale);
        let clamp_img = |p: Point| Point::new(
            p.x.clamp(0.0, self.image_size.width),
            p.y.clamp(0.0, self.image_size.height),
        );
        let inside_img = |p: Point| {
            p.x >= 0.0 && p.x <= self.image_size.width &&
            p.y >= 0.0 && p.y <= self.image_size.height
        };
        if let canvas::Event::Mouse(m) = event {
            match m {
                mouse::Event::ButtonPressed(mouse::Button::Left) => {
                    if let Some(p) = cursor.position_in(bounds) {
                        let img = to_img(p);
                        if inside_img(img) {
                            return (event::Status::Captured, Some(AnnotatorMessage::Pressed(img)));
                        }
                        return (event::Status::Ignored, None);
                    }
                }
                mouse::Event::CursorMoved { .. } => {
                    if let Some(p) = cursor.position_in(bounds) {                        return (event::Status::Ignored, Some(AnnotatorMessage::Moved(clamp_img(to_img(p)))));
                    }
                }
                mouse::Event::ButtonReleased(mouse::Button::Left) => {
                    if let Some(p) = cursor.position_in(bounds) {
                        return (event::Status::Captured, Some(AnnotatorMessage::Released(clamp_img(to_img(p)))));
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        (event::Status::Ignored, None)
    }
}

fn draw_ann<F: Fn(Point) -> Point>(frame: &mut Frame, a: &Annotation, to_disp: &F, scale: f32, redacts: &[(usize, Point, Point)], ann_idx: usize) {
    let sp = to_disp;
    let sw = (a.width * scale).max(3.0);
    let r = a.color;

    // Skip text/counter if a LATER redact covers its position.
    // (Text always renders on top of shapes in iced — so we manually
    //  hide it if a redact drawn afterward would have covered it.)
    if matches!(a.tool, Tool::Counter | Tool::Text) {
        let p = sp(a.start);
        let covered = redacts.iter().any(|&(ridx, ra, rb)| {
            ridx > ann_idx
                && p.x >= ra.x.min(rb.x) && p.x <= ra.x.max(rb.x)
                && p.y >= ra.y.min(rb.y) && p.y <= ra.y.max(rb.y)
        });
        if covered { return; }
    }

    match a.tool {
        Tool::Rect => {
            let p1 = sp(a.start); let p2 = sp(a.end);
            let x = p1.x.min(p2.x); let y = p1.y.min(p2.y);
            let w = (p2.x - p1.x).abs().max(6.0);
            let h = (p2.y - p1.y).abs().max(6.0);
            frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(x, y), Size::new(w, h), Color::from_rgba(r.r, r.g, r.b, 0.55));
            frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(x, y), Size::new(w, sw), r);
            frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(x, y + h - sw), Size::new(w, sw), r);
            frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(x, y), Size::new(sw, h), r);
            frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(x + w - sw, y), Size::new(sw, h), r);
        }
        Tool::Redact => {
            let p1 = sp(a.start); let p2 = sp(a.end);
            let x = p1.x.min(p2.x); let y = p1.y.min(p2.y);
            let w = (p2.x - p1.x).abs().max(4.0);
            let h = (p2.y - p1.y).abs().max(4.0);
            // Draw redact as a massive black block. It covers other shapes.
            frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(x, y), Size::new(w, h), Color::BLACK);
        }
        Tool::Arrow => {
            let p1 = sp(a.start); let p2 = sp(a.end);
            let dx = p2.x - p1.x; let dy = p2.y - p1.y;
            let ang = dy.atan2(dx);
            let hl = (sw * 4.0).max(14.0);
            let spread = 0.45;

            // Arrow shaft (stops slightly before the tip)
            let body_path = canvas::Path::new(|b| {
                b.move_to(p1);
                let bx = p2.x - hl * 0.6 * ang.cos();
                let by = p2.y - hl * 0.6 * ang.sin();
                b.line_to(Point::new(bx, by));
            });
            frame.stroke(&body_path, canvas::Stroke::default().with_color(r).with_width(sw).with_line_cap(canvas::LineCap::Round));

            // Arrow head (triangle)
            let head_path = canvas::Path::new(|b| {
                b.move_to(p2);
                b.line_to(Point::new(p2.x - hl * (ang - spread).cos(), p2.y - hl * (ang - spread).sin()));
                b.line_to(Point::new(p2.x - hl * (ang + spread).cos(), p2.y - hl * (ang + spread).sin()));
                b.close();
            });
            frame.fill(&head_path, r);
        }
        Tool::Text => {
            if a.text.is_empty() { return; }
            let p = sp(a.start);
            let font_size = (a.font_size * scale).max(8.0);
            let line_height = font_size * 1.2;
            for (i, line) in a.text.lines().enumerate() {
                frame.fill_text(canvas::Text {
                    content: line.to_string(),
                    position: Point::new(p.x, p.y + i as f32 * line_height),
                    color: r,
                    size: iced::Pixels(font_size),
                    ..canvas::Text::default()
                });
            }
        }
        Tool::Counter => {

            let p = sp(a.start);
            let radius = (sw * 3.0).max(14.0);
            let path = canvas::Path::new(|b| {
                b.circle(p, radius);
            });
            frame.fill(&path, r);
            frame.stroke(&path, canvas::Stroke::default().with_color(Color::WHITE).with_width(2.5));

            frame.fill_text(canvas::Text {
                content: a.number.to_string(),
                position: p, // Center of the circle
                color: Color::WHITE,
                size: iced::Pixels(radius * 1.2),
                horizontal_alignment: iced::alignment::Horizontal::Center,
                vertical_alignment: iced::alignment::Vertical::Center,
                ..canvas::Text::default()
            });
        }        Tool::Ellipse => {
            let p1 = sp(a.start); let p2 = sp(a.end);
            let cx = (p1.x + p2.x) / 2.0;
            let cy = (p1.y + p2.y) / 2.0;
            let rx = ((p2.x - p1.x).abs() / 2.0).max(3.0);
            let ry = ((p2.y - p1.y).abs() / 2.0).max(3.0);
            let segments = 32;
            let path = canvas::Path::new(|b| {
                for i in 0..=segments {
                    let theta = (i as f32 / segments as f32) * std::f32::consts::TAU;
                    let x = cx + rx * theta.cos();
                    let y = cy + ry * theta.sin();
                    if i == 0 { b.move_to(Point::new(x, y)); }
                    else { b.line_to(Point::new(x, y)); }
                }
            });
            frame.stroke(&path, canvas::Stroke::default().with_color(r).with_width(sw));
        }
        Tool::Pen => {
            if a.points.len() < 2 { return; }
            let path = canvas::Path::new(|b| {
                b.move_to(sp(a.points[0]));
                for p in &a.points[1..] {
                    b.line_to(sp(*p));
                }
            });
            frame.stroke(&path, canvas::Stroke::default().with_color(r).with_width(sw));
        }


    }
}

// ── LSB steganography ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
//
// 16-bit length header + UTF-8 payload, 1 bit per RGB channel (LSB).
// Changes each channel's value by at most 1/255 — imperceptible.
// Survives PNG round-tripping (grim + wl-copy + image viewers).





// ── ImageMagick annotation compose ────────────────────────────────────

pub fn magick_or_convert() -> Option<&'static str> {
    if std::process::Command::new("magick").arg("--version").output()
        .map(|o| o.status.success()).unwrap_or(false) { Some("magick") }
    else if std::process::Command::new("convert").arg("--version").output()
        .map(|o| o.status.success()).unwrap_or(false) { Some("convert") }
    else { None }
}

fn find_annotation_font() -> Option<String> {
    // Try fc-match first (works on NixOS, Arch, Debian, etc.)
    if let Ok(out) = std::process::Command::new("fc-match")
        .args(["-f", "%{file}", "sans-serif:bold"])
        .output()
    {
        if out.status.success() {
            let path = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).trim().to_string();
            if !path.is_empty() && std::path::Path::new(&path).exists() {
                return Some(path);
            }
        }
    }
    // Fallback: check common font paths
    for p in [
        "/run/current-system/sw/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf",
        "/run/current-system/sw/share/fonts/truetype/liberation/LiberationSans-Bold.ttf",
        "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf",
        "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/liberation/LiberationSans-Bold.ttf",
    ] {
        if std::path::Path::new(p).exists() {
            return Some(p.to_string());
        }
    }
    None
}

pub fn compose_annotations(source: &Path, annotations: &[Annotation], out: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
    let Some(bin) = magick_or_convert() else { return Err("ImageMagick not installed".into()); };
    let font_path = find_annotation_font();
    let mut args: Vec<String> = vec![source.to_str().unwrap_or("").into()];
    let color_arg = |c: Color| format!("rgba({},{},{},{})",
        (c.r*255.0) as u8, (c.g*255.0) as u8, (c.b*255.0) as u8, c.a);

    for a in annotations {
        match a.tool {
            Tool::Rect => {
                let (x1, y1) = (a.start.x as i32, a.start.y as i32);
                let (x2, y2) = (a.end.x as i32, a.end.y as i32);
                let (lx, ty) = (x1.min(x2), y1.min(y2));
                let (rx, by) = (x1.max(x2), y1.max(y2));
                args.extend([
                    "-stroke".into(), color_arg(a.color),
                    "-strokewidth".into(), format!("{}", a.width as i32),
                    "-fill".into(), "none".into(),
                    "-draw".into(), format!("rectangle {},{} {},{}", lx, ty, rx, by),
                ]);
            }
            Tool::Redact => {
                let (x1, y1) = (a.start.x as i32, a.start.y as i32);
                let (x2, y2) = (a.end.x as i32, a.end.y as i32);
                let (lx, ty) = (x1.min(x2), y1.min(y2));
                let (rx, by) = (x1.max(x2), y1.max(y2));
                args.extend(["-fill".into(), "black".into(), "-stroke".into(), "none".into(),
                    "-draw".into(), format!("rectangle {},{} {},{}", lx, ty, rx, by)]);
            }
            Tool::Arrow => {
                let (x1, y1) = (a.start.x as i32, a.start.y as i32);
                let (x2, y2) = (a.end.x as i32, a.end.y as i32);
                args.extend([
                    "-stroke".into(), color_arg(a.color),
                    "-strokewidth".into(), format!("{}", a.width as i32),
                    "-draw".into(), format!("line {},{} {},{}", x1, y1, x2, y2),
                ]);
                let ang = ((y2-y1) as f32).atan2((x2-x1) as f32);
                let hl = (a.width * 4.0).max(12.0);
                let p1x = (x2 as f32 - hl * (ang-0.5).cos()) as i32;
                let p1y = (y2 as f32 - hl * (ang-0.5).sin()) as i32;
                let p2x = (x2 as f32 - hl * (ang+0.5).cos()) as i32;
                let p2y = (y2 as f32 - hl * (ang+0.5).sin()) as i32;
                args.extend(["-fill".into(), color_arg(a.color), "-stroke".into(), "none".into(),
                    "-draw".into(), format!("polygon {},{} {},{} {},{}", x2, y2, p1x, p1y, p2x, p2y)]);
            }
            Tool::Text => {
                if a.text.is_empty() { continue; }
                if let Some(ref fp) = font_path {
                    args.extend(["-font".into(), fp.clone()]);
                }
                args.extend([
                    "-fill".into(), color_arg(a.color),
                    "-stroke".into(), "none".into(),
                    "-pointsize".into(), format!("{}", a.font_size as i32),
                    "-gravity".into(), "NorthWest".into(),
                    "-annotate".into(), format!("+{}+{}", a.start.x as i32, a.start.y as i32),
                    a.text.clone(),
                ]);
            }
            Tool::Counter => {
                let (x, y) = (a.start.x as i32, a.start.y as i32);
                let r = (a.width * 3.0).max(14.0) as i32;
                args.extend([
                    "-fill".into(), color_arg(a.color),
                    "-stroke".into(), "white".into(),
                    "-strokewidth".into(), "2".into(),
                    "-draw".into(), format!("circle {},{} {},{}", x, y, x + r, y),
                ]);
                let font_size = (r as f32 * 1.2).max(12.0);
                let text = a.number.to_string();
                let char_offset_x = (text.len() as f32 * font_size * 0.25) as i32;
                let char_offset_y = (font_size * 0.4) as i32;
                if let Some(ref fp) = font_path {
                    args.extend(["-font".into(), fp.clone()]);
                }
                args.extend([
                    "-fill".into(), "white".into(),
                    "-stroke".into(), "none".into(),
                    "-pointsize".into(), format!("{}", font_size as i32),
                    "-gravity".into(), "NorthWest".into(),
                    "-annotate".into(), format!("+{}+{}", x - char_offset_x, y - char_offset_y),
                    text,
                ]);
            }
            Tool::Ellipse => {
                let (x1, y1) = (a.start.x as i32, a.start.y as i32);
                let (x2, y2) = (a.end.x as i32, a.end.y as i32);
                let (lx, ty) = (x1.min(x2), y1.min(y2));
                let (rx, by) = (x1.max(x2), y1.max(y2));
                args.extend([
                    "-stroke".into(), color_arg(a.color),
                    "-strokewidth".into(), format!("{}", a.width as i32),
                    "-fill".into(), "none".into(),
                    "-draw".into(), format!("ellipse {},{} {},{} 0,360", (lx+rx)/2, (ty+by)/2, (rx-lx)/2, (by-ty)/2),
                ]);
            }
            Tool::Pen => {
                if a.points.len() < 2 { continue; }
                let pts: Vec<String> = a.points.iter()
                    .map(|p| format!("{},{}", p.x as i32, p.y as i32)).collect();
                args.extend([
                    "-stroke".into(), color_arg(a.color),
                    "-strokewidth".into(), format!("{}", a.width as i32),
                    "-fill".into(), "none".into(),
                    "-draw".into(), format!("polyline {}", pts.join(" ")),
                ]);
            }
            }
    }
    args.push(out.to_str().unwrap_or("").into());
    let ok = std::process::Command::new(bin).args(&args).status()
        .map(|s| s.success()).unwrap_or(false);
    if !ok { return Err(format!("{bin} failed")); }
    Ok(())
}

pub fn save_annotated_image(
    source: PathBuf, annotations: Vec<Annotation>, watermark: Option<String>,
) -> Result<Vec<PathBuf>, String> {
    let save_dir = dirs::picture_dir()
        .or_else(|| dirs::home_dir().map(|h| h.join("Pictures")))
        .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/tmp"))
        .join("Screenshots");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&save_dir).map_err(|e| format!("mkdir: {e}"))?;
    
    let stamp = chrono::Local::now().format("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S").to_string();
    let time_str = chrono::Local::now().format("%H-%M").to_string();

    let composed_bytes = if annotations.is_empty() {
        std::fs::read(&source).map_err(|e| format!("read source: {e}"))?
    } else {
        let composed_tmp = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("mbs-composed-{}.png", stamp));
        compose_annotations(&source, &annotations, &composed_tmp)?;
        let bytes = std::fs::read(&composed_tmp).map_err(|e| format!("read composed: {e}"))?;
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&composed_tmp);
        bytes
    };

    let mut produced = Vec::new();
    
    let date_str = chrono::Local::now().format("%Y-%m-%d").to_string();
    let names: Vec<String> = watermark
        .as_deref()
        .unwrap_or("")
        .split(',')
        .map(|s| s.trim())
        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
        .map(|s| {
            if !s.contains(&date_str) {
                format!("{} {}", s, date_str)
            } else {
                s.to_string()
            }
        })
        .collect();

    let final_names = if names.is_empty() {
        vec!["Screenshot".to_string()]
    } else {
        names
    };

    for (i, name) in final_names.iter().enumerate() {
        // Stagger multiple saves so cliphist/wl-copy have time to register them
        if i > 0 {
            std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(5));
        }

        let safe_name: String = name.chars()
            .map(|c| if c.is_alphanumeric() || c == ' ' || c == '-' || c == '_' { c } else { '_' })
            .collect();
        
        let out = save_dir.join(format!("{} {} {}.png", safe_name, date_str, time_str));

        match crate::overlays::plate_watermark::add_plate_watermark(&composed_bytes, name) {
            Ok(wm_bytes) => {
                std::fs::write(&out, &wm_bytes).map_err(|e| format!("write: {e}"))?;
                eprintln!("[save] plate OK, {} bytes -> {:?}", wm_bytes.len(), out);
                
                let filename = out.file_name().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy().to_string();
                
                // Get existing IDs to find the new one
                let mut existing_clip_ids = std::collections::HashSet::new();
                if let Ok(list_out) = std::process::Command::new("cliphist").args(["list"]).output() {
                    for line in String::from_utf8_lossy(&list_out.stdout).lines() {
                        if let Some((id_str, _)) = line.split_once('\t') {
                            if let Ok(id) = id_str.parse::<u64>() { existing_clip_ids.insert(id); }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Copy the UNIQUE watermarked bytes
                if let Ok(mut child) = std::process::Command::new("wl-copy")
                    .args(["-t", "image/png"])
                    .stdin(std::process::Stdio::piped())
                    .spawn()
                {
                    use std::io::Write;
                    if let Some(mut stdin) = child.stdin.take() {
                        let _ = stdin.write_all(&wm_bytes);
                    }
                    let _ = child.wait();

                    let cache_dir = dirs::cache_dir().unwrap_or_else(std::env::temp_dir);
                    let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(&cache_dir);
                    let map_path = cache_dir.join("mbs-clip-names.txt");

                    let mut found = false;
                    for _ in 0..20 {
                        std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(50));
                        if let Ok(list_out) = std::process::Command::new("cliphist").args(["list"]).output() {
                            let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&list_out.stdout);
                            for line in text.lines() {
                                if let Some((id_str, _)) = line.split_once('\t') {
                                    if let Ok(id) = id_str.trim().parse::<u64>() {
                                        if !existing_clip_ids.contains(&id) {
                                            let entry = format!("{}\t{}\n", id, filename);
                                            if let Ok(mut f) = std::fs::OpenOptions::new().create(true).append(true).open(&map_path) {
                                                let _ = f.write_all(entry.as_bytes());
                                                eprintln!("[save] Mapped ID {} to {}", id, filename);
                                            }
                                            found = true;
                                            break;
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        if found { break; }
                    }
                }
                produced.push(out);
            }
            Err(e) => eprintln!("[save] plate FAILED: {e}"),
        }
    }

    Ok(produced)
}

// ── slurp / grim ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

pub fn slurp_region() -> Option<String> {
    let out = std::process::Command::new("slurp").output().ok()?;
    if !out.status.success() { return None; }
    let s = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).trim().to_string();
    if s.is_empty() { None } else { Some(s) }
}

pub fn grim_capture(region: Option<&str>) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    let rt = std::env::var("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR").unwrap_or_else(|_| "/tmp".into());
    let dir = PathBuf::from(rt).join("mbs-annotator");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).map_err(|e| format!("mkdir: {e}"))?;
    let path = dir.join(format!("shot_{}.png",
        chrono::Local::now().format("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")));
    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new("grim");
    if let Some(r) = region { cmd.args(["-g", r]); }
    cmd.arg(&path);
    let ok = cmd.status().map(|s| s.success()).unwrap_or(false);
    if !ok { return Err("grim failed".into()); }
    Ok(path)
}

pub fn capture_fullscreen_to_temp() -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    grim_capture(None)
}

=== FILE: ./src/overlays/launcher.rs ===
use iced::widget::{button, column, container, row, scrollable, text, text_input, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length, Padding};
use std::fs;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use tokio::process::Command;

use crate::config::settings::ShellSettings;
use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType};
use crate::style::{shell_font, TextExt};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct AppEntry {
    pub name: String,
    pub exec: String,
    pub icon: String,
    pub icon_path: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum LauncherMessage {
    SearchChanged(String),
    ExecuteApp(String),
    Dismiss,
}

pub const LAUNCHER_SEARCH_ID: &str = "launcher_search";

pub struct AppLauncher {
    pub search_query: String,
    pub all_apps: Vec<AppEntry>,
    pub filtered_apps: Vec<AppEntry>,
    pub focus_requested: bool,
}

impl Default for AppLauncher {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl AppLauncher {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        let mut all_apps = vec![
            AppEntry { name: "Settings & Options".into(), exec: "internal:settings".into(), icon: "⚙".into(), icon_path: None },
            AppEntry { name: "Calculator & Converter".into(), exec: "internal:calc".into(), icon: "🧮".into(), icon_path: None },
            AppEntry { name: "Clipboard Manager".into(), exec: "internal:clip".into(), icon: "📋".into(), icon_path: None },
            AppEntry { name: "Quick Notes".into(), exec: "internal:notes".into(), icon: "📝".into(), icon_path: None },
            AppEntry { name: "Password Store".into(), exec: "internal:pass".into(), icon: "🔑".into(), icon_path: None },
            AppEntry { name: "Power & Session".into(), exec: "internal:power".into(), icon: "⚡".into(), icon_path: None },
            AppEntry { name: "Tasks & Todos".into(), exec: "internal:todo".into(), icon: "✅".into(), icon_path: None },
            AppEntry { name: "Reminders & Alarms".into(), exec: "internal:reminders".into(), icon: "⏰".into(), icon_path: None },
            AppEntry { name: "Dictionary Lookup".into(), exec: "internal:dict".into(), icon: "📖".into(), icon_path: None },
            AppEntry { name: "Unicode Glyphs".into(), exec: "internal:unicode".into(), icon: "🔣".into(), icon_path: None },
            AppEntry { name: "Web Search".into(), exec: "internal:web".into(), icon: "🌐".into(), icon_path: None },
            AppEntry { name: "Screenshot (Quickshot)".into(), exec: "moon-beauty-shell --screenshot --annotate".into(), icon: "📷".into(), icon_path: None },
        ];
        all_apps.extend(Self::scan_desktop_files());

        Self {
            search_query: String::new(),
            filtered_apps: all_apps.clone(),
            all_apps,
            focus_requested: true,
        }
    }

    pub fn scan_desktop_files() -> Vec<AppEntry> {
        let mut apps = Vec::new();
        let home = dirs::home_dir().unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/tmp"));
        let dirs = [
            PathBuf::from("/usr/share/applications"),
            PathBuf::from("/usr/local/share/applications"),
            home.join(".local/share/applications"),
            PathBuf::from("/run/current-system/sw/share/applications"),
            home.join(".nix-profile/share/applications"),
        ];

        let mut seen = std::collections::HashSet::new();
        let mut total_files = 0usize;
        let mut hidden = 0usize;

        for dir in &dirs {
            if let Ok(entries) = fs::read_dir(dir) {
                for entry in entries.flatten() {
                    let path = entry.path();
                    if path.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) == Some("desktop") {
                        total_files += 1;
                        if let Ok(content) = fs::read_to_string(&path) {
                            let mut name = None;
                            let mut exec = None;
                            let mut icon_name: Option<String> = None;
                            let mut nodisplay = false;

                            for line in content.lines() {
                                if line.starts_with("Name=") && name.is_none() {
                                    name = Some(line["Name=".len()..].trim().to_string());
                                } else if line.starts_with("Exec=") && exec.is_none() {
                                    let raw_exec = line["Exec=".len()..].trim();
                                    let cleaned = raw_exec
                                        .split('%')
                                        .next()
                                        .unwrap_or(raw_exec)
                                        .trim()
                                        .to_string();
                                    exec = Some(cleaned);
                                } else if line.starts_with("Icon=") && icon_name.is_none() {
                                    icon_name = Some(line["Icon=".len()..].trim().to_string());
                                } else if line.eq_ignore_ascii_case("NoDisplay=true") {
                                    nodisplay = true;
                                }
                            }

                            if nodisplay { hidden += 1; continue; }
                            if let (Some(n), Some(e)) = (name, exec) {
                                let key = format!("{}|{}", n.to_lowercase(), e.to_lowercase());
                                if !seen.contains(&key) {
                                    seen.insert(key);
                                    let icon_path = icon_name
                                        .as_deref()
                                        .and_then(Self::resolve_icon);
                                    apps.push(AppEntry {
                                        name: n,
                                        exec: e,
                                        icon: "🚀".to_string(),
                                        icon_path,
                                    });
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        apps.sort_by_key(|a| a.name.to_lowercase());
        let with_icons = apps.iter().filter(|a| a.icon_path.is_some()).count();
        eprintln!(
            "[launcher] {} .desktop files scanned ({} hidden), {} apps loaded, {} with icons",
            total_files, hidden, apps.len(), with_icons,
        );
        apps
    }

    fn resolve_icon(icon_name: &str) -> Option<String> {
        use std::path::Path;
        if icon_name.is_empty() { return None; }
        if icon_name.starts_with('/') {
            return if Path::new(icon_name).exists() {
                Some(icon_name.to_string())
            } else { None };
        }
        let home = dirs::home_dir().unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/tmp"));
        let roots = [
            PathBuf::from("/run/current-system/sw/share/icons/hicolor"),
            PathBuf::from("/usr/share/icons/hicolor"),
            PathBuf::from("/usr/local/share/icons/hicolor"),
            home.join(".local/share/icons/hicolor"),
            home.join(".nix-profile/share/icons/hicolor"),
        ];
        let sizes = ["48x48", "64x64", "128x128", "256x256", "512x512", "scalable", "symbolic"];
        let exts = ["png", "svg", "xpm"];
        for root in &roots {
            for size in &sizes {
                for ext in &exts {
                    let p = root.join(size).join("apps")
                        .join(format!("{}.{}", icon_name, ext));
                    if p.exists() {
                        return Some(p.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        for pix in ["/run/current-system/sw/share/pixmaps", "/usr/share/pixmaps"] {
            for ext in &exts {
                let p = PathBuf::from(pix).join(format!("{}.{}", icon_name, ext));
                if p.exists() {
                    return Some(p.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
                }
            }
        }
        None
    }

    pub fn update(&mut self, message: LauncherMessage) -> iced::Task<LauncherMessage> {
        if let LauncherMessage::SearchChanged(q) = message {
            eprintln!("[launcher-input] {:?}", q);
            self.search_query = q.clone();
            let lower = q.trim().to_lowercase();
            if lower.is_empty() {
                self.filtered_apps = self.all_apps.clone();
            } else {
                let mut filtered: Vec<AppEntry> = self
                    .all_apps
                    .iter()
                    .filter(|a| {
                        a.name.to_lowercase().contains(&lower)
                            || a.exec.to_lowercase().contains(&lower)
                    })
                    .cloned()
                    .collect();
                if let Some(alias) = Self::routing_alias(&lower) {
                    if !filtered.iter().any(|a| a.exec == alias.exec) {
                        filtered.insert(0, alias);
                    }
                }
                self.filtered_apps = filtered;
            }
        }
        iced::Task::none()
    }

    fn routing_alias(query: &str) -> Option<AppEntry> {
        let (name, exec) = match query {
            "cfg" | "set" | "settings" => ("Settings & Options", "internal:settings"),
            "calc" | "calculator" => ("Calculator", "internal:calc"),
            "clip" | "clipboard" => ("Clipboard Manager", "internal:clip"),
            "todo" | "td" => ("Tasks & Todos", "internal:todo"),
            "note" | "notes" => ("Quick Notes", "internal:notes"),
            "pass" | "password" => ("Password Store", "internal:pass"),
            "power" | "pwr" => ("Power & Session", "internal:power"),
            "remind" | "reminder" | "reminders" => ("Reminders & Alarms", "internal:reminders"),
            "dict" | "def" | "dictionary" => ("Dictionary", "internal:dict"),
            "unicode" => ("Unicode", "internal:unicode"),
            "email" | "mail" | "em" => ("Email", "internal:email"),
            "rng" | "dice" | "roll" => ("RNG / Dice", "internal:rng"),
            "web" | "search" => ("Web Search", "internal:web"),
            "amogus" | "amongus" | "sus" => ("Among Us", "internal:amogus"),
            _ => return None,
        };
        Some(AppEntry { name: name.into(), exec: exec.into(), icon: "⚙".into(), icon_path: None })
    }

    pub fn focus_search(&mut self) -> iced::Task<LauncherMessage> {
        iced::widget::text_input::focus(LAUNCHER_SEARCH_ID)
    }

    pub async fn launch(exec: &str) {
        if exec.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        let _ = Command::new("systemd-run")
            .args(["--user", "--collect", "--quiet", "--slice=app.slice", "sh", "-c", exec])
            .spawn();
    }

    pub fn view<'a>(&'a self, theme: &'a ShellTheme, settings: &ShellSettings) -> Element<'a, LauncherMessage> {
        let (win_w, win_h) = settings.get_window_size("launcher", 840, 650);

        let cut_f = theme.overlay_cut;
        let angle_deg = theme.overlay_angle;
        let rad = angle_deg.to_radians();
        let sqrt2 = std::f32::consts::SQRT_2;
        let dx = (cut_f * rad.cos() * sqrt2).min(win_w * 0.45).max(0.0);
        let dy = (cut_f * rad.sin() * sqrt2).min(win_h * 0.45).max(0.0);

        let pad_top = 18.0_f32;
        let pad_bottom = 16.0_f32;

        // Inward horizontal clearance for the search bar (y ≈ 18px)
        let search_inward = if dy > pad_top {
            (dx * (1.0 - (pad_top / dy))).max(0.0) + 16.0
        } else {
            16.0
        };

        // Inward horizontal clearance for the scrollable list items (y_top ≈ 72px, y_bottom ≈ win_h - 45px)
        let list_top_inset = if dy > 72.0 {
            (dx * (1.0 - (72.0 / dy))).max(0.0) + 16.0
        } else {
            16.0
        };
        let list_bottom_inset = if dy > 45.0 {
            (dx * (1.0 - (45.0 / dy))).max(0.0) + 16.0
        } else {
            16.0
        };
        let list_side_inset = list_top_inset.max(list_bottom_inset).max(20.0);

        // Inward clearance for bottom footer
        let footer_inward = if dy > pad_bottom + 8.0 {
            (dx * (1.0 - ((pad_bottom + 8.0) / dy))).max(0.0) + 16.0
        } else {
            16.0
        };

        let input = text_input("Search applications or settings...", &self.search_query)
            .id(LAUNCHER_SEARCH_ID)
            .font(shell_font(iced::font::Weight::Normal))
            .on_input(LauncherMessage::SearchChanged)
            .on_submit(
                if let Some(first) = self.filtered_apps.first() {
                    LauncherMessage::ExecuteApp(first.exec.clone())
                } else {
                    LauncherMessage::ExecuteApp(self.search_query.clone())
                },
            )
            .padding(14)
            .size(theme.font_size_overlay)
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .style(move |_t, status| {
                text_input::Style {
                    background: iced::Background::Color(theme.surface),
                    border: iced::Border {
                        color: match status {
                            text_input::Status::Focused => theme.focus,
                            text_input::Status::Hovered => theme.muted,
                            _ => theme.border,
                        },
                        width: theme.control_border_width,
                        radius: 8.0.into(),
                    },
                    icon: theme.muted,
                    placeholder: theme.muted,
                    value: theme.text,
                    selection: theme.surface_alt,
                }
            });

        let input_row = container(input)
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .padding(Padding {
                top: 0.0,
                right: search_inward,
                bottom: 0.0,
                left: search_inward,
            });

        let mut list_col = column![].spacing(6).width(Length::Fill);
        for (idx, app) in self.filtered_apps.iter().take(40).enumerate() {
            let exec_cmd = app.exec.clone();
            let icon_str = if app.exec.starts_with("internal:") { "⚙" } else { "🚀" };
            let icon_elem: Element<'_, LauncherMessage> = if let Some(ref path) = app.icon_path {
                iced::widget::image(iced::widget::image::Handle::from_path(path.clone()))
                    .width(Length::Fixed(24.0))
                    .height(Length::Fixed(24.0))
                    .into()
            } else {
                text(icon_str).size(16).into()
            };

            let is_first = idx == 0;
            let row_item = button(
                row![
                    icon_elem,
                    column![
                        text(&app.name)
                            .size(theme.font_size_overlay)
                            .bold(),
                        text(&app.exec)
                            .size(theme.font_size_normal.saturating_sub(2))
                            .shell()
                            .color(theme.muted),
                    ]
                    .spacing(2)
                    .width(Length::Fill),
                ]
                .spacing(12)
                .align_y(Alignment::Center),
            )
            .on_press(LauncherMessage::ExecuteApp(exec_cmd))
            .padding([8, 14])
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .style(move |_t, status| {
                let is_hovered = matches!(status, button::Status::Hovered | button::Status::Pressed);
                let bg = if is_hovered {
                    Some(iced::Background::Color(theme.surface_alt))
                } else if is_first {
                    Some(iced::Background::Color(theme.surface))
                } else {
                    None
                };
                let border_color = if is_hovered {
                    theme.focus
                } else if is_first {
                    theme.border
                } else {
                    Color::TRANSPARENT
                };

                button::Style {
                    background: bg,
                    border: iced::Border {
                        color: border_color,
                        width: if is_hovered || is_first {
                            theme.control_border_width
                        } else {
                            0.0
                        },
                        radius: 6.0.into(),
                    },
                    text_color: if is_hovered {
                        theme.text_strong
                    } else {
                        theme.text
                    },
                    ..Default::default()
                }
            });

            list_col = list_col.push(row_item);
        }

        let list_container = container(scrollable(list_col).height(Length::Fill).width(Length::Fill))
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .height(Length::Fill)
            .padding(Padding {
                top: 0.0,
                right: list_side_inset,
                bottom: 0.0,
                left: list_side_inset,
            });

        let bottom_footer = container(
            text("ESC to dismiss  •  Enter to open")
                .size(11)
                .shell()
                .color(theme.muted)
                .align_x(Alignment::Center),
        )
        .width(Length::Fill)
        .padding(Padding {
            top: 0.0,
            right: footer_inward,
            bottom: 0.0,
            left: footer_inward,
        });

        let card_bg = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::AngledHexagon { cut: cut_f, angle_deg })
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.border, theme.border_width)
            .size(Length::Fixed(win_w), Length::Fixed(win_h));

        let card_content = container(
            column![
                input_row,
                list_container,
                bottom_footer,
            ]
            .spacing(12)
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .height(Length::Fill),
        )
        .padding(Padding {
            top: pad_top,
            right: 0.0,
            bottom: pad_bottom,
            left: 0.0,
        })
        .width(Length::Fixed(win_w))
        .height(Length::Fixed(win_h));

        let card = Stack::new().push(card_bg.view()).push(card_content);

        let backdrop = button(container(column![]).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fill))
            .on_press(LauncherMessage::Dismiss)
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::from_rgba8(0, 0, 0, 0.65))),
                ..Default::default()
            });

        Stack::new()
            .push(backdrop)
            .push(container(card).center_x(Length::Fill).center_y(Length::Fill))
            .into()
    }
}

=== FILE: ./src/overlays/weather_tooltip.rs ===
use iced::widget::{column, row, text, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length, Padding, Task, Theme};
use iced_layershell::reexport::{Anchor, KeyboardInteractivity, Layer};
use iced_layershell::settings::{LayerShellSettings, Settings};
use iced_layershell::Application;
use std::time::Instant;

use crate::backend::weather::{WeatherAlert, WeatherData};
use crate::backend::WeatherBackend;
use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::config::ShellSettings;
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType, SlantSide};

const W: u32 = 340;
const H: u32 = 220;
const W_START: f32 = 130.0;
const AUTO_MS: u128 = 10_000;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum Message { Tick, Loaded(Option<WeatherData>), Dismiss }

impl TryInto<iced_layershell::actions::LayershellCustomActionsWithInfo<()>> for Message {
    type Error = Message;
    fn try_into(self) -> Result<iced_layershell::actions::LayershellCustomActionsWithInfo<()>, Self::Error> {
        Err(self)
    }
}

pub struct App {
    opened: Instant,
    weather: Option<WeatherData>,
    fetched: bool,
    slant_mode: String,
}

impl Application for App {
    type Executor = iced::executor::Default;
    type Message = Message;
    type Theme = Theme;
    type Flags = ();

    fn new(_: ()) -> (Self, Task<Message>) {

        let slant_mode = std::env::var("MBS_WX_SLANT").unwrap_or_else(|_| "left".into());

        let app = Self {
            opened: Instant::now(),
            weather: None,
            fetched: false,
            slant_mode,
        };
        let task = Task::perform(
            async { WeatherBackend::fetch().await.ok() },
            Message::Loaded,
        );
        (app, task)
    }

    fn namespace(&self) -> String { "mbs-wx-tip".into() }

    fn style(&self, _theme: &Self::Theme) -> iced_layershell::Appearance {
        iced_layershell::Appearance {
            background_color: Color::TRANSPARENT,
            text_color: Color::WHITE,
        }
    }

    fn update(&mut self, m: Message) -> Task<Message> {
        match m {
            Message::Tick => {
                if self.opened.elapsed().as_millis() > AUTO_MS { std::process::exit(0); }
            }
            Message::Loaded(w) => { self.weather = w; self.fetched = true; }
            Message::Dismiss => std::process::exit(0),
        }
        Task::none()
    }

    fn view(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let settings = ShellSettings::load();
        let theme = ShellTheme::from_settings(&settings);

        let anim_w = W as f32;
        let anim_h = H as f32;

        let slant = (theme.slant_width * (anim_h / settings.bar_height as f32)).max(0.0);
        let (sl, sr) = match self.slant_mode.as_str() {
            "right" => (SlantSide::Right, SlantSide::Right),
            "center" => (SlantSide::Left, SlantSide::Right),
            _ => (SlantSide::Left, SlantSide::Left),
        };

        // Match weather alert level
        let border_color = match &self.weather {
            Some(w) => match &w.alert {
                WeatherAlert::Danger(_) => theme.danger,
                WeatherAlert::Upcoming(_) => theme.warning,
                WeatherAlert::None => theme.text,
            },
            None => theme.text,
        };

        let bg_shape = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Slant { left: sl, right: sr })
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(border_color, theme.control_border_width)
            .slant_width(slant)
            .size(Length::Fixed(anim_w), Length::Fixed(anim_h));

        let content = self.build_content(&theme);

        Stack::new()
            .push(bg_shape.view())
            .push(content)
            .into()
    }

    fn subscription(&self) -> iced::Subscription<Message> {
        iced::Subscription::batch([
            iced::time::every(std::time::Duration::from_millis(16)).map(|_| Message::Tick),
            iced::keyboard::on_key_press(|k, _| {
                if k == iced::keyboard::Key::Named(iced::keyboard::key::Named::Escape) {
                    Some(Message::Dismiss)
                } else { None }
            }),
        ])
    }

    fn theme(&self) -> Theme { Theme::Dark }
}

impl App {
    fn build_content(&self, t: &ShellTheme) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let (fg, muted, warn, danger) = (t.text, t.muted, t.warning, t.danger);
        let pad_x = (t.slant_width * 2.0 + 12.0).max(28.0);
        let pad = Padding { top: 20.0, right: pad_x, bottom: 20.0, left: pad_x };

        if let Some(wx) = &self.weather {
            let alert: Element<Message> = match &wx.alert {
                WeatherAlert::Danger(s) => text(s.clone()).size(13).color(danger).into(),
                WeatherAlert::Upcoming(s) => text(s.clone()).size(13).color(warn).into(),
                WeatherAlert::None => text("").size(0).into(),
            };
            let mut hrs = row![].spacing(14).align_y(Alignment::Center);
            for hh in wx.hourly.iter().take(6) {
                hrs = hrs.push(
                    column![
                        text(&hh.time_label).size(11).color(muted),
                        text(hh.icon).size(20),
                        text(format!("{}°", hh.temp_f)).size(12).color(fg),
                    ].spacing(2).align_x(Alignment::Center),
                );
            }
            column![
                row![
                    text(wx.icon).size(40),
                    column![
                        text(format!("{}°F", wx.temp_f)).size(28).color(fg),
                        text(format!("{}°C", wx.temp_c)).size(14).color(muted),
                    ].spacing(0),
                    text(wx.desc).size(16).color(fg).width(Length::Fill),
                ].spacing(16).align_y(Alignment::Center),
                alert,
                hrs,
            ].spacing(14).padding(pad)
             .width(Length::Fixed(W as f32))
             .height(Length::Fixed(H as f32))
             .into()
        } else if self.fetched {
            column![text("Weather unavailable").size(16).color(muted)]
                .spacing(8).padding(pad)
                .width(Length::Fixed(W as f32))
                .height(Length::Fixed(H as f32))
                .into()
        } else {
            column![text("Loading weather…").size(16).color(muted)]
                .spacing(8).padding(pad)
                .width(Length::Fixed(W as f32))
                .height(Length::Fixed(H as f32))
                .into()
        }
    }
}

pub fn run() -> Result<(), iced_layershell::Error> {
    let s = ShellSettings::load();
    let cx: i32 = std::env::var("MBS_CAPSULE_CX")
        .ok().and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(200);
    let sw: i32 = std::env::var("MBS_SCREEN_W")
        .ok().and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(1920);
    let grow_right = cx < sw / 2;

    let bar_h = s.bar_height as i32;
    let half_start = (W_START / 2.0) as i32;

    let (anchor, margin) = if grow_right {
        (
            Anchor::Top | Anchor::Left,
            (bar_h, 0, 0, (cx - half_start).max(0)),
        )
    } else {
        (
            Anchor::Top | Anchor::Right,
            (bar_h, (sw - cx - half_start).max(0), 0, 0),
        )
    };

    let layer = LayerShellSettings {
        size: Some((W, H)),
        exclusive_zone: -1,
        anchor,
        layer: Layer::Overlay,
        keyboard_interactivity: KeyboardInteractivity::OnDemand,
        margin,
        ..Default::default()
    };
    App::run(Settings { layer_settings: layer, ..Default::default() })
}
