=== FILE: ./src/overlays/system_tooltip.rs ===
use crate::style::TextExt;
use iced::widget::{button, column, row, text, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length, Padding, Task, Theme};
use iced_layershell::reexport::{Anchor, KeyboardInteractivity, Layer};
use iced_layershell::settings::{LayerShellSettings, Settings};
use iced_layershell::Application;
use std::time::Instant;

use crate::backend::system::{SystemCollector, ProcessItem};
use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::config::ShellSettings;
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType, SlantSide};

const W: u32 = 340;
const H: u32 = 240;
const W_START: f32 = 130.0;
const AUTO_MS: u128 = 10_000;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum Data {
    Cpu(crate::backend::system::CpuMetrics),
    Gpu(crate::backend::system::GpuMetrics),
    Ram(crate::backend::system::RamMetrics),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum Message {
    Tick,
    Loaded(Data),
    Dismiss,
}

impl TryInto<iced_layershell::actions::LayershellCustomActionsWithInfo<()>> for Message {
    type Error = Message;
    fn try_into(self) -> Result<iced_layershell::actions::LayershellCustomActionsWithInfo<()>, Self::Error> {
        Err(self)
    }
}

pub struct App {
    opened: Instant,
    target: String,
    data: Option<Data>,
    slant_mode: String,
}

impl Application for App {
    type Executor = iced::executor::Default;
    type Message = Message;
    type Theme = Theme;
    type Flags = ();

    fn new(_: ()) -> (Self, Task<Message>) {
        let target = std::env::var("MBS_SYS_TARGET").unwrap_or_else(|_| "cpu".into());
        let slant_mode = std::env::var("MBS_WX_SLANT").unwrap_or_else(|_| "left".into());

        let app = Self {
            opened: Instant::now(),
            target: target.clone(),
            data: None,
            slant_mode,
        };

        let task = if target == "cpu" {
            Task::perform(SystemCollector::collect_cpu(), |d| Message::Loaded(Data::Cpu(d)))
        } else if target == "gpu" {
            Task::perform(SystemCollector::collect_gpu("card0", false, 0), |d| Message::Loaded(Data::Gpu(d)))
        } else {
            Task::perform(SystemCollector::collect_ram(), |d| Message::Loaded(Data::Ram(d)))
        };

        (app, task)
    }

    fn namespace(&self) -> String { format!("mbs-sys-tip-{}", self.target) }

    fn style(&self, _theme: &Self::Theme) -> iced_layershell::Appearance {
        iced_layershell::Appearance {
            background_color: Color::TRANSPARENT,
            text_color: Color::WHITE,
        }
    }

    fn update(&mut self, m: Message) -> Task<Message> {
        match m {
            Message::Tick => {
                if self.opened.elapsed().as_millis() > AUTO_MS { std::process::exit(0); }
            }
            Message::Loaded(d) => { self.data = Some(d); }
            Message::Dismiss => std::process::exit(0),
        }
        Task::none()
    }

    fn view(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let settings = ShellSettings::load();
        let theme = ShellTheme::from_settings(&settings);

        let anim_w = W as f32;
        let anim_h = H as f32;

        let slant = (theme.slant_width * (anim_h / settings.bar_height as f32)).max(0.0);
        let (sl, sr) = match self.slant_mode.as_str() {
            "right" => (SlantSide::Right, SlantSide::Right),
            "center" => (SlantSide::Left, SlantSide::Right),
            _ => (SlantSide::Left, SlantSide::Left),
        };

        // Match the bar module's dynamic alert color
        let border_color = match &self.data {
            Some(Data::Cpu(c)) => {
                if c.temp_c >= 80 || c.usage_pct >= 90 {
                    theme.danger
                } else if c.temp_c >= 70 || c.usage_pct >= 75 {
                    theme.warning
                } else {
                    theme.text
                }
            }
            Some(Data::Gpu(g)) => {
                if g.temp_c >= theme.gpu0_temp_danger || g.vram_free_gib <= theme.gpu0_vram_danger {
                    theme.danger
                } else if g.temp_c >= theme.gpu0_temp_warn || g.vram_free_gib <= theme.gpu0_vram_warn {
                    theme.warning
                } else {
                    theme.text
                }
            }
            Some(Data::Ram(r)) => {
                let total = r.total_gib;
                let avail = r.effective_avail_gib;
                let usage_ratio = if total > 0.0 { ((total - avail).max(0.0)) / total } else { 0.0 };
                if usage_ratio >= 0.85 {
                    theme.danger
                } else if usage_ratio >= 0.50 {
                    theme.warning
                } else {
                    theme.text
                }
            }
            None => theme.text,
        };

        let bg_shape = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Slant { left: sl, right: sr })
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(border_color, theme.control_border_width)
            .slant_width(slant)
            .size(Length::Fixed(anim_w), Length::Fixed(anim_h));

        let content = self.build_content(&theme);

        Stack::new()
            .push(bg_shape.view())
            .push(content)
            .into()
    }

    fn subscription(&self) -> iced::Subscription<Message> {
        iced::Subscription::batch([
            iced::time::every(std::time::Duration::from_millis(16)).map(|_| Message::Tick),
            iced::keyboard::on_key_press(|k, _| {
                if k == iced::keyboard::Key::Named(iced::keyboard::key::Named::Escape) {
                    Some(Message::Dismiss)
                } else { None }
            }),
        ])
    }

    fn theme(&self) -> Theme { Theme::Dark }
}

impl App {
    fn build_content(&self, t: &ShellTheme) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let (fg, muted, succ) = (t.text, t.muted, t.success);
        let pad_x = (t.slant_width * 2.0 + 12.0).max(28.0);
        let pad = Padding { top: 16.0, right: pad_x, bottom: 16.0, left: pad_x };

        let close_btn = button(text("✕").size(11).color(muted))
            .on_press(Message::Dismiss)
            .padding([2, 6])
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() });

        let body: Element<Message> = if let Some(d) = &self.data {
            match d {
                Data::Cpu(c) => column![
                    row![text("CPU METRICS").size(14).bold().color(fg), row![].width(Length::Fill), close_btn].align_y(Alignment::Center),
                    text(format!("Usage: {}%", c.usage_pct)).size(15).color(succ),
                    text(format!("Temp: {}°C", c.temp_c)).size(13).color(muted),
                    Self::render_procs(&c.top_procs, t),
                ].spacing(6).into(),
                Data::Gpu(g) => column![
                    row![text(format!("GPU: {}", g.name)).size(14).bold().color(fg), row![].width(Length::Fill), close_btn].align_y(Alignment::Center),
                    text(format!("Usage: {}%", g.usage_pct)).size(15).color(succ),
                    text(format!("Temp: {}°C  •  {}W", g.temp_c, g.power_w)).size(13).color(muted),
                    text(format!("VRAM Free: {} GiB", g.vram_free_gib)).size(13).color(muted),
                    Self::render_procs(&g.top_procs, t),
                ].spacing(6).into(),
                Data::Ram(r) => column![
                    row![text("RAM USAGE").size(14).bold().color(fg), row![].width(Length::Fill), close_btn].align_y(Alignment::Center),
                    text(format!("Avail: {:.1} / {:.1} GiB", r.effective_avail_gib, r.total_gib)).size(15).color(succ),
                    text(format!("Zram Saved: {:.1} GiB", r.zram_saved_gib)).size(13).color(muted),
                    Self::render_procs(&r.top_procs, t),
                ].spacing(6).into(),
            }
        } else {
            column![
                row![text("LOADING METRICS...").size(14).color(muted), row![].width(Length::Fill), close_btn].align_y(Alignment::Center)
            ].into()
        };

        column![body].padding(pad)
            .width(Length::Fixed(W as f32))
            .height(Length::Fixed(H as f32))
            .into()
    }

    fn render_procs<'a>(procs: &'a [ProcessItem], t: &ShellTheme) -> Element<'a, Message> {
        let mut c = column![].spacing(4);
        for p in procs.iter().take(3) {
            c = c.push(
                row![
                    text(&p.name).size(12).color(t.text).width(Length::Fill),
                    text(&p.metric).size(12).color(t.muted),
                ]
            );
        }
        c.into()
    }
}

pub fn run() -> Result<(), iced_layershell::Error> {
    let s = ShellSettings::load();
    let cx: i32 = std::env::var("MBS_CAPSULE_CX").ok().and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(200);
    let sw: i32 = std::env::var("MBS_SCREEN_W").ok().and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(1920);
    let grow_right = cx < sw / 2;

    let bar_h = s.bar_height as i32;
    let half_start = (W_START / 2.0) as i32;

    let (anchor, margin) = if grow_right {
        (Anchor::Top | Anchor::Left, (bar_h, 0, 0, (cx - half_start).max(0)))
    } else {
        (Anchor::Top | Anchor::Right, (bar_h, (sw - cx - half_start).max(0), 0, 0))
    };

    let layer = LayerShellSettings {
        size: Some((W, H)),
        exclusive_zone: -1,
        anchor,
        layer: Layer::Overlay,
        keyboard_interactivity: KeyboardInteractivity::OnDemand,
        margin,
        ..Default::default()
    };
    App::run(Settings { layer_settings: layer, ..Default::default() })
}

=== FILE: ./src/overlays/tray_popup.rs ===
use iced::widget::{button, column, container, row, text, MouseArea, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length, Task, Theme};
use iced_layershell::reexport::{Anchor, KeyboardInteractivity, Layer};
use iced_layershell::settings::{LayerShellSettings, Settings};
use iced_layershell::Application;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::time::Instant;

use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::config::ShellSettings;
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType, SlantSide};

const W: u32 = 300;
const H: u32 = 380;
const W_START: f32 = 100.0;
const H_START: f32 = 48.0;
const ANIM_MS: f32 = 320.0;
const AUTO_MS: u128 = 25_000;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct TrayItem {
    pub bus: String,
    pub path: String,
    pub id: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub icon_path: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum Message {
    Tick,
    Dismiss,
    ItemsLoaded(Vec<TrayItem>),
    Activate(TrayItem),
    ContextMenu(TrayItem),
}

impl TryInto<iced_layershell::actions::LayershellCustomActionsWithInfo<()>> for Message {
    type Error = Message;
    fn try_into(self) -> Result<iced_layershell::actions::LayershellCustomActionsWithInfo<()>, Self::Error> {
        Err(self)
    }
}

pub struct App {
    opened: Instant,
    grow_right: bool,
    slant_mode: String,
    items: Vec<TrayItem>,
    loaded: bool,
}

impl Application for App {
    type Executor = iced::executor::Default;
    type Message = Message;
    type Theme = Theme;
    type Flags = ();

    fn new(_: ()) -> (Self, Task<Message>) {
        let cx: i32 = std::env::var("MBS_CAPSULE_CX").ok().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(200);
        let sw: i32 = std::env::var("MBS_SCREEN_W").ok().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(1920);
        let grow_right = cx < sw / 2;
        let slant_mode = std::env::var("MBS_WX_SLANT").unwrap_or_else(|_| "left".into());
        let app = Self {
            opened: Instant::now(),
            grow_right,
            slant_mode,
            items: Vec::new(),
            loaded: false,
        };
        let task = Task::perform(fetch_tray_items(), Message::ItemsLoaded);
        (app, task)
    }

    fn namespace(&self) -> String { "mbs-tray-tip".into() }

    fn style(&self, _theme: &Self::Theme) -> iced_layershell::Appearance {
        iced_layershell::Appearance {
            background_color: Color::TRANSPARENT,
            text_color: Color::WHITE,
        }
    }

    fn update(&mut self, m: Message) -> Task<Message> {
        match m {
            Message::Tick => {
                if self.opened.elapsed().as_millis() > AUTO_MS { std::process::exit(0); }
            }
            Message::ItemsLoaded(items) => {
                eprintln!("[tray-popup] loaded {} item(s)", items.len());
                self.items = items;
                self.loaded = true;
            }
            Message::Activate(item) => {
                eprintln!("[tray-popup] activate {}/{}", item.bus, item.path);
                std::thread::spawn(move || {
                    let rt = tokio::runtime::Builder::new_current_thread()
                        .enable_all().build().ok();
                    if let Some(rt) = rt {
                        rt.block_on(async move {
                            if let Err(e) = call_sni_method(&item.bus, &item.path, "Activate").await {
                                eprintln!("[tray-popup] Activate failed: {e}");
                            }
                        });
                    }
                });
                std::process::exit(0);
            }
            Message::ContextMenu(item) => {
                eprintln!("[tray-popup] context menu {}/{}", item.bus, item.path);
                std::thread::spawn(move || {
                    let rt = tokio::runtime::Builder::new_current_thread()
                        .enable_all().build().ok();
                    if let Some(rt) = rt {
                        rt.block_on(async move {
                            if let Err(e) = call_sni_method(&item.bus, &item.path, "ContextMenu").await {
                                eprintln!("[tray-popup] ContextMenu failed: {e}");
                            }
                        });
                    }
                });
                std::process::exit(0);
            }
            Message::Dismiss => std::process::exit(0),
        }
        Task::none()
    }

    fn view(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let t = (self.opened.elapsed().as_millis() as f32 / ANIM_MS).min(1.0);

        let w_phase = (t / 0.35).min(1.0);
        let w_ease = 1.0 - (1.0 - w_phase).powi(3);
        let anim_w = W_START + (W as f32 - W_START) * w_ease;

        let h_phase = ((t - 0.25) / 0.75).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
        let h_ease = 1.0 - (1.0 - h_phase).powi(3);
        let anim_h = H_START + (H as f32 - H_START) * h_ease;

        let settings = ShellSettings::load();
        let theme = ShellTheme::from_settings(&settings);

        let slant = (theme.slant_width * (H_START / settings.bar_height as f32)).max(0.0);
        let (sl, sr) = match self.slant_mode.as_str() {
            "right"  => (SlantSide::Right, SlantSide::Right),
            "center" => (SlantSide::Left,  SlantSide::Right),
            _        => (SlantSide::Left,  SlantSide::Left),
        };

        let bg_shape = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Slant { left: sl, right: sr })
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(theme.text, theme.control_border_width)
            .slant_width(slant)
            .size(Length::Fixed(anim_w), Length::Fixed(anim_h));

        let body: Element<Message> = if !self.loaded {
            container(text("Loading tray items…").size(13).color(theme.muted))
                .padding(16).center_x(Length::Fill).into()
        } else if self.items.is_empty() {
            column![
                text("No tray items registered").size(13).color(theme.muted),
                text("Start a tray app (Steam, CoreCtrl, music player, …)").size(11).color(theme.muted),
            ]
            .spacing(6)
            .padding(16)
            .into()
        } else {
            let _hover_bg = theme.surface_alt;
            let text_color = theme.text;
            let icon_color = theme.success;

            let mut col = column![].spacing(2).width(Length::Fill);
            for item in &self.items {
                let label = if !item.title.is_empty() { item.title.clone() } else { item.id.clone() };
                let icon_elem: Element<Message> = if let Some(path) = &item.icon_path {
                    iced::widget::image(iced::widget::image::Handle::from_path(path.clone()))
                        .width(Length::Fixed(22.0))
                        .height(Length::Fixed(22.0))
                        .into()
                } else {
                    text("●").size(18).color(icon_color).into()
                };

                let row_inner = container(
                    row![
                        icon_elem,
                        text(label).size(13).color(text_color).width(Length::Fill),
                    ]
                    .spacing(10)
                    .align_y(Alignment::Center)
                    .padding([8, 10]),
                )
                .width(Length::Fill);

                let interactive = MouseArea::new(row_inner)
                    .on_press(Message::Activate(item.clone()))
                    .on_right_press(Message::ContextMenu(item.clone()));

                col = col.push(interactive);
            }
            container(col).padding(6).into()
        };

        let clip_align = if self.grow_right {
            iced::alignment::Horizontal::Left
        } else {
            iced::alignment::Horizontal::Right
        };

        let clipped = container(body)
            .width(Length::Fixed(anim_w))
            .height(Length::Fixed(anim_h))
            .clip(true)
            .align_x(clip_align)
            .align_y(iced::alignment::Vertical::Top);

        let inner = Stack::new().push(bg_shape.view()).push(clipped);

        let positioned = container(inner)
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .height(Length::Fill)
            .align_x(clip_align)
            .align_y(iced::alignment::Vertical::Top)
            .style(|_| container::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::TRANSPARENT)),
                ..Default::default()
            });

        let catcher = button(container(row![]).width(Length::Fill).height(Length::Fill))
            .on_press(Message::Dismiss)
            .padding(0)
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .height(Length::Fill)
            .style(|_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(Color::TRANSPARENT)),
                ..Default::default()
            });

        Stack::new().push(catcher).push(positioned).into()
    }

    fn subscription(&self) -> iced::Subscription<Message> {
        iced::Subscription::batch([
            iced::time::every(std::time::Duration::from_millis(16)).map(|_| Message::Tick),
            iced::keyboard::on_key_press(|k, _| {
                if k == iced::keyboard::Key::Named(iced::keyboard::key::Named::Escape) {
                    Some(Message::Dismiss)
                } else { None }
            }),
        ])
    }

    fn theme(&self) -> Theme { Theme::Dark }
}

// ── D-Bus helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

fn parse_registered(s: &str) -> Option<(String, String)> {
    if let Some(idx) = s.find('/') {
        Some((s[..idx].to_string(), s[idx..].to_string()))
    } else if !s.is_empty() {
        Some((s.to_string(), "/StatusNotifierItem".to_string()))
    } else {
        None
    }
}

async fn fetch_tray_items() -> Vec<TrayItem> {
    let conn = match zbus::Connection::session().await {
        Ok(c) => c,
        Err(e) => { eprintln!("[tray-popup] session bus: {e}"); return Vec::new(); }
    };

    let watcher = match zbus::Proxy::new(
        &conn,
        "org.kde.StatusNotifierWatcher",
        "/StatusNotifierWatcher",
        "org.kde.StatusNotifierWatcher",
    ).await {
        Ok(p) => p,
        Err(e) => { eprintln!("[tray-popup] watcher proxy: {e}"); return Vec::new(); }
    };

    let raw_items: Vec<String> = match watcher.get_property("RegisteredStatusNotifierItems").await {
        Ok(v) => v,
        Err(e) => { eprintln!("[tray-popup] RegisteredStatusNotifierItems: {e}"); return Vec::new(); }
    };
    eprintln!("[tray-popup] registered: {:?}", raw_items);

    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for raw in raw_items {
        let Some((bus, path)) = parse_registered(&raw) else { continue };
        let proxy = match zbus::Proxy::new(&conn, bus.as_str(), path.as_str(), "org.kde.StatusNotifierItem").await {
            Ok(p) => p,
            Err(e) => { eprintln!("[tray-popup] {} proxy: {e}", raw); continue; }
        };

        let id: String = proxy.get_property("Id").await.unwrap_or_default();
        let title: String = proxy.get_property("Title").await.unwrap_or_default();
        let status: String = proxy.get_property("Status").await.unwrap_or_default();
        if status.eq_ignore_ascii_case("Passive") { continue; }

        let icon_name: String = proxy.get_property("IconName").await.unwrap_or_default();
        let mut icon_path = if !icon_name.is_empty() { resolve_icon(&icon_name) } else { None };
        if icon_path.is_none() {
            if let Ok(pixmaps) = proxy.get_property::<Vec<(i32, i32, Vec<u8>)>>("IconPixmap").await {
                if let Some((w, h, data)) = pixmaps.into_iter().max_by_key(|(w, h, _)| (*w, *h)) {
                    icon_path = save_pixmap(&id, w.max(0) as u32, h.max(0) as u32, &data);
                }
            }
        }

        eprintln!("[tray-popup]   {} -> id={:?} title={:?} icon={:?}", raw, id, title, icon_path);
        out.push(TrayItem { bus, path, id, title, icon_path });
    }
    out
}

async fn call_sni_method(bus: &str, path: &str, method: &str) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>> {
    let conn = zbus::Connection::session().await?;
    let proxy = zbus::Proxy::new(&conn, bus, path, "org.kde.StatusNotifierItem").await?;
    proxy.call_method(method, &(0i32, 0i32)).await?;
    Ok(())
}

fn resolve_icon(icon_name: &str) -> Option<String> {
    use std::path::Path;
    if icon_name.is_empty() { return None; }
    if icon_name.starts_with('/') {
        return if Path::new(icon_name).exists() { Some(icon_name.to_string()) } else { None };
    }
    let home = dirs::home_dir().unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/tmp"));
    let roots = [
        PathBuf::from("/run/current-system/sw/share/icons/hicolor"),
        PathBuf::from("/usr/share/icons/hicolor"),
        PathBuf::from("/usr/local/share/icons/hicolor"),
        home.join(".local/share/icons/hicolor"),
        home.join(".local/share/flatpak/exports/share/icons/hicolor"),
        home.join(".nix-profile/share/icons/hicolor"),
    ];
    let sizes = ["48x48", "64x64", "128x128", "256x256", "512x512", "scalable", "symbolic"];
    let exts = ["png", "svg", "xpm"];
    for root in &roots {
        for size in &sizes {
            for ext in &exts {
                let p = root.join(size).join("apps").join(format!("{}.{}", icon_name, ext));
                if p.exists() { return Some(p.to_string_lossy().into_owned()); }
            }
        }
    }
    for pix in ["/run/current-system/sw/share/pixmaps", "/usr/share/pixmaps"] {
        for ext in &exts {
            let p = PathBuf::from(pix).join(format!("{}.{}", icon_name, ext));
            if p.exists() { return Some(p.to_string_lossy().into_owned()); }
        }
    }
    None
}

fn save_pixmap(id: &str, w: u32, h: u32, data: &[u8]) -> Option<String> {
    if w == 0 || h == 0 { return None; }
    if data.len() != (w as usize * h as usize * 4) { return None; }
    let mut rgba = Vec::with_capacity(data.len());
    // SNI pixmap format is ARGB32 in network byte order: [A, R, G, B]
    for chunk in data.as_chunks::<4>().0 {
        rgba.push(chunk[1]); // R
        rgba.push(chunk[2]); // G
        rgba.push(chunk[3]); // B
        rgba.push(chunk[0]); // A
    }
    let img: image::RgbaImage = image::ImageBuffer::from_raw(w, h, rgba)?;
    let safe: String = id.chars().map(|c| if c.is_alphanumeric() { c } else { '_' }).collect();
    let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("mbs-tray-{}.png", safe));
    img.save(&path).ok()?;
    Some(path.to_string_lossy().into_owned())
}

pub fn run() -> Result<(), iced_layershell::Error> {
    let s = ShellSettings::load();
    let cx: i32 = std::env::var("MBS_CAPSULE_CX").ok().and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(200);
    let sw: i32 = std::env::var("MBS_SCREEN_W").ok().and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(1920);
    let grow_right = cx < sw / 2;

    let bar_h = s.bar_height as i32;
    let half_start = (W_START / 2.0) as i32;

    let (anchor, margin) = if grow_right {
        (Anchor::Top | Anchor::Left, (bar_h, 0, 0, (cx - half_start).max(0)))
    } else {
        (Anchor::Top | Anchor::Right, (bar_h, (sw - cx - half_start).max(0), 0, 0))
    };

    let layer = LayerShellSettings {
        size: Some((W, H)),
        exclusive_zone: -1,
        anchor,
        layer: Layer::Overlay,
        keyboard_interactivity: KeyboardInteractivity::OnDemand,
        margin,
        ..Default::default()
    };
    App::run(Settings { layer_settings: layer, ..Default::default() })
}

=== FILE: ./src/overlays/alarm_tooltip.rs ===
use iced::widget::{button, column, container, row, scrollable, text, text_input, Stack};
use iced::{Alignment, Color, Element, Length, Padding, Task, Theme};
use iced_layershell::reexport::{Anchor, KeyboardInteractivity, Layer};
use iced_layershell::settings::{LayerShellSettings, Settings};
use iced_layershell::Application;
use std::time::Instant;

use crate::config::theme::ShellTheme;
use crate::config::ShellSettings;
use crate::overlays::reminders::{save_reminders, ReminderEntry, RemindersOverlay};
use crate::style::shape_box::{ShapeBox, ShapeType, SlantSide};

const W: u32 = 380;
const H: u32 = 290;
const W_START: f32 = 130.0;
const AUTO_MS: u128 = 60_000;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum Message {
    Tick,
    TaskChanged(String),
    WhenChanged(String),
    QuickWhen(String),
    AddTimer,
    DeleteTimer(u64),
    Dismiss,
}

impl TryInto<iced_layershell::actions::LayershellCustomActionsWithInfo<()>> for Message {
    type Error = Message;
    fn try_into(self) -> Result<iced_layershell::actions::LayershellCustomActionsWithInfo<()>, Self::Error> {
        Err(self)
    }
}

pub struct App {
    opened: Instant,
    slant_mode: String,
    task_text: String,
    when_text: String,
    entries: Vec<ReminderEntry>,
}

impl Application for App {
    type Executor = iced::executor::Default;
    type Message = Message;
    type Theme = Theme;
    type Flags = ();

    fn new(_: ()) -> (Self, Task<Message>) {
        let slant_mode = std::env::var("MBS_WX_SLANT").unwrap_or_else(|_| "left".into());
        let entries = RemindersOverlay::load();

        let app = Self {
            opened: Instant::now(),
            slant_mode,
            task_text: String::new(),
            when_text: String::new(),
            entries,
        };
        (app, Task::none())
    }

    fn namespace(&self) -> String { "mbs-alarm-tip".into() }

    fn style(&self, _theme: &Self::Theme) -> iced_layershell::Appearance {
        iced_layershell::Appearance {
            background_color: Color::TRANSPARENT,
            text_color: Color::WHITE,
        }
    }

    fn update(&mut self, m: Message) -> Task<Message> {
        match m {
            Message::Tick => {
                if self.opened.elapsed().as_millis() > AUTO_MS { std::process::exit(0); }
                self.entries = RemindersOverlay::load();
            }
            Message::TaskChanged(t) => self.task_text = t,
            Message::WhenChanged(w) => self.when_text = w,
            Message::QuickWhen(w) => {
                self.when_text = w;
                return self.update(Message::AddTimer);
            }
            Message::AddTimer => {
                let w = self.when_text.trim();
                if !w.is_empty() {
                    let label = if self.task_text.trim().is_empty() { "Timer".to_string() } else { self.task_text.trim().to_string() };
                    let (epoch, str_repr) = RemindersOverlay::parse_when(w);
                    let max_id = self.entries.iter().map(|e| e.id).max().unwrap_or(0);
                    self.entries.push(ReminderEntry {
                        id: max_id + 1,
                        target_epoch: epoch,
                        text: label,
                        target_str: str_repr,
                        alerted: false,
                    });
                    save_reminders(&self.entries);
                    self.task_text.clear();
                    self.when_text.clear();
                }
            }
            Message::DeleteTimer(id) => {
                self.entries.retain(|e| e.id != id);
                save_reminders(&self.entries);
            }
            Message::Dismiss => std::process::exit(0),
        }
        Task::none()
    }

    fn view(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let settings = ShellSettings::load();
        let theme = ShellTheme::from_settings(&settings);

        let anim_w = W as f32;
        let anim_h = H as f32;

        let slant = (theme.slant_width * (anim_h / settings.bar_height as f32)).max(0.0);
        let (sl, sr) = match self.slant_mode.as_str() {
            "right" => (SlantSide::Right, SlantSide::Right),
            "center" => (SlantSide::Left, SlantSide::Right),
            _ => (SlantSide::Left, SlantSide::Left),
        };

        // Match active timer warning border
        let now = chrono::Local::now().timestamp();
        let has_active = self.entries.iter().any(|e| e.target_epoch > now);
        let border_color = if has_active { theme.warning } else { theme.text };

        let bg_shape = ShapeBox::new()
            .shape(ShapeType::Slant { left: sl, right: sr })
            .background(theme.background)
            .border(border_color, theme.control_border_width)
            .slant_width(slant)
            .size(Length::Fixed(anim_w), Length::Fixed(anim_h));

        let content = self.build_content(&theme);

        Stack::new()
            .push(bg_shape.view())
            .push(content)
            .into()
    }

    fn subscription(&self) -> iced::Subscription<Message> {
        iced::Subscription::batch([
            iced::time::every(std::time::Duration::from_millis(500)).map(|_| Message::Tick),
            iced::keyboard::on_key_press(|k, _| {
                if k == iced::keyboard::Key::Named(iced::keyboard::key::Named::Escape) {
                    Some(Message::Dismiss)
                } else { None }
            }),
        ])
    }

    fn theme(&self) -> Theme { Theme::Dark }
}

impl App {
    fn build_content<'a>(&'a self, t: &ShellTheme) -> Element<'a, Message> {
        let fg = t.text;
        let muted = t.muted;
        let success = t.success;
        let warning = t.warning;
        let danger = t.danger;
        let surface_alt = t.surface_alt;
        let border = t.border;
        let bg = t.background;

        let pad_x = (t.slant_width * 2.0 + 12.0).max(28.0);
        let pad = Padding { top: 16.0, right: pad_x, bottom: 16.0, left: pad_x };

        let header = row![
            text("⏰ TIMERS & ALARMS").size(13).color(success),
            row![].width(Length::Fill),
            button(text("✕").size(11).color(muted)).on_press(Message::Dismiss).style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() })
        ].align_y(Alignment::Center);

        let quick_btn = move |lbl: &'static str, val: &'static str| {
            button(text(lbl).size(11).color(fg))
                .on_press(Message::QuickWhen(val.into()))
                .padding([2, 6])
                .style(move |_t, _s| button::Style {
                    background: Some(iced::Background::Color(surface_alt)),
                    border: iced::Border { color: border, width: 1.0, radius: 3.0.into() },
                    ..Default::default()
                })
        };

        let quick_row = row![
            text("Quick:").size(11).color(muted),
            quick_btn("+1m", "1m"),
            quick_btn("+5m", "5m"),
            quick_btn("+15m", "15m"),
            quick_btn("+30m", "30m"),
            quick_btn("+1h", "1h"),
        ].spacing(6).align_y(Alignment::Center);

        let input_row = row![
            text_input("Note (e.g. Tea)", &self.task_text).on_input(Message::TaskChanged).padding([4, 8]).size(12).width(Length::FillPortion(1)),
            text_input("When (5m, 5pm)", &self.when_text).on_input(Message::WhenChanged).on_submit(Message::AddTimer).padding([4, 8]).size(12).width(Length::FillPortion(1)),
            button(text("Set").size(12).color(bg)).on_press(Message::AddTimer).padding([4, 10]).style(move |_t, _s| button::Style {
                background: Some(iced::Background::Color(fg)),
                border: iced::Border { radius: 4.0.into(), ..Default::default() },
                ..Default::default()
            }),
        ].spacing(6).align_y(Alignment::Center);

        let now = chrono::Local::now().timestamp();
        let mut timers_col = column![].spacing(4).width(Length::Fill);

        for e in self.entries.iter().filter(|e| e.target_epoch > now) {
            let id = e.id;
            let diff = (e.target_epoch - now).max(0);
            let rem_label = if diff >= 3600 {
                format!("{}h {}m left", diff / 3600, (diff % 3600) / 60)
            } else if diff >= 60 {
                format!("{}m {}s left", diff / 60, diff % 60)
            } else {
                format!("{}s left", diff)
            };

            let item_row = row![
                text(&e.text).size(12).color(fg).width(Length::Fill),
                text(rem_label).size(11).color(warning),
                button(text("✕").size(11).color(danger)).on_press(Message::DeleteTimer(id)).padding([1, 4]).style(|_t, _s| button::Style { background: None, ..Default::default() })
            ].spacing(8).align_y(Alignment::Center);

            timers_col = timers_col.push(item_row);
        }

        let scroll_list = container(scrollable(timers_col).height(Length::Fill)).height(Length::Fixed(120.0)).width(Length::Fill);

        column![
            header,
            quick_row,
            input_row,
            scroll_list,
        ].spacing(10).padding(pad).width(Length::Fixed(W as f32)).height(Length::Fixed(H as f32)).into()
    }
}

pub fn run() -> Result<(), iced_layershell::Error> {
    eprintln!("[alarm-tooltip-run] Starting alarm tooltip process...");
    let s = ShellSettings::load();
    let cx: i32 = std::env::var("MBS_CAPSULE_CX").ok().and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(200);
    let sw: i32 = std::env::var("MBS_SCREEN_W").ok().and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(1920);
    let grow_right = cx < sw / 2;

    let bar_h = s.bar_height as i32;
    let half_start = (W_START / 2.0) as i32;

    let (anchor, margin) = if grow_right {
        (Anchor::Top | Anchor::Left, (bar_h, 0, 0, (cx - half_start).max(0)))
    } else {
        (Anchor::Top | Anchor::Right, (bar_h, (sw - cx - half_start).max(0), 0, 0))
    };

    let layer = LayerShellSettings {
        size: Some((W, H)),
        exclusive_zone: -1,
        anchor,
        layer: Layer::Overlay,
        keyboard_interactivity: KeyboardInteractivity::OnDemand,
        margin,
        ..Default::default()
    };
    App::run(Settings { layer_settings: layer, ..Default::default() })
}

//! Freeze-frame capture via grim.

pub fn capture_fullscreen() -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
    capture_output(None)
}

pub fn capture_output(name: Option<&str>) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new("grim");
    if let Some(n) = name {
        cmd.args(["-o", n]);
    }
    cmd.arg("-");
    let output = cmd.output().map_err(|e| format!("grim spawn: {e}"))?;
    if !output.status.success() {
        let err = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
        return Err(format!("grim failed: {}", err.trim()));
    }
    Ok(output.stdout)
}

=== FILE: ./src/screenshot/selector.rs ===
use iced::widget::canvas::{Canvas, Frame, Geometry, Program};
use iced::widget::Stack;
use iced::{Color, Element, Length, Point, Rectangle, Size, Theme};
use iced_layershell::reexport::{Anchor, KeyboardInteractivity, Layer};
use iced_layershell::settings::{LayerShellSettings, Settings};
use iced_layershell::Application;
use iced_layershell::to_layer_message;
use std::path::PathBuf;

#[to_layer_message]
#[derive(Clone)]
pub enum Message {
    Press(Point),
    Drag(Point),
    Release,
    Confirm,
    Cancel,
}

impl std::fmt::Debug for Message {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str("Message")
    }
}


pub struct SelectorApp {
    handle: iced::widget::image::Handle,
    img_w: u32,
    img_h: u32,
    rgba: Vec<u8>,
    start: Option<Point>,
    current: Option<Point>,
    out_path: PathBuf,
}

impl Default for SelectorApp {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            handle: iced::widget::image::Handle::from_bytes(Vec::<u8>::new()),
            img_w: 1,
            img_h: 1,
            rgba: vec![0, 0, 0, 0],
            start: None,
            current: None,
            out_path: PathBuf::new(),
        }
    }
}


impl SelectorApp {
    fn selection(&self) -> Option<(f32, f32, f32, f32)> {
        let (s, c) = (self.start?, self.current?);
        let x0 = s.x.min(c.x);
        let y0 = s.y.min(c.y);
        let x1 = s.x.max(c.x);
        let y1 = s.y.max(c.y);
        if x1 - x0 < 4.0 || y1 - y0 < 4.0 {
            return None;
        }
        Some((x0, y0, x1 - x0, y1 - y0))
    }
}

struct SelectorCanvas {
    start: Option<Point>,
    current: Option<Point>,
}

impl Program<Message> for SelectorCanvas {
    type State = ();

    fn update(
        &self,
        _state: &mut (),
        event: iced::widget::canvas::Event,
        bounds: Rectangle,
        cursor: iced::mouse::Cursor,
    ) -> (iced::widget::canvas::event::Status, Option<Message>) {
        use iced::mouse;
        use iced::widget::canvas::event::Status;
        match event {
            iced::widget::canvas::Event::Mouse(mouse::Event::ButtonPressed(mouse::Button::Left)) => {
                if let Some(p) = cursor.position_in(bounds) {
                    return (Status::Captured, Some(Message::Press(p)));
                }
            }
            iced::widget::canvas::Event::Mouse(mouse::Event::CursorMoved { .. }) => {
                if self.start.is_some() {
                    if let Some(p) = cursor.position_in(bounds) {
                        return (Status::Captured, Some(Message::Drag(p)));
                    }
                }
            }
            iced::widget::canvas::Event::Mouse(mouse::Event::ButtonReleased(mouse::Button::Left))
                if self.start.is_some() => {
                    return (Status::Captured, Some(Message::Release));
                }
            _ => {}
        }
        (Status::Ignored, None)
    }

    fn draw(
        &self,
        _state: &(),
        renderer: &iced::Renderer,
        _theme: &Theme,
        bounds: Rectangle,
        _cursor: iced::mouse::Cursor,
    ) -> Vec<Geometry> {
        let mut frame = Frame::new(renderer, bounds.size());

        let sel = if let (Some(s), Some(c)) = (self.start, self.current) {
            let x0 = s.x.min(c.x);
            let y0 = s.y.min(c.y);
            let x1 = s.x.max(c.x);
            let y1 = s.y.max(c.y);
            Some((x0, y0, x1 - x0, y1 - y0))
        } else {
            None
        };

        let dim = Color::from_rgba(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.55);

        match sel {
            Some((sx, sy, sw, sh)) if sw > 1.0 && sh > 1.0 => {
                frame.fill_rectangle(Point::ORIGIN, Size::new(bounds.width, sy), dim);
                frame.fill_rectangle(
                    Point::new(0.0, sy + sh),
                    Size::new(bounds.width, (bounds.height - sy - sh).max(0.0)),
                    dim,
                );
                frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(0.0, sy), Size::new(sx, sh), dim);
                frame.fill_rectangle(
                    Point::new(sx + sw, sy),
                    Size::new((bounds.width - sx - sw).max(0.0), sh),
                    dim,
                );
                let border = Color::WHITE;
                let b = 1.5;
                frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(sx, sy), Size::new(sw, b), border);
                frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(sx, sy + sh - b), Size::new(sw, b), border);
                frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(sx, sy), Size::new(b, sh), border);
                frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(sx + sw - b, sy), Size::new(b, sh), border);
            }
            _ => {
                frame.fill_rectangle(Point::ORIGIN, bounds.size(), dim);
            }
        }

        vec![frame.into_geometry()]
    }
}

impl Application for SelectorApp {
    type Executor = iced::executor::Default;
    type Message = Message;
    type Theme = Theme;
    type Flags = SelectorApp;

    fn new(flags: Self::Flags) -> (Self, iced::Task<Message>) {
        (flags, iced::Task::none())
    }

    fn namespace(&self) -> String {
        "mbs-freeze-selector".to_string()
    }

    fn update(&mut self, message: Message) -> iced::Task<Message> {
        match message {
            Message::Press(p) => {
                self.start = Some(p);
                self.current = Some(p);
            }
            Message::Drag(p) => {
                if self.start.is_some() {
                    self.current = Some(p);
                }
            }
            Message::Release => {
                // Auto-confirm on mouse release (matches slurp UX). If the
                // selection is too small, stay open so the user can drag again.
                if self.selection().is_some() {
                    return self.update(Message::Confirm);
                }
            }
            Message::Confirm => {
                if let Some((x, y, w, h)) = self.selection() {
                    let src = match image::RgbaImage::from_raw(self.img_w, self.img_h, self.rgba.clone()) {
                        Some(i) => i,
                        None => {
                            eprintln!("[selector] invalid rgba buffer");
                            std::process::exit(1);
                        }
                    };
                    let xi = (x as u32).min(self.img_w.saturating_sub(1));
                    let yi = (y as u32).min(self.img_h.saturating_sub(1));
                    let wi = (w as u32).min(self.img_w - xi).max(1);
                    let hi = (h as u32).min(self.img_h - yi).max(1);
                    let cropped = image::imageops::crop_imm(&src, xi, yi, wi, hi).to_image();
                    if let Err(e) = cropped.save(&self.out_path) {
                        eprintln!("[selector] save failed: {e}");
                        std::process::exit(1);
                    }
                    std::process::exit(0);
                } else {
                    eprintln!("[selector] no selection");
                }
            }
            Message::Cancel => {
                std::process::exit(130);
            }
            _ => {}  // to_layer_message injected variants — ignore
        }
        iced::Task::none()
    }

    fn view(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let img = iced::widget::image(self.handle.clone())
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .height(Length::Fill)
            .content_fit(iced::ContentFit::Fill);

        let canvas = Canvas::new(SelectorCanvas {
            start: self.start,
            current: self.current,
        })
        .width(Length::Fill)
        .height(Length::Fill);

        Stack::new().push(img).push(canvas).into()
    }

    fn subscription(&self) -> iced::Subscription<Message> {
        iced::keyboard::on_key_press(|key, _mods| match key {
            iced::keyboard::Key::Named(iced::keyboard::key::Named::Enter) => Some(Message::Confirm),
            iced::keyboard::Key::Named(iced::keyboard::key::Named::Escape) => Some(Message::Cancel),
            _ => None,
        })
    }


}
pub fn run(input_png: PathBuf, output_png: PathBuf, binded_output: Option<String>) -> Result<(), String> {
    let bytes = std::fs::read(&input_png).map_err(|e| format!("read input: {e}"))?;
    let img = image::load_from_memory(&bytes).map_err(|e| format!("decode: {e}"))?;
    let rgba = img.to_rgba8();
    let (w, h) = rgba.dimensions();
    let rgba_raw = rgba.into_raw();
    let handle = iced::widget::image::Handle::from_bytes(bytes);

    let app = SelectorApp {
        handle,
        img_w: w,
        img_h: h,
        rgba: rgba_raw,
        start: None,
        current: None,
        out_path: output_png,
    };

    let layer = LayerShellSettings {
        size: Some((0, 0)),
        exclusive_zone: -1,
        anchor: Anchor::Top | Anchor::Bottom | Anchor::Left | Anchor::Right,
        layer: Layer::Overlay,
        keyboard_interactivity: KeyboardInteractivity::Exclusive,
        binded_output_name: binded_output,
        ..Default::default()
    };

    let settings = Settings {
        flags: app,
        layer_settings: layer,
        ..Default::default()
    };

    <SelectorApp as Application>::run(settings).map_err(|e| format!("run: {e:?}"))
}

=== FILE: ./src/screenshot/output.rs ===
use std::process::Command;

#[allow(dead_code)]
pub fn focused_output() -> Option<String> {
    let out = Command::new("swaymsg")
        .args(["-t", "get_outputs", "-r"])
        .output()
        .ok()?;
    if !out.status.success() {
        return None;
    }
    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
    let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&text).ok()?;
    v.as_array()?
        .iter()
        .find(|o| o.get("focused").and_then(|f| f.as_bool()) == Some(true))
        .and_then(|o| o.get("name"))
        .and_then(|n| n.as_str())
        .map(String::from)
}


pub fn list_all_outputs() -> Vec<(String, i32, i32, u32, u32)> {
    let out = Command::new("swaymsg")
        .args(["-t", "get_outputs", "-r"])
        .output()
        .ok()
        .and_then(|o| if o.status.success() { String::from_utf8(o.stdout).ok() } else { None });
    let Some(text) = out else { return Vec::new(); };
    let Ok(v) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&text) else { return Vec::new(); };
    v.as_array()
        .map(|arr| {
            arr.iter()
                .filter_map(|o| {
                    let name = o.get("name")?.as_str()?.to_string();
                    let rect = o.get("rect")?;
                    let x = rect.get("x")?.as_i64()? as i32;
                    let y = rect.get("y")?.as_i64()? as i32;
                    let w = rect.get("width")?.as_i64()? as u32;
                    let h = rect.get("height")?.as_i64()? as u32;
                    Some((name, x, y, w, h))
                })
                .collect()
        })
        .unwrap_or_default()
}

=== FILE: ./src/screenshot/fragment.rs ===
//! Per-monitor freeze-frame selector.
//! Each fragment displays a local crop of the full desktop image.
//! Fragments coordinate cross-monitor drags via a shared state file.

use iced::widget::canvas::{Canvas, Frame, Geometry, Program};
use iced::widget::Stack;
use iced::{Color, Element, Length, Point, Rectangle, Size, Task, Theme};
use iced_layershell::reexport::{Anchor, KeyboardInteractivity, Layer};
use iced_layershell::settings::{LayerShellSettings, Settings};
use iced_layershell::to_layer_message;
use iced_layershell::Application;
use std::io::Write;
use std::path::PathBuf;

#[to_layer_message]
#[derive(Clone)]
pub enum Message {
    Tick,
    Press(Point),
    Drag(Point),
    Release(Point),
    Escape,
}

// Manual Debug impl because the macro injects variants that don't derive Debug
impl std::fmt::Debug for Message {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str("Message")
    }
}

pub fn read_state_file(path: &std::path::Path) -> (String, i32, i32, i32, i32) {
    let Ok(content) = std::fs::read_to_string(path) else {
        return ("idle".into(), 0, 0, 0, 0);
    };
    let mut it = content.lines();
    let state = it.next().unwrap_or("idle").trim().to_string();
    let sx = it.next().and_then(|s| s.trim().parse::<i32>().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
    let sy = it.next().and_then(|s| s.trim().parse::<i32>().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
    let cx = it.next().and_then(|s| s.trim().parse::<i32>().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
    let cy = it.next().and_then(|s| s.trim().parse::<i32>().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
    (state, sx, sy, cx, cy)
}

pub fn write_state_file(path: &std::path::Path, state: &str, sx: i32, sy: i32, cx: i32, cy: i32) {
    let tmp = path.with_extension("tmp");
    if let Ok(mut f) = std::fs::File::create(&tmp) {
        let _ = write!(f, "{}\n{}\n{}\n{}\n{}\n", state, sx, sy, cx, cy);
        let _ = f.sync_all();
    }
    let _ = std::fs::rename(&tmp, path);
}

pub struct FragmentApp {
    monitor: String,
    gx: i32,
    gy: i32,
    handle: iced::widget::image::Handle,
    state_file: PathBuf,
    is_initiator: bool,
    render_rect: Option<Rectangle>,
}

impl Default for FragmentApp {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            monitor: String::new(),
            gx: 0,
            gy: 0,
            handle: iced::widget::image::Handle::from_bytes(Vec::<u8>::new()),
            state_file: PathBuf::new(),
            is_initiator: false,
            render_rect: None,
        }
    }
}

impl Application for FragmentApp {
    type Executor = iced::executor::Default;
    type Message = Message;
    type Theme = Theme;
    type Flags = FragmentApp;

    fn new(flags: Self::Flags) -> (Self, Task<Message>) {
        (flags, Task::none())
    }

    fn namespace(&self) -> String {
        format!("mbs-freeze-frag-{}", self.monitor)
    }

    fn update(&mut self, message: Message) -> Task<Message> {
        match message {
            Message::Tick => {
                let (state, sx, sy, cx, cy) = read_state_file(&self.state_file);
                match state.as_str() {
                    "done" => std::process::exit(0),
                    "cancelled" => std::process::exit(130),
                    "dragging" => {
                        let lx0 = (sx - self.gx) as f32;
                        let ly0 = (sy - self.gy) as f32;
                        let lx1 = (cx - self.gx) as f32;
                        let ly1 = (cy - self.gy) as f32;
                        self.render_rect = Some(Rectangle {
                            x: lx0.min(lx1),
                            y: ly0.min(ly1),
                            width: (lx1 - lx0).abs(),
                            height: (ly1 - ly0).abs(),
                        });
                    }
                    _ => {
                        self.render_rect = None;
                    }
                }
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::Press(local) => {
                let (state, _, _, _, _) = read_state_file(&self.state_file);
                if state == "idle" {
                    self.is_initiator = true;
                    let gx = self.gx + local.x as i32;
                    let gy = self.gy + local.y as i32;
                    write_state_file(&self.state_file, "dragging", gx, gy, gx, gy);
                }
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::Drag(local) => {
                if !self.is_initiator {
                    let (state, _, _, _, _) = read_state_file(&self.state_file);
                    if state == "dragging" {
                        // We picked up the pointer mid-drag from another monitor.
                        self.is_initiator = true;
                    } else {
                        return Task::none();
                    }
                }
                let (state, sx, sy, _, _) = read_state_file(&self.state_file);
                if state == "dragging" {
                    let gx = self.gx + local.x as i32;
                    let gy = self.gy + local.y as i32;
                    write_state_file(&self.state_file, "dragging", sx, sy, gx, gy);
                }
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::Release(local) => {
                let (state, sx, sy, _, _) = read_state_file(&self.state_file);
                if state == "dragging" {
                    let gx = self.gx + local.x as i32;
                    let gy = self.gy + local.y as i32;
                    write_state_file(&self.state_file, "done", sx, sy, gx, gy);
                    std::process::exit(0);
                }
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::Escape => {
                let (state, _, _, _, _) = read_state_file(&self.state_file);
                if state != "done" {
                    write_state_file(&self.state_file, "cancelled", 0, 0, 0, 0);
                }
                std::process::exit(130);
            }
            _ => Task::none(),
        }
    }

    fn view(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let img = iced::widget::image(self.handle.clone())
            .width(Length::Fill)
            .height(Length::Fill)
            .content_fit(iced::ContentFit::Fill);

        let canvas = Canvas::new(FragmentCanvas {
            rect: self.render_rect,
        })
        .width(Length::Fill)
        .height(Length::Fill);

        Stack::new().push(img).push(canvas).into()
    }

    fn subscription(&self) -> iced::Subscription<Message> {
        iced::Subscription::batch([
            iced::time::every(std::time::Duration::from_millis(16)).map(|_| Message::Tick),
            iced::keyboard::on_key_press(|key, _mods| match key {
                iced::keyboard::Key::Named(iced::keyboard::key::Named::Escape) => {
                    Some(Message::Escape)
                }
                _ => None,
            }),
        ])
    }
}

struct FragmentCanvas {
    rect: Option<Rectangle>,
}

impl Program<Message> for FragmentCanvas {
    type State = ();

    fn update(
        &self,
        _s: &mut (),
        event: iced::widget::canvas::Event,
        _bounds: Rectangle,
        cursor: iced::mouse::Cursor,
    ) -> (iced::widget::canvas::event::Status, Option<Message>) {
        use iced::mouse;
        use iced::widget::canvas::event::Status;

        // Use unclamped cursor position so we get motion events even when the
        // cursor leaves this surface (which happens during a Wayland implicit
        // grab when dragging from one monitor to another).
        match event {
            iced::widget::canvas::Event::Mouse(mouse::Event::ButtonPressed(
                mouse::Button::Left,
            )) => {
                if let Some(p) = cursor.position() {
                    return (Status::Captured, Some(Message::Press(p)));
                }
            }
            iced::widget::canvas::Event::Mouse(mouse::Event::CursorMoved { .. }) => {
                if let Some(p) = cursor.position() {
                    return (Status::Captured, Some(Message::Drag(p)));
                }
            }
            iced::widget::canvas::Event::Mouse(mouse::Event::ButtonReleased(
                mouse::Button::Left,
            )) => {
                if let Some(p) = cursor.position() {
                    return (Status::Captured, Some(Message::Release(p)));
                }
            }
            _ => {}
        }
        (Status::Ignored, None)
    }

    fn draw(
        &self,
        _s: &(),
        renderer: &iced::Renderer,
        _t: &Theme,
        bounds: Rectangle,
        _c: iced::mouse::Cursor,
    ) -> Vec<Geometry> {
        let mut frame = Frame::new(renderer, bounds.size());
        let dim = Color::from_rgba(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.55);

        match self.rect {
            Some(r) if r.width > 1.0 && r.height > 1.0 => {
                let sx = r.x.max(0.0);
                let sy = r.y.max(0.0);
                let ex = (r.x + r.width).min(bounds.width);
                let ey = (r.y + r.height).min(bounds.height);

                if sy > 0.0 {
                    frame.fill_rectangle(Point::ORIGIN, Size::new(bounds.width, sy), dim);
                }
                if ey < bounds.height {
                    frame.fill_rectangle(
                        Point::new(0.0, ey),
                        Size::new(bounds.width, bounds.height - ey),
                        dim,
                    );
                }
                if sy < ey {
                    if sx > 0.0 {
                        frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(0.0, sy), Size::new(sx, ey - sy), dim);
                    }
                    if ex < bounds.width {
                        frame.fill_rectangle(
                            Point::new(ex, sy),
                            Size::new(bounds.width - ex, ey - sy),
                            dim,
                        );
                    }
                }

                let b = 1.5;
                let border = Color::WHITE;
                let w = (ex - sx).max(0.0);
                let h = (ey - sy).max(0.0);
                if w > 0.0 && h > 0.0 {
                    frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(sx, sy), Size::new(w, b), border);
                    frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(sx, ey - b), Size::new(w, b), border);
                    frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(sx, sy), Size::new(b, h), border);
                    frame.fill_rectangle(Point::new(ex - b, sy), Size::new(b, h), border);
                    
                    // Live Dimensions HUD
                    let dim_text = format!("{} x {}", w as u32, h as u32);
                    frame.fill_text(iced::widget::canvas::Text {
                        content: dim_text,
                        position: Point::new(ex + 8.0, ey + 8.0),
                        color: Color::WHITE,
                        size: iced::Pixels(16.0),
                        ..Default::default()
                    });
                }
            }
            _ => {
                frame.fill_rectangle(Point::ORIGIN, bounds.size(), dim);
            }
        }

        vec![frame.into_geometry()]
    }
}

pub fn run(
    monitor: String,
    gx: i32,
    gy: i32,
    local_png: PathBuf,
    state_file: PathBuf,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let bytes = std::fs::read(&local_png).map_err(|e| format!("read: {e}"))?;
    let handle = iced::widget::image::Handle::from_bytes(bytes);
    let app = FragmentApp {
        monitor: monitor.clone(),
        gx,
        gy,
        handle,
        state_file,
        is_initiator: false,
        render_rect: None,
    };
    
    // layer_settings is passed via the Settings struct, not a trait method
    let layer = LayerShellSettings {
        size: Some((0, 0)),
        exclusive_zone: -1,
        anchor: Anchor::Top | Anchor::Bottom | Anchor::Left | Anchor::Right,
        layer: Layer::Overlay,
        keyboard_interactivity: KeyboardInteractivity::Exclusive,
        binded_output_name: Some(monitor),
        ..Default::default()
    };

    let settings = Settings {
        flags: app,
        layer_settings: layer,
        ..Default::default()
    };
    <FragmentApp as Application>::run(settings).map_err(|e| format!("run: {e:?}"))
}

=== FILE: ./src/main.rs ===
use iced::widget::{container, Stack};
use iced::{Color, Element, Length, Padding, Task as Command, Theme};
use iced_layershell::reexport::{Anchor, KeyboardInteractivity, Layer};
use iced_layershell::settings::{LayerShellSettings, Settings};
use iced_layershell::Application;

use moon_beauty_shell::backend::*;
use moon_beauty_shell::bar::{CapsuleAction, TopBar, TopBarMessage};
use moon_beauty_shell::config::{ShellSettings, ShellTheme};
use moon_beauty_shell::ipc::{send_client_command, IpcCommand, OverlayTarget};
use moon_beauty_shell::overlays::*;
use moon_beauty_shell::overlays::annotator::Tool;

mod screenshot;

fn detect_notif_surface(args: &[String]) -> bool {
    args.iter().any(|a| a == "--notif-surface")
}

fn detect_standalone_overlay(args: &[String]) -> Option<OverlayTarget> {
    for (i, arg) in args.iter().enumerate() {
        if arg == "--annotator-session" { return Some(OverlayTarget::Annotator); }
        if arg == "--overlay" || arg == "--ipc" {
            if let Some(target_str) = args.get(i + 1) {
                return OverlayTarget::from_str(target_str);
            }
        }
        if let Some(flag) = arg.strip_prefix("--") {
            if let Some(target) = OverlayTarget::from_str(flag) {
                return Some(target);
            }
        }
    }
    None
}

// Sync IPC helper — send_client_command uses tokio::net::UnixStream,
// which requires a tokio reactor. main() has no runtime, so we use
// std::os::unix::net::UnixStream directly. Same wire format.
fn send_ipc_sync(cmd: &IpcCommand) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>> {
    use std::io::{Read, Write};
    use std::os::unix::net::UnixStream;
    let path = std::env::var("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR")
        .map(|d| std::path::PathBuf::from(d).join("moon-beauty-shell.sock"))
        .map_err(|e| format!("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR: {e}"))?;
    let mut stream = UnixStream::connect(&path)?;
    let encoded = serde_json::to_vec(cmd)?;
    stream.write_all(&encoded)?;
    stream.shutdown(std::net::Shutdown::Write)?;
    let mut resp = String::new();
    stream.read_to_string(&mut resp)?;
    Ok(resp)
}


fn resolve_exe() -> std::path::PathBuf {
    std::env::current_exe()
        .ok()
        .map(|p| {
            let s = p.to_string_lossy().into_owned();
            // /proc/PID/exe on Linux returns "…/bin (deleted)" after `cargo build`
            // replaces the running binary. Strip the suffix so spawn still works.
            if let Some(stripped) = s.strip_suffix(" (deleted)") {
                std::path::PathBuf::from(stripped)
            } else {
                p
            }
        })
        .unwrap_or_else(|| std::path::PathBuf::from("moon-beauty-shell"))
}

pub fn main() -> Result<(), iced_layershell::Error> {
    let args: Vec<String> = std::env::args().collect();

    // --quickshot was replaced by --screenshot --annotate.
    if args.iter().any(|a| a == "--quickshot" || a == "quickshot") {
        eprintln!("[error] --quickshot is gone. Screenshot workflows now:");
        eprintln!("        --screenshot              capture region and save");
        eprintln!("        --screenshot --annotate   capture and open annotator");
        eprintln!("        --watermark \"name\"        embed invisible watermark");
        eprintln!();
        eprintln!("        Update your keybinds accordingly.");
        std::process::exit(2);
    }

    // ── Screenshot CLI ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // --screenshot [--annotate] [--watermark "name"]
    // MUST be above the notif-surface spawn block, or the flag falls
    // through and boots the full bar.
    // Temporary: verify freeze-frame capture works before UI build
    if args.iter().any(|a| a == "--capture-test") {
        match screenshot::capture::capture_fullscreen() {
            Ok(bytes) => {
                let path = "/tmp/mbs-capture-test.png";
                let _ = std::fs::write(path, &bytes);
                match image::load_from_memory(&bytes) {
                    Ok(img) => println!(
                        "captured {}x{} → {} ({} bytes)",
                        img.width(), img.height(), path, bytes.len()
                    ),
                    Err(e) => eprintln!("decode: {e}"),
                }
            }
            Err(e) => eprintln!("capture failed: {e}"),
        }
        std::process::exit(0);
    }

    // Child: render freeze-frame selector, write crop to disk
    if args.iter().any(|a| a == "--freeze-fragment") {
        let idx = args.iter().position(|a| a == "--freeze-fragment").unwrap();
        let monitor = args.get(idx + 1).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
        let gx: i32 = args.get(idx + 2).and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
        let gy: i32 = args.get(idx + 3).and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
        let local_png = args.get(idx + 4).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
        let state_file = args.get(idx + 5).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
        if monitor.is_empty() || local_png.is_empty() || state_file.is_empty() {
            eprintln!("usage: --freeze-fragment <name> <gx> <gy> <local.png> <state_file>");
            std::process::exit(2);
        }
        match screenshot::fragment::run(
            monitor,
            gx,
            gy,
            std::path::PathBuf::from(local_png),
            std::path::PathBuf::from(state_file),
        ) {
            Ok(_) => std::process::exit(0),
            Err(e) => { eprintln!("[freeze-fragment] {e}"); std::process::exit(1); }
        }
    }

    if args.iter().any(|a| a == "--freeze-selector") {
        let idx = args.iter().position(|a| a == "--freeze-selector").unwrap();
        let input = args.get(idx + 1).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
        let output = args.get(idx + 2).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
        let binded = args.get(idx + 3).cloned();
        if input.is_empty() || output.is_empty() {
            eprintln!("usage: --freeze-selector <input.png> <output.png> [output-name]");
            std::process::exit(2);
        }
        match screenshot::selector::run(
            std::path::PathBuf::from(input),
            std::path::PathBuf::from(output),
            binded,
        ) {
            Ok(_) => std::process::exit(0),
            Err(e) => { eprintln!("[freeze-selector] {e}"); std::process::exit(1); }
        }
    }

    // Orchestrator: capture focused output → spawn child → report crop
    if args.iter().any(|a| a == "--freeze-test") {
        let bytes = match screenshot::capture::capture_output(None) {
            Ok(b) => b,
            Err(e) => { eprintln!("[freeze] capture failed: {e}"); std::process::exit(1); }
        };
        let img = match image::load_from_memory(&bytes) {
            Ok(i) => i,
            Err(e) => { eprintln!("[freeze] decode failed: {e}"); std::process::exit(1); }
        };
        eprintln!("[freeze] captured {}x{} ({} bytes)", img.width(), img.height(), bytes.len());

        let tmp_in = "/tmp/mbs-freeze-in.png";
        let tmp_out = "/tmp/mbs-freeze-out.png";
        std::fs::write(tmp_in, &bytes).ok();
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(tmp_out);

        let exe = resolve_exe();
        let status = std::process::Command::new(exe)
            .args(["--freeze-selector", tmp_in, tmp_out])
            .status();
        match status {
            Ok(s) if s.success() && std::path::Path::new(tmp_out).exists() => {
                let cropped = std::fs::read(tmp_out).unwrap_or_default();
                if let Ok(i) = image::load_from_memory(&cropped) {
                    eprintln!("[freeze] ✓ cropped {}x{} → {}", i.width(), i.height(), tmp_out);
                }
                std::process::exit(0);
            }
            Ok(_) => { eprintln!("[freeze] cancelled"); std::process::exit(1); }
            Err(e) => { eprintln!("[freeze] child spawn failed: {e}"); std::process::exit(1); }
        }
    }

    if args.iter().any(|a| a == "--screenshot") {
        let quick = args.iter().any(|a| a == "--quick" || a == "--no-annotate");
        let annotate = !quick;
        let watermark_cli = args.iter()
            .position(|a| a == "--watermark")
            .and_then(|i| args.get(i + 1).cloned());
        let _ = watermark_cli;

        // Multi-monitor: capture the entire desktop once, then spawn one fragment
        // per monitor with a local crop. Fragments coordinate drags via a shared state file.
        let outputs = screenshot::output::list_all_outputs();
        if outputs.is_empty() {
            eprintln!("[screenshot] no outputs found");
            std::process::exit(1);
        }

        let full_bytes = match screenshot::capture::capture_output(None) {
            Ok(b) => b,
            Err(e) => { eprintln!("[screenshot] capture failed: {e}"); std::process::exit(1); }
        };
        let full_img = match image::load_from_memory(&full_bytes) {
            Ok(i) => i.to_rgba8(),
            Err(e) => { eprintln!("[screenshot] decode failed: {e}"); std::process::exit(1); }
        };

        // The full image's origin corresponds to (min_x, min_y) in global space.
        let min_x = outputs.iter().map(|(_, x, _, _, _)| *x).min().unwrap_or(0);
        let min_y = outputs.iter().map(|(_, _, y, _, _)| *y).min().unwrap_or(0);

        let pid = std::process::id();
        let state_file = std::path::PathBuf::from(format!("/tmp/mbs-freeze-state-{}.txt", pid));
        screenshot::fragment::write_state_file(&state_file, "idle", 0, 0, 0, 0);

        let exe = resolve_exe();
        let mut children: Vec<(String, std::process::Child)> = Vec::new();

        for (name, x, y, w, h) in &outputs {
            let lx = (*x - min_x) as u32;
            let ly = (*y - min_y) as u32;
            if lx + *w > full_img.width() || ly + *h > full_img.height() {
                continue;
            }
            let crop = image::imageops::crop_imm(&full_img, lx, ly, *w, *h).to_image();
            let local_png = format!("/tmp/mbs-freeze-{}-{}.png", name, pid);
            if crop.save(&local_png).is_err() { continue; }

            match std::process::Command::new(&exe)
                .args([
                    "--freeze-fragment",
                    name,
                    &x.to_string(),
                    &y.to_string(),
                    &local_png,
                    state_file.to_str().unwrap_or(""),
                ])
                .spawn()
            {
                Ok(child) => children.push((name.clone(), child)),
                Err(e) => eprintln!("[screenshot] spawn {} failed: {e}", name),
            }
        }

        if children.is_empty() {
            eprintln!("[screenshot] no fragments spawned");
            std::process::exit(1);
        }

        // Poll state file
        let mut rect: Option<(i32, i32, i32, i32)> = None;
        loop {
            let (state, sx, sy, cx, cy) = screenshot::fragment::read_state_file(&state_file);
            if state == "done" { rect = Some((sx, sy, cx, cy)); break; }
            if state == "cancelled" { break; }
            let mut any_alive = false;
            for (_, child) in children.iter_mut() {
                match child.try_wait() {
                    Ok(Some(_)) => {}
                    Ok(None) => any_alive = true,
                    Err(_) => {}
                }
            }
            if !any_alive { break; }
            std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(20));
        }

        for (_, mut child) in children.drain(..) {
            let _ = child.kill();
            let _ = child.wait();
        }

        // Clean up per-monitor crops
        for (name, _, _, _, _) in &outputs {
            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(format!("/tmp/mbs-freeze-{}-{}.png", name, pid));
        }
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&state_file);

        let Some((sx, sy, cx, cy)) = rect else {
            eprintln!("[screenshot] cancelled");
            std::process::exit(130);
        };

        let rx = (sx.min(cx) - min_x).max(0) as u32;
        let ry = (sy.min(cy) - min_y).max(0) as u32;
        let rw = (sx - cx).unsigned_abs();
        let rh = (sy - cy).unsigned_abs();

        if rw < 4 || rh < 4 || rx + rw > full_img.width() || ry + rh > full_img.height() {
            eprintln!("[screenshot] invalid selection");
            std::process::exit(130);
        }

        let cropped = image::imageops::crop_imm(&full_img, rx, ry, rw, rh).to_image();
        let out_path = format!("/tmp/mbs-freeze-out-{}.png", pid);
        if cropped.save(&out_path).is_err() {
            eprintln!("[screenshot] save crop failed");
            std::process::exit(1);
        }
        eprintln!("[screenshot] cropped → {}", out_path);

        if annotate {
            let cmd = IpcCommand::Open(OverlayTarget::Annotator, Some(out_path.clone()));
            match send_ipc_sync(&cmd) {
                Ok(_) => {}
                Err(e) => {
                    eprintln!("[screenshot] annotator IPC failed: {e}");
                    std::process::exit(1);
                }
            }
        } else {
            let home = std::env::var("HOME").unwrap_or_default();
            let dir = std::path::PathBuf::from(home).join("Pictures").join("Screenshots");
            let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir);
            let ts = std::time::SystemTime::now()
                .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
                .map(|d| d.as_secs()).unwrap_or(0);
            let dest = dir.join(format!("shot_{}.png", ts));
            let _ = std::fs::copy(&out_path, &dest);
            eprintln!("[screenshot] saved → {}", dest.display());
        }
        std::process::exit(0);
    }

    // CLI Notification Push: moon-beauty-shell --notify "LusterDawn" "Message"
    if args.len() >= 3 && (args[1] == "--notify" || (args.len() >= 4 && args[1] == "--ipc" && args[2] == "notify")) {
        let summary = if args[1] == "--notify" { &args[2] } else { &args[3] };
        let body = if args[1] == "--notify" { args.get(3).map(|s| s.as_str()).unwrap_or("") } else { args.get(4).map(|s| s.as_str()).unwrap_or("") };
        let _ = std::process::Command::new("notify-send")
            .args(["-a", "Desktop", summary, body])
            .spawn();
        std::process::exit(0);
    }

    let is_notif_surface = detect_notif_surface(&args);
    let standalone_target = detect_standalone_overlay(&args);
    let settings = ShellSettings::load();

    if !is_notif_surface && standalone_target.is_none() {
        let _ = std::process::Command::new("pkill")
            .args(["-f", "--", "moon-beauty-shell --notif-surface"])
            .status();
        std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(300));
        let exe = resolve_exe();
        let _ = std::process::Command::new(exe).arg("--notif-surface").spawn();
    }

    let binded_output_name = if settings.notif_screen_name != "Auto" && settings.notif_screen_name != "Auto (Primary)" && !settings.notif_screen_name.is_empty() {
        Some(settings.notif_screen_name.clone())
    } else {
        None
    };

    if is_notif_surface {
        eprintln!("[notif-surface] startup binded_output_name={:?}", binded_output_name);
    }
    let layer_settings = if is_notif_surface {
        let corner = &settings.notif_corner;
        let is_top = corner.contains("top");
        let is_right = corner.contains("right");
        let anchor = match (is_top, is_right) {
            (true, true)   => Anchor::Top    | Anchor::Right,
            (true, false)  => Anchor::Top    | Anchor::Left,
            (false, true)  => Anchor::Bottom | Anchor::Right,
            (false, false) => Anchor::Bottom | Anchor::Left,
        };
        let _strip_w = (settings.notif_card_width as i32
            + 2 * settings.notif_margin_x + 40).max(200) as u32;
        let _strip_h = (3 * settings.notif_card_height as i32
            + 2 * settings.notif_margin_x + 100).max(300) as u32;
        LayerShellSettings {
            size: Some((1, 1)),
            exclusive_zone: -1,
            anchor,
            layer: Layer::Overlay,
            keyboard_interactivity: KeyboardInteractivity::None,
            binded_output_name,
            ..Default::default()
        }
    } else if standalone_target.as_ref() == Some(&OverlayTarget::Annotator) {
        // Floating annotator window. wlr-layer-shell centers when all four
        // sides are anchored with a fixed size. Overlay layer keeps it above
        // the bar. Tune (W, H) to taste — this reads well on 1440p.
        LayerShellSettings {
            size: Some((1600, 1000)),
            exclusive_zone: -1,
            anchor: Anchor::Top | Anchor::Bottom | Anchor::Left | Anchor::Right,
            layer: Layer::Overlay,
            keyboard_interactivity: KeyboardInteractivity::Exclusive,
            binded_output_name,
            ..Default::default()
        }
    } else if standalone_target.is_some() {
        LayerShellSettings {
            size: Some((0, 0)),
            exclusive_zone: -1,
            anchor: Anchor::Top | Anchor::Bottom | Anchor::Left | Anchor::Right,
            layer: Layer::Overlay,
            keyboard_interactivity: KeyboardInteractivity::Exclusive,
            binded_output_name,
            ..Default::default()
        }
    } else {
        LayerShellSettings {
            size: Some((0, settings.bar_height)),
            exclusive_zone: settings.bar_height as i32,
            anchor: Anchor::Top | Anchor::Left | Anchor::Right,
            layer: Layer::Top,
            keyboard_interactivity: KeyboardInteractivity::None,
            binded_output_name,
            ..Default::default()
        }
    };

        if args.iter().any(|a| a == "--sys-tooltip") {
        match moon_beauty_shell::overlays::system_tooltip::run() {
            Ok(()) => std::process::exit(0),
            Err(e) => { eprintln!("[sys-tooltip] {e}"); std::process::exit(1); }
        }
    }

    if args.iter().any(|a| a == "--tray-popup") {
        match moon_beauty_shell::overlays::tray_popup::run() {
            Ok(()) => std::process::exit(0),
            Err(e) => { eprintln!("[tray-popup] {e}"); std::process::exit(1); }
        }
    }

    if args.iter().any(|a| a == "--weather-tooltip") {
        match moon_beauty_shell::overlays::weather_tooltip::run() {
            Ok(()) => std::process::exit(0),
            Err(e) => { eprintln!("[weather-tooltip] {e}"); std::process::exit(1); }
        }
    }

    if args.iter().any(|a| a == "--alarm-tooltip") {
        match moon_beauty_shell::overlays::alarm_tooltip::run() {
            Ok(()) => std::process::exit(0),
            Err(e) => { eprintln!("[alarm-tooltip] {e}"); std::process::exit(1); }
        }
    }

    MoonBeautyShellApp::run(Settings {
        layer_settings,
        ..Default::default()
    })
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum Message {
    TickHardware,
    TickClock,
    TickToasts,
    MetricsCollected(CpuMetrics, RamMetrics, GpuMetrics, AudioStatus, AudioStatus, Option<MprisTrack>),
    IpcReceived(IpcCommand),
    PollIpc,
    PollNotifs,
    ResizeNotifSurface(u32, u32),
    WindowResized(f32),
    TopBar(TopBarMessage),
    Launcher(LauncherMessage),
    Calc(CalcMessage),
    Clipboard(ClipboardMessage),
    Notes(NotesMessage),
    Pass(PassMessage),
    Power(PowerMessage),
    Todo(TodoMessage),
    Reminders(ReminderMessage),
    Dict(DictMessage),
    Unicode(UnicodeMessage),
    Web(WebSearchMessage),
    Amogus(AmogusMessage),
    Annotator(AnnotatorMessage),
    Rng(RngMessage),
    Email(EmailMessage),
    Notif(NotifMessage),
    Settings(SettingsMessage),
    Lock(LockMessage),
    DismissActiveOverlay,
    CloseShell,
}

impl TryInto<iced_layershell::actions::LayershellCustomActionsWithInfo<()>> for Message {
    type Error = Message;
    fn try_into(
        self,
    ) -> Result<iced_layershell::actions::LayershellCustomActionsWithInfo<()>, Self::Error>
    {
        match self {
            Message::ResizeNotifSurface(w, h) => Ok(
                iced_layershell::actions::LayershellCustomActionsWithInfo::SizeChange((w, h)),
            ),
            other => Err(other),
        }
    }
}

pub struct MoonBeautyShellApp {
    weather_tooltip_child: Option<std::process::Child>,
    settings: ShellSettings,
    theme: ShellTheme,
    telemetry: UnifiedTelemetry,
    active_overlay: Option<OverlayTarget>,
    is_standalone: bool,
    system_collector: SystemCollector,
    standalone_child: Option<(OverlayTarget, std::process::Child)>,
    ipc_rx: std::sync::mpsc::Receiver<IpcCommand>,
    is_notif_surface: bool,
